### [1] [Free energy fluctuations of symmetric perceptron models up to and including criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31736)
*Hang Du,Shuyang Gong,Brice Huang,Zhangsong Li*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文证明：在一般偶激活函数下，球面与Ising感知机模型（spherical and Ising perceptron）的自由能（free energy）在高温度内部及临界边界均满足高斯中心极限定理（Gaussian CLT）；临界处涨落尺度为 ((log N)/6)^{1/2}，与临界Sherrington–Kirkpatrick（SK）模型一致，提示该标度具有普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究已刻画SK模型自由能涨落，但感知机类模型（含对称二值感知机、U-perceptron、二分SK模型）在二阶矩回复（second moment RS）区域内部及临界边界上的自由能涨落规律尚不明确，尤其临界标度是否普适有待检验。

Method: 采用临界重加权矩方法（critical reweighted moment method），证明感知机模型自由能涨落在高概率下可由对应球面Hopfield模型的涨落解释（误差为 o(1) 加性项），且该对应关系在临界点依然成立，从而将涨落与无序（disorder）的谱边缘间距（spectral edge spacing）相联系。

Result: 得到自由能的高斯CLT，在二阶矩RS区域的“高温”内部与“临界”边界上均成立；临界情形下CLT的尺度为 ((log N)/6)^{1/2}，与临界SK模型的自由能涨落相匹配。

Conclusion: 感知机模型的临界自由能涨落与SK模型一致，暗示该 ((log N)/6)^{1/2} 标度为普适标度；同时确立了感知机与球面Hopfield模型之间涨落层面的对应关系，并且该关系在临界处不失效。

Abstract: We study the free energy fluctuations of spherical and Ising perceptron models with general even activations. These include the symmetric binary perceptron, the $U$-perceptron, and the bipartite Sherrington--Kirkpatrick model. Our main result is a Gaussian CLT for the free energy that holds both in the ``high-temperature'' interior of a second moment RS regime and on its ``critical'' boundary. At criticality, the CLT has scale $(\frac{\log N}{6})^{1/2}$, which matches the free energy fluctuations of the critical Sherrington--Kirkpatrick model and suggests this scaling is universal.
  The proof shows that the perceptron model's free energy fluctuations are explained, up to an additive $o(1)$ error with high probability, by those of a corresponding spherical Hopfield model, and that this relation persists even at criticality. This proof strategy builds on a critical reweighted moment method developed by the first and third authors \cite{du2026fluctuations} to identify the critical fluctuations of the SK free energy, which links these fluctuations to the spectral edge spacing of the disorder.

</details>


### [2] [Probabilistic representation and Monte Carlo method for nonlinear Dirac equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31942)
*Jean-Pierre Fouque,Tomoyuki Ichiba,Ka Lok Lam*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文利用分段确定性马尔可夫过程（PDMP）的随机点过程演算，为1+1维三次非线性Dirac方程构造概率表示，并据此提出时间推进型Monte Carlo全局数值解法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dirac方程刻画量子粒子的相对论演化，但非线性Dirac方程（如Soler模型）缺乏兼具严格性的概率表示与全局数值求解框架，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在一般状态空间上采用分段确定性马尔可夫过程（piecewise-deterministic Markov processes, PDMPs）的点过程随机演算，结合多类型分支电报过程（multi-type branching telegraph process）与连续型及跳型乘性泛函，并引入Feynman-Kac势；随后利用解析导出的先验L∞估计将单步表示推广为多步时间推进表示，并构建时间推进Monte Carlo算法。

Result: 主定理给出分类结果，应用于Soler模型时得到概率表示的尖锐有限时间容许性（sharp finite-time admissibility）；揭示"局部概率表示但全局PDE适定"（local probabilistic representation, but global PDE well-posedness）现象，并给出Monte Carlo算法严格的收敛性证明及PDE侧的稳定性结果。

Conclusion: 底层过程参数不影响概率表示的有效性，但可用于Monte Carlo方法的方差缩减（variance reduction），为非线性Dirac方程的数值求解提供了新途径。

Abstract: Dirac equations describe the relativistic evolution of quantum particles. Using the point-process stochastic calculus of piecewise-deterministic Markov processes (PDMPs) on general state spaces, which naturally allows additional Feynman-Kac potentials, we develop a probabilistic representation for the nonlinear Dirac equations in $1+1$ spacetime dimension with cubic nonlinearity and $L_\infty$-type problem data using a multi-type branching telegraph process together with both continuous and jump-type multiplicative functionals. Our main theorem leads to a classification result which, when applied to the specific Soler model, gives a sharp finite-time admissibility of the probabilistic representation. By extending the one-step representation to a multi-step, time-marching representation using an analytically-derived a priori $L_\infty$ estimate, we observe a ``local probabilistic representation, but global PDE well-posedness" phenomenon. We proceed to propose a time-marching Monte Carlo algorithm as a global numerical solver for the Soler system with a rigorous convergence proof and establish an additional stability result on the PDE side. Finally, while the underlying process parameters do not affect the validity of the probabilistic representation, we observe their potential for variance reduction in the Monte Carlo methods.

</details>


### [3] [Approximating the Gibbs Measure of Spherical Spin Glasses to Within $o(N^{-1/2})$ in the Replica Symmetric Phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32649)
*Brice Huang,Andrea Montanari,Huy Tuan Pham*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文在复制对称相（replica symmetric phase）下研究混合球面自旋玻璃（mixed spherical spin glasses）在外加弱磁场中的磁化矢量，给出逐坐标展开，包含二次近似后的首阶三次修正，逐坐标误差为 $o(N^{-1/2})$，并证明整个矢量的高阶矩界（high-moment bound）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是获得比二次近似更精细的磁化矢量刻画，并服务于后续在 TAP 临界点附近窄带内对倾斜球面自旋玻璃（tilted spherical spin glass）有效哈密顿量的分析；现有归一化 Wasserstein 精度（normalized Wasserstein accuracy）不足以支撑全变差采样（total-variation sampling）等应用。

Method: 在复制对称相与弱外场设定下，对磁化矢量作逐坐标展开（coordinatewise expansion），在二次近似基础上加入首阶三次修正，并证明对应的全矢量高阶矩界。

Result: 得到含三次修正的逐坐标展开，每坐标误差为 $o(N^{-1/2})$；同时建立全矢量相应的高阶矩界。伴随论文将该估计用于限制在 TAP 临界点附近窄带的倾斜球面自旋玻璃有效哈密顿量，得到锐利近似（sharp approximation）。

Conclusion: 该展开为归一化 Wasserstein 精度不足的场景提供更锐利的近似基础，特别是基于随机局部化（stochastic localization）的全变差采样。

Abstract: We study the magnetization vector of mixed spherical spin glasses in a weak external field, in the replica symmetric phase. We derive a coordinatewise expansion of the magnetization which includes the leading cubic correction to the quadratic approximation, with an error that is $o(N^{-1/2})$ per coordinate. We also prove a corresponding high-moment bound for the full vector. Finally, our companion paper applies these estimates to the effective Hamiltonian obtained by restricting a tilted spherical spin glass to a narrow band around a TAP critical point. The resulting sharp approximation is designed for applications in which normalized Wasserstein accuracy is insufficient, including total-variation sampling by stochastic localization.

</details>


### [4] [Avalanches of the Abelian sandpile on the hierarchical lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32687)
*Luca Cattani,Leandro Chiarini,Alessandra Cipriani*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文研究有限分形图（hierarchical / Migdal–Kadanoff lattices）上阿贝尔沙堆模型（Abelian sandpile model）的雪崩与波，给出关键顶点的幂律尾部界、关键可达范围的极限结果，以及高次顶点处均匀波规模的标度极限（scaling limit）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有沙堆模型研究多集中于规则格点或一般图，而有限分形图（fractal graphs）兼具层次结构与自相似性，其上的雪崩统计与波传播行为尚缺乏严格的定量刻画，故需在此类可重整化（renormalization）的层次格点上建立精细的极限理论。

Method: 在层次（Migdal–Kadanoff）格点序列上考察阿贝尔沙堆模型，围绕特定“关键”（pivotal）顶点启动的雪崩（avalanche）与均匀波（uniform wave），通过概率尾部估计与极限分析，推导幂律尾部界、关键可达范围（pivotal reach）的极限及波规模的标度极限。

Result: 获得三项主要结果：其一，关键顶点处雪崩与均匀波的幂律尾部界（power-law tail bounds）相互匹配；其二，关于雪崩关键可达范围的极限结果；其三，高次（high degree）顶点处启动的均匀波规模的标度极限（scaling limit）。

Conclusion: 在有限分形层次格点上，阿贝尔沙堆模型的雪崩与波呈现可严格刻画的幂律行为，并具备明确的极限与标度极限，表明分形结构下的沙堆动力学具有稳定的普适性统计规律。

Abstract: We study the Abelian sandpile model on a sequence of finite fractal graphs: the hierarchical, or Migdal--Kadanoff, lattices. Our first main result consists of matching power-law tail bounds for the avalanche and the uniform wave started at certain "pivotal" vertices. We also establish a limiting result for the pivotal reach of the avalanche and, finally, a scaling limit for the size of a uniform wave started at a vertex of high degree.

</details>


### [5] [Non-equilibrium density fluctuations for the stirring process with weak boundary dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32701)
*Panagiota Birmpa,Patrícia Gonçalves,Dimitrios Tsagkarogiannis*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究一维搅拌过程（stirring process）在边界注入与移除粒子作用下的非平衡涨落，证明了涨落极限为带有时间依赖Robin边界条件的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象为区间$\{-N,\ldots,N\}$上、耦合左右储库边界动力学（注入与移除粒子，窗口大小为$K$）的一维搅拌过程，目标是刻画其非平衡涨落行为。

Method: 从与光滑初始剖面（initial profile）对应的乘积测度（product measure）出发，通过对流体力学方程（hydrodynamic equation）中边界条件线性化，得到时间依赖Robin边界条件；涨落极限以作用在满足该边界条件的检验函数上的Laplacian与梯度算子表述。

Result: 极限Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程由作用在满足时间依赖Robin边界条件（time-dependent Robin boundary conditions）的检验函数上的Laplacian与梯度算子给出。

Conclusion: 分析的核心要素是对中心化占据变量（centered occupation variables）任意阶空间与时空$v$-函数（$v$-functions）的精细界，该界在配套论文中证明。

Abstract: We consider the one-dimensional stirring process on the segment $\{-N,\ldots,N\}$, coupled to boundary dynamics that inject particles from the right reservoir and remove particles from the left reservoir, each acting on a window of size $K$. We investigate the non-equilibrium fluctuations of the system, starting from a product measure associated with a smooth initial profile. The limit Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process is given in terms of the Laplacian and gradient operators acting on test functions satisfying time-dependent Robin boundary conditions that are obtained by linearizing the boundary conditions in the hydrodynamic equation. A central ingredient in our analysis is the derivation of sharp bounds on the space and space-time $v$-functions of arbitrary degree for the centered occupation variables; these bounds were proved in the companion article \cite{birmpa2025nonequilibriumfluctuationsstirringprocess}.

</details>


### [6] [The boosted loop-erased random walk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32821)
*Gefei Cai,Xinyi Li,Daisuke Shiraishi,Aoteng Xia*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 论文研究环擦除随机游走（loop-erased random walk, LERW）的近临界（near-critical）版本：按擦除前游走长度对权重进行放大（boost），并在二维单位圆盘与三维单位球中构造了自然参数化（natural parametrization）下的标度极限（scaling limit），揭示其与经典 LERW 标度极限的相互绝对连续（mutual absolute continuity）相变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于 Makarov–Smirnov 关于近临界格点模型（near-critical lattice models）的问题，即探讨在临界点附近扰动模型权重后，标度极限如何变化以及是否仍与临界极限绝对连续。

Method: 构造按底层随机游走长度加权的“放大”LERW，在二维单位圆盘和三维单位球中取标度极限并采用自然参数化；在平面弦（chordal）情形下，还在区域谱阈值（spectral threshold）以下构造连续环路倾斜（loop tilt），并计算其 Loewner 驱动函数（Loewner driving function）。

Result: 放大后的标度极限与经典 LERW 标度极限的相互绝对连续性随放大速率发生相变，圆盘中相变点为 m^2=π^2/4；放大增强时曲线在宏观上趋于空间填充（space-filling），未访问立方体边长为 O(m^{-1})，而每个有限放大下的 Hausdorff 维数（Hausdorff dimension）保持不变。

Conclusion: 结果给出了近临界 LERW 标度极限的完整刻画，回应了 Makarov–Smirnov 的问题，并表明放大仅改变宏观填充性质与测度绝对连续性，而不改变曲线的 Hausdorff 维数。

Abstract: We consider a near-critical version of loop-erased random walk (LERW) by boosting the weight of the underlying random walk according to its length before loop-erasure. We construct the scaling limit in natural parametrization in the two-dimensional unit disk and the three-dimensional unit ball. The boosted scaling limit undergoes a phase transition in mutual absolute continuity with the classical LERW scaling limit; in the disk the transition occurs at boost rate $m^2=π^2/4$. As the boost increases, the curve becomes macroscopically space-filling, in the sense that unvisited cubes have side length $O(m^{-1})$, while its Hausdorff dimension at each finite boost is unchanged. These results are motivated by Makarov--Smirnov's questions on near-critical lattice models. In the planar chordal setting, we also construct a continuum loop tilt below the spectral threshold of the domain and calculate its Loewner driving function.

</details>


### [7] [Near-critical Ising interfaces in the thermal direction: Absolute continuity with respect to SLE$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32860)
*Yu Feng*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明在二维临界 Ising 模型中，单个界面（interface）的标度极限律相对 SLE₃ 相互绝对连续，尽管整个近临界构型的律在热扰动方向上是奇异的。机制在于对数增长的比热在完整区域与沿界面切割区域之间相互抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知近临界 FK-Ising 构型的标度极限律相对临界律奇异，自旋构型被预期同理，但单个界面的行为尚不明确。论文旨在厘清单个界面在热方向扰动下是否仍保持绝对连续性。

Method: 考虑网格 δ、位于 Dobrushin 方块内、β=β_c−mδ（m≠0 固定）的 Ising 模型，计算界面律相对其临界律的 Radon–Nikodym 导数（Radon–Nikodym derivative），并给出其在正负各阶矩上关于 δ 的一致界。

Result: Radon–Nikodym 导数的所有正阶与负阶矩均一致有界，故界面任一子列标度极限的律与 SLE₃ 的律相互绝对连续（mutually absolutely continuous）。

Conclusion: 结果的机制在于对数增长的比热（specific heat，即哈密顿量方差按边数归一化）在完整区域与沿界面切割的区域之间相互抵消，从而抑制了热方向上的奇异效应。

Abstract: The scaling limits of interfaces in the critical two-dimensional Ising model are described by the Schramm--Loewner evolution $\mathrm{SLE}_3$. While, in the scaling limit, the law of the whole near-critical FK-Ising configuration is known to be singular with respect to the critical law in the thermal direction, and the same is expected for the spin configuration, we prove in this paper that the law of a single interface is not. More precisely, we consider the Ising model with mesh $δ$ in the Dobrushin square at $β=β_c-mδ$, where $m\ne0$ is fixed, and we prove that the Radon-Nikodym derivative of the law of the interface with respect to its critical law has moments of every positive and negative order, bounded uniformly in $δ$, so that the law of every subsequential scaling limit of the interface is mutually absolutely continuous with respect to that of $\mathrm{SLE}_3$. Our analysis explains the mechanism behind this result: the logarithmically growing specific heat (that is, the variance of the Hamiltonian, normalized by the number of edges) cancels between the full domain and the domain slit along the interface.

</details>


### [8] [Probabilistic ultraviolet renormalization of the two-dimensional Sine-Gordon model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32927)
*Benjamin Bonnefont,Hermanni Rajamäki,Vincent Vargas*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文用概率方法给出二维 Sine-Gordon 模型（Sine-Gordon model）紫外重整化（ultraviolet renormalization）的构造，覆盖有界凸域上任意粒子活度（particle activity）α∈ℝ 与次临界区间（subcritical regime）β²∈[0,4)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型形式上由密度 exp(α∫:cos(βX):) 描述，其中 X 为对数相关高斯场（log-correlated Gaussian field）；既有构造未覆盖完整的次临界范围，本文旨在扩展其适用范围。

Method: 将 Lacoin、Rhodes 与 Vargas 提出的鞅方法（martingale method）应用于二维情形，并借助基于电荷共轭对称性（charge conjugation symmetry）的精细估计（refined estimates）完成构造。

Result: 在有界凸域（bounded convex domains）上，对任意 α∈ℝ 及整个次临界区间 β²∈[0,4)（位于 Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless 阈值以下）均给出有效构造。

Conclusion: 结果在更广的一类协方差（covariance）上处理了完整次临界区间，从而推广了已有构造。

Abstract: We give a probabilistic approach to the ultraviolet renormalization of the two-dimensional Sine-Gordon model. The model is formally described by the density $\exp(α\int \mathopen{:} \cos(βX) \mathclose{:} )$, where $X$ is a log-correlated Gaussian field. Our construction applies on bounded convex domains for every particle activity $α\in\mathbb R$ and throughout the subcritical range $β^2\in[0,4)$, below the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless threshold. We apply the martingale method introduced by Lacoin, Rhodes and Vargas to two dimensions by using refined estimates based on charge conjugation symmetries. Our result extends existing constructions by treating the full subcritical regime for a broad class of covariances.

</details>

### [9] ['Dancing' two-dimensional patterns in nematics above the flexoelectric instability threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31849)
*E. S. Pikina*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理出发求解交变外场中向列相液晶的非线性方程组，导出了柔性电不稳定性阈值以上真正二维图案的独特“舞蹈”行为，并给出其作为Hopf分岔附近稳定极限环的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液晶中各类电流体动力学不稳定性是反复出现的研究主题，但向列相柔性电不稳定性（flexoelectric instability）的非线性阶段仍是最少被研究的部分，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过求解交变外场中向列相液晶的非线性方程组，限定于介电常数各向异性（dielectric permittivity anisotropy）与电导率各向异性（conductivity anisotropy）均为负的液晶；并建立该现象的广义二维类Ginzburg-Landau唯象理论（Ginzburg-Landau-like phenomenological theory）。

Result: 从第一性原理导出了柔性电不稳定性阈值以上真正二维图案的独特“舞蹈”（dancing）行为；所得解为Hopf分岔（Hopf bifurcation）附近上方的稳定极限环（stable limit cycle），其有效范围限于解的特征指数（characteristic exponent）为复数的参数区间。

Conclusion: 该工作凸显了非线性物理的一项关键进展，为非线性动力系统行为提供了有说服力的范例，并深化了对柔性电不稳定性非线性阶段的理解。

Abstract: Various types of electrohydrodynamic instabilities in liquid crystals (LCs) are a recurring theme in LC research. The nonlinear stage of the flexoelectric instability in nematics remains the least studied aspect of this phenomenon. By solving the system of nonlinear equations for a nematic in an alternating external field, we derive from first principles the unique "dancing" behavior of truly two-dimensional patterns above the flexoelectric instability threshold. Our analysis is restricted to liquid crystals with negative dielectric permittivity and electrical conductivity anisotropies. The resulting solution represents a stable limit cycle near above the Hopf bifurcation, valid within the parameters range where the solutions characteristic exponent is complex. The generalized two-dimensional Ginzburg-Landau-like phenomenological theory of the phenomenon is developed.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.soft

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显微成像方法难以应对软材料与生物材料的空间异质性与低信噪比问题，且多数流变光学（rheo-optical）方法聚焦宏观力而非局部扰动，而局部应力在生物与工业应用中十分重要。

Conclusion: 该分析平台填补了微观流变学（micro-rheology）的关键空白，为探测从生物聚合物网络到3D打印材料及软体机器人的非平衡动力学（non-equilibrium dynamics）提供通用工具。

Abstract: Understanding the microscopic behavior of materials in response to mechanical perturbations is a central challenge in soft matter, yet existing methods, which typically rely on microscopy-based imaging, often struggle with spatial heterogeneity and low signal-to-noise that are characteristic of many soft and biological materials. Additionally, many existing rheo-optical methods focus on the response to macro-scale forces rather than localized perturbations despite the importance of local stresses in biological and industrial applications of soft materials. Here, we present STroboscopically Averaged, time-Resolved Differential Dynamic Microscopy (starDDM) to investigate a sample's deformation across time and distance due to a cyclically applied local force. As proof-of-principle, we use this model-independent and tracer-free approach to map the motions of active cytoskeleton composites imposed by the movement of an optically trapped probe particle embedded within the complex network. We reveal how motor-driven restructuring and varying strain rates tune the magnitude and propagation of the response, which we further show decays with distance from the perturbation. This analytical platform fills a critical gap in micro-rheology, offering a versatile tool for probing the non-equilibrium dynamics of soft materials ranging from biopolymer networks to 3D-printed materials and soft robotics.

### [11] [Machine Learning Accelerated Simulation of Charge Regulation Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32713)
*Vijay Yadav,Sunita Kumari,Prabhat K. Jaiswal*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出电荷调节机器学习（CR-ML）方法，用图注意力网络替代计算昂贵的蒙特卡洛采样，从瞬时构型直接预测表面位点电离概率，速度较CR-MC快一个数量级且保持平衡性质一致。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从纳米颗粒到聚电解质，许多溶剂化材料通过表面基团的解离或缔合获得电荷，即电荷调节（charge regulation, CR）。现有混合CR-MC/MD方法需动态调整表面电荷，但蒙特卡洛采样成本可与MD本身相当，计算代价高昂，限制了大规模模拟。

Method: 提出电荷调节机器学习（charge regulation machine learning, CR-ML），采用图注意力网络（graph attention network），以瞬时构型、表面基团解离常数及溶液条件为输入，预测每个表面位点的电离概率。

Result: 在疏水聚阴离子（hydrophobic polyanion）体系中，CR-ML复现了CR-MC得到的平衡构象与电离度；在由聚电解质介导的双纳米颗粒更复杂体系中，CR-ML准确预测了两颗粒间产生的电荷不对称性（charge asymmetry）。电荷更新速度比CR-MC快约一个数量级，同时准确复现其平衡性质。

Conclusion: 该框架为电荷调节材料的大规模模拟提供路径，有助于理解并设计其响应行为（responsive behavior）。

Abstract: Many solvated materials, from nanoparticles to polyelectrolytes, acquire charge through the dissociation or association of surface groups, a phenomenon known as charge regulation (CR). Recent methods use hybrid charge regulation Monte Carlo (CR-MC)/molecular dynamics (MD) to evolve the system while dynamically adjusting charges on the surface groups. However, this approach is computationally expensive, as Monte Carlo sampling can be as costly as the accompanying MD. In this work, we introduce charge regulation machine learning (CR-ML), a graph attention network that predicts the ionization probability of every surface site from the instantaneous configuration, the dissociation constant of surface groups, and the solution conditions. We show that CR-ML reproduces the equilibrium conformations and degree of ionization obtained with CR-MC for a hydrophobic polyanion. We further test CR-ML on the more complex system of two nanoparticles mediated by a polyelectrolyte, where it accurately predicts the charge asymmetry that develops between the two particles. Across these systems, CR-ML updates charges an order of magnitude faster than CR-MC while accurately reproducing its equilibrium properties. This framework opens a path toward large-scale simulations of charge-regulating materials for understanding and designing their responsive behavior.

Conclusion: 该体系为设计可重构胶体结构提供了通用平台（versatile platform），在微型机器人（micro-robotics）与智能材料（smart materials）方面具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We investigate the field-directed assembly of patchy colloidal particles composed of magnetic hematite cubes encapsulated in TPM shells. These Pacman-like particles exhibit specific interactions via their magnetic patch, enabling tunable structures under external fields. A magnetic field drives patch-to-patch interactions, while an AC electric field induces stacking and spreading. By using this multi-field combination and by varying field parameters, such as frequency and amplitude, we achieve programmable transitions between distinct assembly modes. Our work offers a versatile platform for designing reconfigurable colloidal architectures with potential applications in micro-robotics and smart materials.

</details>


### [13] [Time-temperature superposition from multi-temperature chirp rheometry of thermorheologically simple materials via physics-informed Gaussian processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32895)
*Isaac Y. Miranda-Valdez,Kourosh Mobredi,Juha Koivisto,Mikko J. Alava*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作将时间-温度叠加（time-temperature superposition, TTS）约束嵌入基于啁啾流变测量（chirp rheometry）的物理信息机器学习框架，直接从时域应力-应变信号联合推断水平位移因子、本构参数与噪声尺度，并在合成数据与环氧树脂数据集上验证了模型辨识能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从瞬态响应数据推断本构律（constitutive law）是软凝聚态物理的核心反问题，但松弛谱常超出单次实验时间窗口；TTS虽可为热流变简单材料提供约束，却缺乏将其直接用于时域信号、无需先提取频域谱或手工平移曲线的推断方法。

Method: 通过啁啾流变学采集多温度时域应力-应变信号，将紧致分数阶粘弹性模型（fractional viscoelastic models）基于玻尔兹曼叠加原理（Boltzmann superposition principle）编码为因果卷积特征，并采用稀疏变分高斯过程回归（sparse variational Gaussian process regression）对多温度记录进行联合拟合。

Result: 对合成的分数阶麦克斯韦（Fractional Maxwell）数据，方法在3%至25%的应力噪声水平下均可从预设模型库中恢复生成本构律及隐含位移；应用于跨越玻璃化转变区的DGEBA-IPDA环氧数据集时，合并似然结合贝叶斯信息准则（Bayesian information criterion）可区分相互竞争的分数阶流变模型。

Conclusion: 研究将TTS主曲线（master curve）构建重新表述为对潜时间重标度与线性粘弹性的概率推断，同时指出同方差噪声（homoscedastic noise）与谱信息弱的区域等局限。

Abstract: Inferring constitutive laws from transient response data is a central inverse problem in soft condensed-matter physics, particularly when the relevant relaxation spectrum extends beyond the time window of a single experiment. Time-temperature superposition (TTS) constrains this problem for thermorheologically simple materials in the linear viscoelastic regime by allowing temperature to rescale the characteristic relaxation time without changing the underlying shape of the relaxation spectrum. We incorporate this constraint into a physics-informed machine-learning framework for time-resolved mechanical spectroscopy via chirp rheometry. We encode compact fractional viscoelastic models as causal convolutional features derived from the Boltzmann superposition principle, and we jointly fit multi-temperature chirp records using sparse variational Gaussian process regression. The model infers horizontal shift factors, constitutive parameters, and an observational-noise scale directly from time-domain stress-strain signals measured in the linear viscoelastic regime, without first extracting frequency-domain spectra or manually shifting separate curves. For synthetic Fractional Maxwell data, the method recovers the generating constitutive law from a prescribed model library and latent shifts over stress-noise levels from 3% to 25%. Applied to a DGEBA-IPDA epoxy dataset spanning the glass-transition regime, the pooled likelihood distinguishes competing fractional rheology models using the Bayesian information criterion. These results recast TTS master-curve construction as probabilistic inference over latent time rescaling and linear viscoelasticity, while also identifying limitations, such as homoscedastic noise and weakly informative spectral regimes.

</details>


### [14] [Folding of Free Polymers Under Confinement by Responsive Hydrogels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32912)
*Mahesh Aryal,Alan R. Denton*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文建立自洽平均场理论（self-consistent mean-field theory），描述自由高分子线团溶液中水凝胶的平衡溶胀与内部组成，揭示自由高分子引起非单调溶胀响应，并表明凝胶孔隙限域可使线团趋于紧凑球状构象，类似折叠蛋白态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制生物高分子在拥挤与受限环境（如细胞内部）中的折叠仍是重要难题；大分子拥挤（macromolecular crowding）与空间限域（spatial confinement）会显著影响蛋白折叠与去折叠的转变路径，需借助软性可调介质（如水凝胶）构建可模拟此类环境的实验平台。

Method: 将Flory-Rehner框架扩展至含自由高分子线团的情形，把线团建模为涨落椭球（fluctuating ellipsoids），其尺寸与形状由回转张量（gyration tensor）的本征值表征；线团在凝胶孔中的限域以有效自由体积分数（free-volume fraction）刻画，该参数由硬壁间自回避行走链（self-avoiding-walk chains）的数值计算参数化得到；并在线圈分子量与自由高分子浓度范围内数值求解耦合的化学势平衡条件。

Result: 自由高分子驱动水凝胶呈非单调溶胀响应：低浓度下为渗透溶胀（osmotic swelling），较高浓度下转为去溶胀（deswelling），与实验定性一致；凝胶孔内限域降低嵌入线团的回转半径（radius of gyration）与非球形度（asphericity），使其趋向紧凑球状构象，类似于折叠蛋白态。

Conclusion: 结果为将响应性水凝胶（responsive hydrogels）用作可调的人工类伴侣蛋白系统（artificial chaperone-like systems）、以诱导并控制生物高分子折叠提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Controlling the folding of biopolymers in crowded and confined environments, such as the interior of biological cells, remains a significant challenge. Macromolecular crowding and spatial confinement can strongly influence transition pathways between folded and unfolded states of proteins. Such environments can be mimicked in experiments by confining polymers within soft, tunable media, such as hydrogels -- cross-linked polymer networks that swell by absorbing solvent and exhibit stimulus-dependent responsiveness. Here, we develop a self-consistent mean-field theory for the equilibrium swelling and internal composition of a hydrogel immersed in a solution of free polymer coils. The theory extends the Flory-Rehner framework by incorporating free polymer coils, modeled as fluctuating ellipsoids, whose sizes and shapes are characterized by the eigenvalues of the gyration tensor. Confinement of the coils within gel pores is characterized by an effective free-volume fraction, parametrized from numerical calculations for self-avoiding-walk chains between hard walls. Coupled chemical potential equilibrium conditions are solved numerically over ranges of coil molecular weight and free polymer concentration. We find that free polymers drive a non-monotonic swelling response of hydrogels, with osmotic swelling at low concentrations followed by deswelling at higher concentrations, in qualitative agreement with experiment. We further demonstrate that confinement within gel pores reduces the radius of gyration and asphericity of embedded polymer coils, driving them toward compact, globular conformations analogous to folded protein states. These results establish a theoretical foundation for using responsive hydrogels as tunable, artificial chaperone-like systems capable of inducing and controlling biopolymer folding.

</details>

*Ioannis P. Zois*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出 M5-膜磁荷的源适配 Chern–Connes 描述，在非交换与商链环（quotient linking）扇区中构造拓扑荷公式，光滑极限下退化为 M-理论四形式通量积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有偶余维非交换叶状不变（NCFI）框架依赖横向基本循环上循环与偶 K-类配对，而 M5-膜的余维为奇数，现有框架不能直接适用，需构造适配的奇余维修正描述。

Method: 从 M5-膜源的局部余维五几何出发，在四维链环（link）上测量磁荷；以源/链环扩张（source/linking extension）的边界映射提供所需奇 K-类，仅在非交换环面紧化、背景 B-场下 D-膜约化、大 C-场 M5 极限、商/轨道流形链环等物理机制出现时引入非交换或群胚代数。

Result: 得到一个拓扑荷公式；在光滑局部模型中链环为普通四维球面，配对退化为标准 M-理论四形式场强的通量积分。

Conclusion: 该构造仅提供拓扑荷公式，不构成完整 M5-膜动力学或完整 M-理论作用量的推导。

Abstract: We formulate a source-adapted Chern--Connes description of M5-brane magnetic charge in physically motivated noncommutative and quotient linking sectors. The starting point is the local codimension-five geometry of an M5-brane source: the magnetic charge is measured on a four-dimensional link. In the smooth local model the link is the ordinary four-sphere, and the pairing reduces to the standard flux integral of the M-theory four-form field strength.
  The construction is motivated by the published even-codimensional Noncommutative Foliation Invariant (NCFI for short), where Connes' transverse fundamental cyclic cocycle is paired with an even transverse-geometric \(K\)-class. For an M5-brane the relevant codimension is odd, so the required odd \(K\)-class is supplied instead by the boundary map of the source/linking extension. Noncommutative or groupoid algebras are used only when they arise from physical mechanisms: Matrix/M-theory compactification on noncommutative tori, reduction to D-branes in background \(B\)-fields, large \(C\)-field M5 limits, or quotient/orbifold source links. The result is a topological charge formula, not a derivation of the full M5-brane dynamics or of the complete M-theory action.

</details>


### [16] [Local Thermodynamics and Rényi Bounds on Black Hole Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31680)
*Sudhanva Joshi,Sunil Kumar Mishra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该摘要考察AdS空间中热力学稳定黑洞并合（merger）的Rényi熵不等式，指出更强的并合约束依赖于联合初态的关联信息，而非仅由质量与温度决定的热边缘态即可刻画；在正则双洞（canonical two-hole）描述下，稀有能量涨落中的关联可在保持热态与互信息趋于零的同时改变半经典阶的联合Rényi熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作提出用Rényi熵不等式加强反德西特（Anti de Sitter, AdS）空间中热力学稳定黑洞并合的面积定理（area theorem）界。由于更强界依赖联合初态，而质量与温度只确定局域热边缘态（local thermal marginals），需澄清这一区别的实际含义与适用范围。

Method: 在正则双洞（canonical two-hole）描述内量化上述区别：构造仅局限于稀有能量涨落（rare energy fluctuations）的关联，使两个热态保持完全不变、迹距离（trace distance）与互信息（mutual information）趋于零；并在所有直至1的正Rényi阶上一致地确定与受控统计独立（statistical independence）逼近相容的最小熵。

Result: 对于等质量Schwarzschild–AdS₄黑洞，优化后的正则质量约束（canonical mass constraint）在半经典阶（semiclassical order）上退化为面积界（area bound）。该退化仅涉及推断界所需的信息，并不产生更轻的可达遗迹（attainable remnant），也不导致动力学不稳定性（dynamical instability）。

Conclusion: 更强的并合约束需要超越单黑洞热力学、对初态关联加以控制；仅凭质量与温度等单洞热力学量无法给出加强界。

Abstract: Rényi entropy inequalities have been proposed to strengthen the area-theorem bound on mergers of thermodynamically stable black holes in Anti de Sitter (AdS) space. The stronger bounds depend on the joint initial state, whereas masses and temperatures fix only its local thermal marginals. We quantify this distinction inside the canonical two-hole description. Correlations confined to rare energy fluctuations can leave both thermal states exactly intact and drive the trace distance and mutual information to zero, yet still change the joint Rényi entropy at leading semiclassical order. We determine the smallest entropy compatible with a controlled approach to statistical independence, uniformly over all positive Rényi orders up to one. For equal Schwarzschild--AdS$_4$ holes, the resulting optimized canonical mass constraint reduces to the area bound at leading semiclassical order. The reduction concerns only the information needed to infer a bound; it does not produce a lighter attainable remnant or a dynamical instability. Stronger merger constraints therefore require control of the initial correlations beyond single black hole thermodynamics.

</details>


### [17] [Kasner Exponents from the Bouncing Singularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31699)
*Hyunwoo Oh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出黑洞奇点附近的Kasner指数决定了热边界两点关联函数中额外幂次$b_t$，并给出公式$b_t=(d-1)\max\left(\frac{p_i-p_t}{1-p_t},\frac12\right)$，与已有数值结果吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞奇点可通过热边界两点关联函数（thermal boundary two-point correlator）探测，但当存在反弹测地线（bouncing geodesic）时，关联函数中出现额外幂次$b_t$，其物理来源此前缺乏解析刻画，尤其对$g_{tt}g_{rr}\neq-1$的情形尚无公式。

Method: 针对AdS$_{d+1}$中的平面黑洞膜（planar black branes），利用奇点附近的Kasner指数（Kasner exponents）$p_t$与$p_i$，解析推导大$N$下边界关联函数奇异行为中额外幂次$b_t$的表达式。

Result: 推导出$b_t=(d-1)\max\left(\frac{p_i-p_t}{1-p_t},\frac12\right)$；该公式复现了Čeplak与Valach对$g_{tt}g_{rr}=-1$黑洞膜的数值关系，并在$g_{tt}g_{rr}\neq-1$情形下与多数数据一致且拟合优于其关系式。

Conclusion: 奇点附近的Kasner指数可定量决定边界关联函数的奇异幂次$b_t$，据此对Kasner指数已知但$b_t$尚未计算的理论给出预测。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: This work investigates the thermodynamics of non-stationary anti-de Sitter (adS) black holes and their gauge-gravity duals. TL;DR: 该文将耗散流体的变分建模（variational modelling of dissipative fluids）引入相对论弹性理论（relativistic elasticity），构建了可与广义相对论（general relativity）兼容的塑性唯象理论，并借助热力学第二定律（second law of thermodynamics）在相对论框架下给出屈服准则（yield criterion）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星（neutron star）弹性外壳（elastic crust）在其物理中很重要，但现有弹性理论限于微扰（perturbative）范围，无法描述永久塑性形变（permanent plastic deformations），而后者与脉冲星跃变（pulsar glitches）、磁星耀发（magnetar flares）和引力波（gravitational waves）连续辐射相关。塑性建模本身困难，主要源于其耗散（dissipative）特性及所需的多尺度方法（multiscale methods）。

Method: 利用近期耗散流体变分建模（variational modelling of dissipative fluids）的发展，将相对论弹性理论扩展至包含塑性唯象（plastic phenomenology）；并以热力学第二定律（second law of thermodynamics）在相对论情形下引入塑性理论的屈服准则（yield criterion）。

Result: 所得理论具备与现代牛顿形式（modern Newtonian formalism）相同的自由度，同时与广义相对论（general relativity）兼容，并在相对论框架下给出了屈服准则。

Conclusion: 该工作针对塑性建模中耗散性这一首要挑战给出相对论性处理方案，为描述中子星塑性现象提供了与广义相对论相容的理论基础；摘要未涉及对多尺度方法问题的解决或定量验证。

Abstract: The elastic crust of a neutron star has long been established as a key player in relevant aspects of neutron star physics. However, the state-of-the-art theory of elasticity is restricted to the perturbative domain and does not account for permanent plastic deformations which should be relevant for pulsar glitches, magnetar flares, and the continuous emission of gravitational waves. Plasticity, however, is a historically challenging phenomenon to model - even in the Newtonian regime. This is owed mainly to its dissipative character and the multiscale methods required to consistently reproduce observed phenomena. In this work, we address this first challenge by making use of recent developments in the variational modelling of dissipative fluids to extend the theory of relativistic elasticity to include plastic phenomenology. The resulting theory possesses the same freedoms as the modern Newtonian formalism, while now being compatible with general relativity. The second law of thermodynamics is then used to introduce the yield criterion of plasticity theory in the relativistic setting.

</details>

*L. Herrera,A. Di prisco*

Main category: gr-qc

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在探讨对称性的瞬时出现（而非持续存在）如何影响辐射测地线流体的动力学演化，并评估其在引力坍缩（gravitational collapse）研究中的潜在应用。

Method: 引入不对称因子（asymmetry factor）概念，使给定对称性可在特定类时坐标值处瞬时出现；据此详细分析TKV出现时各相关物理变量的变化及其印记。

Conclusion: 对称性的瞬时出现对系统演化具有决定性影响，所揭示的热效应值得关注；相关结果可为引力坍缩研究提供潜在应用。

Abstract: In this study we analyze the effects produced by the sudden appearance of a time-like Killing vector (TKV), on the evolution of radiating geodesic fluids. This is achieved by resorting to the concept of asymmetry factor which allows the instantaneous appearance of a given symmetry for a given value of the time-like coordinate. All relevant physical variables are analyzed in detail showing the imprints of the emergence of TKV at some point of the evolution. The main lesson that can be extracted from the obtained results is that the sudden appearance of a TKV, even for an infinitesimal period of time, drastically changes the subsequent evolution of the system. Of particular relevance is the appearance of a thermal effect linking the asymmetry factor and the relaxation time. The potential applications of these results, on the study of gravitational collapse, are discussed at the end of the paper.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对 Rashba 与 Dresselhaus 耦合共存、且电场与磁场同时作用时二维半导体带间光学响应的统一描述；本文旨在建立该统一框架并寻找可光学辨识两类自旋轨道耦合的判据。

Method: 构建统一理论框架，分析交叉场（crossed fields）下由面内电场产生的有效自旋轨道漂移场（spin-orbit drift field）对 Landau 谱（Landau spectrum）与旋量态（spinor states）的修正；零磁场情形映射为 Landau–Zener 自旋动力学，并结合联合态密度（joint density of states）与 Franz–Keldysh 响应进行分析。

Result: 零外场下带隙以上总联合态密度不变，但出现亚带隙结构：纯 Rashba 耦合给出反平方根奇异性（inverse square root singularity），有限 Dresselhaus 项则给出台阶与对数峰。零磁场下 Franz–Keldysh 响应随电场减小由绝热（diabatic）向非绝热（adiabatic）自旋动力学发生渡越；弱场 Rashba 区中无场奇异性变为有限峰，峰高按 $\mathcal E^{-1/3}$ 标度、峰位趋于 $E_g-E_{\rm SO}$。垂直磁场下自旋轨道耦合使 Landau 吸收线移动并分裂、产生反交叉（anticrossings）而总权重不变，Rashba 分裂由弱场的 $\sqrt{B}$ 依赖转为强场的线性依赖；交叉场中电场展宽 Landau 线并驱动向 Franz–Keldysh 区渡越，自旋轨道耦合使每个双重态（doublet）两成员产生相反移动与不同线宽。

Conclusion: 电场、磁场与 Rashba/Dresselhaus 自旋轨道耦合的联合作用在二维半导体带间光学谱中产生可辨识的特征结构；亚带隙谱形、峰高标度与 Landau 线分裂行为可作为区分与定量表征两类自旋轨道耦合的光学探针。

Abstract: We develop a unified theory of the interband optical response of a two-dimensional semiconductor with simultaneous Rashba and Dresselhaus spin-orbit couplings in electric and magnetic fields. In crossed fields, the in-plane electric field generates an effective spin-orbit drift field that modifies the Landau spectrum and spinor states, while at zero magnetic field the problem maps onto Landau--Zener spin dynamics.At zero external fields, the total joint density of states above the band gap remains unchanged, while characteristic subgap structures appear. Pure Rashba coupling produces an inverse square root singularity, whereas a finite Dresselhaus term gives a step and a logarithmic peak, allowing the two couplings to be distinguished optically. At zero magnetic field, the Franz--Keldysh response shows a crossover from diabatic to adiabatic spin dynamics as the electric field is reduced. In the weak-field Rashba regime, the field-free singularity becomes a finite peak whose height scales as $\mathcal E^{-1/3}$ and whose position approaches $E_g-E_{\rm SO}$, providing a direct measure of the Rashba energy. In a perpendicular magnetic field, spin-orbit coupling shifts and splits the Landau absorption lines and produces anticrossings without changing their total weights. The Rashba splitting changes from a $\sqrt{B}$ dependence at weak fields to a linear dependence at strong fields. Finally, in crossed electric and magnetic fields, the electric field broadens the Landau lines and drives a continuous crossover toward the Franz--Keldysh regime, while spin-orbit coupling produces opposite shifts and different widths for the two members of each doublet.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机在于考察分数量子霍尔体系中边缘态间耦合结构下的热流涨落（heat current fluctuations）与关联特性，借助可精确求解的模型刻画相互作用对热输运统计的影响。

Method: 将ν=1/3下经收缩耦合的两个边缘态映射为含局域杂质的相互作用Tomonaga-Luttinger液体，其相互作用参数κ=1/2满足重费米化方法的适用条件；通过求解重费米化后的哈密顿量（Hamiltonian），计算热流及有限频率的热自关联与交叉关联函数。

Result: 结果表明，关联函数随频率呈非对称行为，分别对应吸收噪声与发射噪声（absorption and emission noise）。在低/高温极限及强/弱背散射（backscattering）区域等极限情形下进行了考察；弱背散射区高电压下Lorenz数趋近4L₀，提示相互作用导致Wiedemann-Franz定律的重标度。对热流涨落的细致分析给出了各区域主要贡献的表达式，并揭示分配噪声占主导。

Conclusion: 该体系的热流涨落可由重费米化方法定量刻画，其频率非对称性、修正的Wiedemann-Franz关系以及分配噪声主导的特征，反映出相互作用对分数量子霍尔边缘热输运统计的决定性作用。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate heat current correlations between two edge states coupled through a constriction in the fractional quantum Hall regime at filling factor $ν= 1/3$. This configuration allows us to map the system onto an interacting Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid with a local impurity, where the interaction parameter takes the value $κ= 1/2$. This specific parameter value enables the application of the refermionization method. By solving the refermionized Hamiltonian, we compute the heat current and evaluate the finite-frequency heat auto- and cross-correlation functions. Our results reveal asymmetric behavior with frequency, corresponding to absorption and emission noise, respectively. Furthermore, we examine several limiting cases, including low- and high-temperature limits as well as strong- and weak-backscattering regimes. In the weak backscattering regime, the Lorenz number approaches $4L_0$ at high voltage, suggesting a rescaling of the Wiedemann-Franz law due to interactions. A detailed study of the heat current fluctuations has made it possible to identify and derive expressions for the dominant contribution in the various regimes of interest, and to reveal the predominance of partition noise.

</details>

Result: BCDI 重建图像揭示了反相磁畴壁（antiphase magnetic domain walls）在反铁磁有序（antiferromagnetically ordered）样品中传播的毫秒时间尺度（milliseconds time-scale）动力学过程。

Conclusion: 单发 BCDI 被证明是追踪有限尺寸体系中畴壁实空间与时间动力学的有力方法，为相关超越摩尔定律应用提供成像手段。

Abstract: Domain wall dynamics and their manipulation in finite-size systems serve as a promising platform for beyond Moore's law applications. Yet, the imaging techniques to track their motion in real-space and time are limited. Here, we use magnetic domain walls formed in permalloy artificial square lattice to demonstrate that single-shot Bragg coherent diffraction imaging (BCDI) is a powerful approach for this purpose. Our BCDI reconstructed images revealed milliseconds time-scale dynamics of the antiphase magnetic domain walls traveling through an antiferromagnetically ordered sample.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前布洛赫点奇点（Bloch point singularities）仅以孤立客体或单一拓扑织构组成部分的形式被观测，缺乏对其集体排布与稳定机制的认知。

Abstract: Bloch point singularities play an important role in magnetic systems, mediating changes in topology in three dimensional configurations. However, until now they have only been observed as isolated objects or as part of individual topological textures. Here we present the experimental observation of a 1D dipole chain of Bloch points in an anisotropic chiral magnet using soft X-ray magnetic vector tomography. We identify that the chain of Bloch point singularities lies along a boundary between regions of opposite chirality and that neighbouring Bloch and anti-Bloch points are stable at separations down to 60 nm. The stability of the Bloch points is determined to be closely related to the surrounding topology of the magnetisation configuration and a threshold separation for the spontaneous annihilation of Bloch point-anti-Bloch point pairs within chiral domain walls is identified to be approximately 90 nm in distance. ### [57] [The Extended Diode Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32090)
*Jianhong Yang,Gonghao Sun,Tinglan Li*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沿用逾 75 年的经典推导存在两处概念简化：多数载流子被当作惰性背景，且结电压与外加偏压被混为一谈。这些简化使高注入区的电流行为只能依赖经验性串联电阻项来修补，缺乏自洽的物理描述。

Method: 建立自洽框架（self-consistent framework），考虑过剩多数载流子与少数载流子耦合所生成的基区电场对内部电势分布（internal potential distribution）的根本性改变，并据此推导出闭式扩展二极管方程。

Result: 所得扩展二极管方程能够复现高注入滚降行为，且不需要引入外部串联电阻，从而去除了经典理论中一直保留的经验性修补项（empirical patch）。

Conclusion: 基区电场是影响 p-n 结内部电势分布与 I-V 特性的基本因素；将载流子耦合纳入自洽描述后，可获得物理上更完备且形式闭合的二极管方程，替代依赖经验参数的传统处理。

Abstract: For more than 75 years, the p-n junction current-voltage characteristic has been derived by treating majority carriers as an inert background and conflating the junction voltage with the applied bias. Here we show that the base electric field, generated by the coupling of excess majority and minority carriers, fundamentally alters the internal potential distribution. This self-consistent framework yields a closed-form extended diode equation that reproduces the high-injection roll-off without invoking an external series resistance, thereby eliminating an empirical patch that has persisted in the classical theory.

</details>


### [58] [A Self-Consistent Two-Carrier Drift-Diffusion Framework for p-n Junctions: Extended Diode Equation, Power Dissipation, and Base Transit Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32112)
*Jianhong Yang,Tinglan Li,Gonghao Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出一个自洽的双载流子漂移-扩散（drift-diffusion）框架，仅沿用经典假设即证明过剩多数载流子是静电强制参与的输运主体，从而在器件层面自然导出含 n=2 滚降（roll-off）的扩展二极管方程，并给出三项无参数预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 p-n 结理论仅由少子扩散推导电流-电压特性，将多数载流子视为惰性背景、准中性基区视为无场、结电压等同于外加偏压；这些前提在高偏压与高注入（high-level injection）下同时失效，观测到的电流滚降只能靠外接经验串联电阻来拟合。

Method: 建立自洽的双载流子漂移-扩散（two-carrier drift-diffusion）框架，不引入超出经典假设的新假定；由精确双侧边界条件闭合双极扩散方程（ambipolar diffusion equation），得到统一的过剩载流子分布，基区电场分解为欧姆（ohmic）分量与 Dember 分量，四个电流分量均以闭式给出且其和与位置无关。

Result: 器件层面闭合为扩展二极管方程，以 Shockley 方程为低注入、长基区极限，并内禀重现 n=2 滚降；结电压成为局限于空间电荷区（space-charge region）的自限压降，与外加偏压之差由精确基区压降描述。高电流下 75-90% 的耗散由结区迁移至准中性基区，场助加速使基区渡越时间最多缩短六倍。三项无参数预测为：边界过剩载流子等于掺杂时穿过 3/2 的普适理想因子（ideality factor）交叉、每十倍掺杂约 119 mV 的高注入起始电压标度、以及延续至高注入区的几何因子。

Conclusion: 多数载流子过剩并非被动背景，而是静电强制的输运参与者；据此可在不借助经验串联电阻的情况下统一描述低注入至高注入的 p-n 结特性，并给出可检验的定量预测。

Abstract: The classical theory of p-n junctions derives the current-voltage characteristic from minority-carrier diffusion alone: majority carriers are treated as an inert background, the quasi-neutral base as field-free, and the junction voltage as equal to the applied bias. These premises fail together under high bias and high-level injection, where the observed current roll-off is reproduced only by appending an empirical series resistance outside the junction physics. Here we develop a self-consistent two-carrier drift-diffusion framework that introduces no assumption beyond the classical ones. Excess majority carriers are shown to be electrostatically forced transport participants: the ambipolar diffusion equation, closed by exact two-sided boundary conditions, yields a unified excess-carrier profile. The resulting base field separates into an ohmic and a Dember component, and all four current components follow in closed form, their sum being position independent. At device level the framework closes into an extended diode equation that contains the Shockley equation as its low-injection, long-base limit and reproduces the n=2 roll-off intrinsically. The junction voltage emerges as a self-limiting drop confined to the space-charge region and separated from the applied bias by exact base drops. Two device-level corrections are quantified: at high current, 75-90% of the dissipation migrates from the junction into the quasi-neutral base, and field-assisted acceleration shortens the base transit time by up to a factor of six. Three parameter-free predictions follow: a universal ideality-factor crossover that passes through 3/2 when the boundary excess equals the doping, a high-injection onset voltage that scales as 119 mV per decade of doping, and a geometry factor that survives into high injection.

</details>

TL;DR: 在单谷有效质量模型下，研究将扭曲光子（twisted photon）的总角动量（TAM）投影转移为n型半导体表面光电子轨道角动量（OAM）所需的材料条件。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨扭曲光子的总角动量投影能否以及如何高效转移到半导体表面发射光电子的轨道角动量，并明确所需的材料参数条件。

Method: 采用单谷有效质量模型（single-valley effective-mass model）描述表面光电发射，计算GaAs、InSb、InAs0.34Sb0.66与Hg0.76Cd0.24Te在类拉盖尔-高斯（Laguerre–Gaussian-like）动量分布扭曲光子照射下的OAM分辨谱；以平均电子OAM、其均方根宽度及OAM投影等于光子TAM投影的电子占比表征转移效果。

Result: 高效转移要求导带电子横向动量远小于光子横向动量宽度，因而有利于低温、小导带有效质量与浅施主能级；GaAs因有效质量较大、施主能级较深而不利。

Conclusion: 论文还讨论了苛刻的光子波包参数、向狄拉克/外尔半金属（Dirac and Weyl semimetals）的推广及发射后OAM选择；认为通过光电发射产生OAM不确定度为零的涡旋电子较困难，但生成具有有限OAM色散的单电子涡旋态仍可行。

Abstract: We study the material requirements for transferring the total-angular-momentum (TAM) projection of a twisted photon to the orbital angular momentum (OAM) of a photoelectron emitted from an $n$-type semiconductor surface. Within a single-valley effective-mass model of surface photoemission, we calculate OAM-resolved spectra for GaAs, InSb, $\mathrm{InAs}_{0.34}\mathrm{Sb}_{0.66}$, and $\mathrm{Hg}_{0.76}\mathrm{Cd}_{0.24}\mathrm{Te}$ irradiated by twisted photons with Laguerre--Gaussian--like momentum profile. The OAM transfer is characterized by the mean electron OAM, its root-mean-squared width, and the fraction of electrons with OAM-projection equal to the photon TAM-projection. Efficient transfer requires the transverse momentum of the conduction electron to be much smaller than the transverse momentum width of the photon, which favors low temperatures, small conduction-band effective masses, and shallow donor levels. GaAs is less favorable within the present model because of its larger effective mass and deeper donor levels. We also discuss the demanding photon-wave packet parameters, possible extensions to Dirac and Weyl semimetals, and post-emission OAM selection. Although the generation of vortex electrons with a vanishing OAM uncertainty via photoemission seems challenging, we argue that it is still possible to generate single-electron vortex states with a finite OAM dispersion.

</details>


### [60] [Magnonic time crystal with lasing coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32199)
*Jiantao Song,C. K. Wei,Shuhuan Ma,Yiming Pan,Zhijian Chen,Li Yu,Wei Lu,Bimu Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在室温钇铁石榴石（yttrium iron garnet, YIG）体系中利用泵浦诱导磁子模式实现磁子时间晶体（magnonic time crystal, MTC），其辐射场呈现激光级相干性（线宽 260.0 Hz、一阶关联衰减时间 995 μs），并进一步将时间晶体动力学映射为频率分辨非线性特征用于储备池计算（reservoir computing）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散时间晶体（discrete time crystal, DTC）的研究多依赖内部序参量测量，如何将这种序转化为高相干、可外部访问的辐射仍是难题：泵浦驱动的非线性过程须在维持次谐波（subharmonic）稳定性的同时提供足够增益以补偿耗散。

Method: 采用钇铁石榴石中的泵浦诱导磁子模式，以微波拍频驱动（microwave beat drive）产生周期倍增（period doubling）；建立理论模型将响应归因于三磁子汇合（three-magnon confluence），并提取辐射场线宽与一阶关联函数，进一步将时间动力学映射为频率分辨非线性特征。

Result: 在有限参数范围内观察到稳健的周期倍增，强驱动下出现高阶 MTC 的光谱特征；辐射线宽窄化至 260.0 Hz，一阶关联衰减时间为 995 μs。模型给出与实验一致的稳定区域，并预测可能的混沌（chaos）转变。

Conclusion: 该结果通过本征磁子-磁子相互作用与激光相干性，将时间晶体序与非线性信息处理（储备池计算）联系起来，为室温下相干时间晶体辐射及其信息应用提供了途径。

Abstract: Discrete time crystals (DTC) can respond at an integer multiple of the drive period. Internal order-parameter measurements have advanced studies of this novel non-equilibrium phase of matter; however, turning this order into highly coherent, externally accessible radiation remains challenging: pump-driven nonlinear processes must supply sufficient gain to compensate dissipation while preserving subharmonic stability. Here we realize a room-temperature magnonic time crystal (MTC) with radiation of lasing coherence using a pump-induced magnon mode in yttrium iron garnet. A microwave beat drive produces robust period doubling over a finite parameter range, with spectral signatures of higher-order MTC at stronger driving. The emitted field exhibits linewidth narrowing to 260.0 Hz and a first-order correlation decay time of $995 μs$. Our model links this response to three-magnon confluence, yields a stability region consistent with experiment and predicts a possible transition to chaos. We further map the complex temporal dynamics into frequency-resolved nonlinear features for reservoir computing. These results bridge time-crystalline order and nonlinear information processing through intrinsic magnon-magnon interactions and lasing coherence.

</details>


### [61] [Nonlocal Thermoelectric Transport in Degenerate Electron Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32272)
*Albert Beardo,Adrián Ramos-Martín,Jordi Tur-Prats,F. Xavier Alvarez,Juan Camacho*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 论文提出通量导数形式（Flux Derivative Formalism, FDF），一种从玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equation）系统导出非局域本构方程（nonlocal constitutive equations）的动理学框架，并据此构建电荷与热输运的闭合宏观描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当输运的特征长度尺度与载流子平均自由程（mean free path）相当时，局域本构描述失效，需要一种既能刻画非局域效应、又无需完整求解玻尔兹曼问题的宏观理论。

Method: 以玻尔兹曼方程为起点发展FDF，推导各向同性电子系统中电荷与热输运的耦合方程及一致的边界条件；在Callaway模型下给出简并电子系统的显式输运系数，并与空间分辨的玻尔兹曼解进行对比验证。

Result: 电阻性碰撞与动量守恒碰撞的相对强度只改变输运系数而不改变非局域本构结构，实现从电阻主导到集体流体力学极限（hydrodynamic limit）的连续描述；电子热方程呈Guyer-Krumhansl形式，其预测在Cu与Ni的尺寸依赖热输运测量中得到验证，并在横向尺寸接近平均自由程时与玻尔兹曼解定量吻合。

Conclusion: 耦合热电输运的边值问题可确定完整的几何依赖输运矩阵，理论预测几何依赖的Seebeck与Peltier系数，同时保持Onsager倒易关系（Onsager reciprocity）与Kelvin关系。

Abstract: When the characteristic length scales of transport become comparable to the carrier mean free path, the local constitutive description breaks down. We develop the Flux Derivative Formalism (FDF), a kinetic framework that provides a systematic route from the Boltzmann equation to nonlocal constitutive equations. We use it to derive coupled equations for charge and heat transport in isotropic electronic systems, together with boundary conditions derived consistently within this approach, yielding a closed macroscopic description that can be applied to complex geometries without solving the full Boltzmann problem. The explicit transport coefficients are derived for degenerate electronic systems within the Callaway model. A central result is that varying the relative strength of resistive and momentum-conserving collisions changes the transport coefficients but not the non-local constitutive structure, providing a continuous description from resistive-dominated transport to the collective hydrodynamic limit. The electronic heat equation takes a Guyer-Krumhansl form, whose predictions are validated against size-dependent thermal-transport measurements in Cu and Ni. For coupled thermoelectric transport, the resulting boundary-value problem determines the complete geometry-dependent transport matrix. Comparison with spatially resolved Boltzmann solutions confirms quantitative accuracy down to transverse dimensions comparable to the carrier mean free path. The theory predicts geometry-dependent Seebeck and Peltier coefficients while preserving Onsager reciprocity and the Kelvin relation.

</details>


### [62] [Quantum Landau Lifshitz Gilbert equation in ferromagnetic conductors and magnetic insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32405)
*Ji Yang Zhu,Zheng Chuan Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作建立一个微观框架，从铁磁导体中的 s-d 交换相互作用（s-d exchange interaction）与磁性绝缘体中的自旋-声子相互作用（spin-phonon interaction）出发，分别推导出量子 LLG 方程及 Gilbert 阻尼项，表明阻尼并非普适标量参数，而可具有空间依赖与张量特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解磁阻尼（magnetic damping）的微观起源对于描述纳米尺度磁系统的磁化动力学（magnetization dynamics）是必要的，而现有描述常以单一标量参数刻画 Gilbert 阻尼，缺乏对具体相互作用来源的微观推导。

Method: 对铁磁导体，以局域磁矩与巡游电子间的 s-d 交换相互作用为出发点（等价于电子-磁振子相互作用（electron-magnon interaction）），由自旋扩散方程（spin diffusion equation）及其中的自旋转移力矩（spin transfer torque）得到量子 LLG 方程；对无自由电子的磁性绝缘体，从有效自旋-声子相互作用出发，利用线性响应理论（linear response theory）解析推导出含 Gilbert 阻尼张量的量子 LLG 方程。

Result: 不同微观相互作用导致定性不同的磁阻尼结构：在导体中阻尼由 s-d 交换（电子-磁振子）过程主导，在绝缘体中则由自旋-声子过程主导；Gilbert 阻尼不能被普适标量参数描述，而可表现出空间依赖性与张量特性。

Conclusion: 磁阻尼的结构由底层微观相互作用决定，因此 Gilbert 阻尼应被视为可能具有空间依赖和张量性质的量，而非单一普适标量；该微观框架为纳米磁系统磁化动力学的描述提供了基于具体相互作用的推导路径。

Abstract: Understanding the microscopic origin of magnetic damping is essential for describing magnetization dynamics in nanoscale magnetic systems. In this work, we develop a microscopic framework to derive Gilbert damping terms from specific interactions in ferromagnetic conductors and magnetic insulators. In ferromagnetic conductors, magnetic damping is dominated by the s d exchange interaction between the localized magnetic moments and the itinerant electrons, which is effectively equivalent to an electronmagnon interaction. From the spin diffusion equation and the spin transfer torque therein, we obtain the corresponding quantum LLG equation. In magnetic insulators, where free electrons are absent, spin phonon interactions become the primary source of the damping.Starting from an effective spin phonon interaction and using linear response theory, we derive a quantum LLG equation with a Gilbert damping tensor analytically. Our results demonstrate that different microscopic interactions lead to qualitatively different structures of magnetic damping. Instead of being described by a universal scalar parameter, Gilbert damping can exhibit spatial dependence and tensorial characteristics determined by the underlying microscopic interactions.

</details>


### [63] [Multifractal Magnons Around Localized Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32457)
*Adarsh Hullahalli,Yusuf Khand,Christos Panagopoulos,Christina Psaroudaki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文指出，在无无序（disorder）与无准周期性（quasiperiodicity）的二维晶体磁体中，单个局域缺陷（如自旋缺陷或拓扑磁斯格明子（skyrmion））可将近简并磁振子（magnon）态混合，产生多尺度干涉图案与多重分形（multifractal）波函数，并可通过与磁性活性内部形变模式杂化使部分多重分形态获得非零磁偶极矩而被均匀交变场激发、在实验上观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重分形波函数通常与无序或准周期性相伴出现；作者旨在说明即使在完全晶体化的二维磁体中，仅凭局域缺陷即可在介观尺度（mesoscopic scale）上产生多重分形磁振子态，并解决其可观测性问题。

Method: 以有限样品中的局域缺陷混合近简并磁振子态为机制，分别针对简单自旋缺陷与拓扑磁斯格明子（手性磁体（chiral magnet）与阻挫磁体（frustrated magnet））进行大尺度自旋波（spin-wave）计算，并对带隙以上模式（above-gap modes）做多重分形诊断。

Result: 大尺度计算在整个可及系统尺寸范围内给出清晰的多重分形诊断信号；斯格明子通过与磁性活性内部形变模式杂化，使部分多重分形态获得非零磁偶极矩，从而可由均匀交变（ac）场激发，并在实际频率分辨率下保持可分辨。

Conclusion: 无需无序或准周期性，局域缺陷诱导的态混合即可产生介观多重分形磁振子态；斯格明子提供了一条直接观测介观磁振子多重分形性（magnon multifractality）的实验路径。

Abstract: Multifractal wavefunctions fluctuate over a hierarchy of spatial scales, remaining spatially extended while exhibiting strongly nonuniform structure. We show that magnons in otherwise crystalline two-dimensional magnets can exhibit such behavior on mesoscopic scales, without disorder or quasiperiodicity. A localized defect can mix discrete groups of nearly degenerate magnon states in a finite sample, generating multiscale interference patterns and multifractal wavefunctions. We demonstrate this mechanism for both a simple spin defect and topological magnetic skyrmions. Large-scale spin-wave calculations for skyrmions in chiral and frustrated magnets reveal clear multifractal diagnostics over the full accessible range of system sizes of above-gap modes. Crucially, skyrmions make these states experimentally observable by hybridizing them with magnetically active internal deformation modes, which gives a subset of the multifractal states a nonzero magnetic dipole moment. These states can therefore be excited by uniform ac fields and remain distinguishable at realistic frequency resolution, providing a direct route to observing mesoscopic magnon multifractality.

</details>


### [64] [Excitonic Stark Response Induced by Quantum Capacitance in van der Waals Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32532)
*Xiangzhi Hong,Yongkang Jian,Yang Chen,Rukai Cang,Yuanlong Guo,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Baigeng Wang,Erfu Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用层间激子（interlayer excitons）作局域探针，证明在存在界面电荷转移（interfacial charge transfer）时，几何电容模型（geometric capacitor model）失效，量子电容（quantum capacitance）主导内建静电学，并提供了原位光学探测局域化学势的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华异质结（van der Waals heterostructures）中的静电控制通常以几何电容模型描述，即内部电场完全由外部门电压决定；本文旨在检验该假设在界面电荷转移存在时是否成立。

Method: 以层间激子作为局域探针，测量与偶极取向相关的能级分裂（dipole-dependent energy splitting），并在门诱导几何电场名义上被抵消的条件下考察其行为。

Result: 观察到即使在几何场名义抵消时仍存在偶极依赖的能级分裂，源于门电场与载流子诱导电场在量子电容作用下的不完全抵消；多体重整化（many-body renormalization）对两种相反偶极取向基本对称。

Conclusion: 量子电容在界面静电学中起核心作用，该分裂可作为局域化学势（local chemical potential）的直接光学度量，并提供原位（in operando）探测量子电容的途径。

Abstract: Precise electrostatic control in van der Waals heterostructures is commonly described by a geometric capacitor model, which assumes that the internal electric field is fully determined by external gate voltages. Here, we show that this description breaks down in the presence of interfacial charge transfer, where quantum capacitance modifies the internal electrostatics. Using interlayer excitons as local probes, we observe a dipole-dependent energy splitting that persists even when the gate-induced geometric field is nominally canceled. This splitting reflects incomplete cancellation between gate- and carrier-induced fields set by quantum capacitance, while many-body renormalization is largely symmetric with respect to the two opposite dipole orientations, allowing the splitting to provide a direct optical measure of the local chemical potential. Our results reveal the central role of quantum capacitance in interface electrostatics and provide a route to probe it in operando.

</details>


### [65] [Magnetic Anisotropy and Magnetization Dynamics in Si/Ni Multilayers Probed by FMR and VSM Magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32565)
*Dushyant Singh,Subha Maity,Tharundev V,Subhavenkadesh SS,Vigneshwari SR,Krista R. Khiangte*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作系统研究了溅射[Si(40 Å)/Ni(t_Ni Å)]₂₀多层膜在室温下的磁各向异性与磁化动力学，通过SQUID-VSM与角度依赖铁磁共振（ferromagnetic resonance, FMR）揭示界面与体各向异性的竞争，并给出各向异性交叉厚度约27 Å。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清Si/Ni多层膜中Ni厚度与界面质量对磁性（饱和磁化、死层、各向异性）的影响，为硅兼容自旋电子学（spintronics）应用提供调控依据。

Method: 采用SQUID-VSM磁强计进行静态磁性测量，并结合角度依赖的铁磁共振（FMR）测量，对溅射制备的[Si(40 Å)/Ni(t_Ni Å)]₂₀多层膜开展室温表征与定量各向异性分析。

Result: 较厚Ni层表现为软铁磁性，较薄样品呈铁磁与超顺磁（superparamagnetic）混合贡献；饱和磁化随厚度增加趋近块体Ni值，界面死层厚度约9 Å。FMR显示易面各向异性及面外附加非均匀自旋波模，有效磁化低于静态饱和磁化，表明存在部分补偿退磁场的垂直各向异性；定量得到K_S=+0.17 erg cm⁻²（利于垂直磁化）与K_V=-1.23×10⁶ erg cm⁻³（利于面内），交叉厚度t_c≈27 Å。

Conclusion: 结果表明界面各向异性与体各向异性相互竞争，Ni厚度与界面质量是裁剪Si/Ni多层膜磁性的关键因素；随Ni厚度减小FMR线型不对称性增强，与界面粗糙度增加一致，对硅兼容自旋电子器件设计具有意义。

Abstract: The magnetic anisotropy and magnetization dynamics of sputtered [Si(40~Å)/Ni($t_{\mathrm{Ni}}$~Å)]$_{20}$ multilayers were systematically investigated at room temperature using SQUID--VSM magnetometry and angle-dependent ferromagnetic resonance (FMR). Static measurements reveal soft ferromagnetic behaviour in thicker Ni layers and mixed ferromagnetic and superparamagnetic contributions at reduced thicknesses. The saturation magnetization increases toward the bulk Ni value with increasing thickness, consistent with Ni nanocrystallite growth. Magnetic dead-layer analysis yields an interfacial dead-layer thickness of approximately $9$~Å per interface, accounting for the suppressed magnetization in thinner Ni layers. Angle-dependent FMR measurements reveal effective easy-plane magnetic anisotropy, while out-of-plane spectra exhibit additional non-uniform spin-wave modes. The effective magnetization remains lower than the static saturation magnetization, indicating a perpendicular anisotropy contribution that partially compensates the demagnetizing field. Quantitative anisotropy analysis yields an interfacial anisotropy constant of $K_S = +0.17~\mathrm{erg\,cm^{-2}}$, favouring perpendicular magnetization, and a volume anisotropy constant of $K_V = -1.23 \times 10^6~\mathrm{erg\,cm^{-3}}$, favouring in-plane alignment, with an extrapolated anisotropy crossover thickness of $t_c \approx 27$~Å. Increasing FMR line-shape asymmetry with decreasing Ni thickness is consistent with enhanced interfacial roughness. These findings highlight the competing interfacial and volume anisotropies and the importance of Ni thickness and interface quality in tailoring Si/Ni multilayers for silicon-compatible spintronic applications.

</details>


### [66] [Nano-ARPES observation of a flat band at the \bar{M} point in 2.4° twisted bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32714)
*Giovanna Feraco,Przemysław Przybysz,Oreste De Luca,Ying Wang,Pavel Dudin,José Avila,Jianting Ye,Paweł Dąbrowski,Paweł J. Kowalczyk,Jagoda Sławińska,Petra Rudolf,Antonija Grubišić-Čabo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究报告在2.4°扭转双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene）中，于远离费米能级（Fermi level）的\bar{M}点意外观测到平带（flat band），为首次在\bar{K}点之外观察到平带，强调需探测整个布里渊区（Brillouin zone）以完整刻画扭转结构中的电子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔角（magic angle）处平带引发的超导与莫特绝缘态使扭转双层石墨烯成为研究多体相与关联效应的平台，但扭转角（twist angle）通常难以调控，因其易发生弛豫与重构等结构不稳定性，从而削弱关联效应，促使作者探索其他角度下的平带行为。

Method: 采用角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy）对2.4°扭转双层石墨烯进行直接测量，观测其能带结构。

Result: 在2.4°扭转双层石墨烯中，于远离费米能级的\bar{M}点出现未被预期的平带，该平带被角分辨光电子能谱直接可视化；此为扭转双层石墨烯中首次在\bar{K}点之外观测到平带。

Conclusion: 结果表明平带可出现在非魔角、非\bar{K}点的位置，需对整个布里渊区进行探测才能完整捕捉扭转结构中的电子态。

Abstract: The emergence of flat bands in twisted bilayer graphene at the so-called magic angle reveals a range of intriguing phases, such as superconductivity and Mott insulating states, resulting from electrons entering a correlated state. This establishes twisted bilayer graphene as an excellent platform for exploring correlation effects in many-body phases and across various twist angles. However, in most cases, the twist angle is not an easy parameter to control because it exhibits structural instabilities such as relaxation and reconstruction that reduce the correlation effects. Here, we report the unexpected appearance of a flat band at the \bar{M} point far from the Fermi level in a 2.4° twisted bilayer graphene, directly visualized by angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy. Our work is the first observation of flat bands away from the \bar{K} point in twisted bilayer graphene and highlights the need to probe the entire Brillouin zone to fully capture electronic states in twisted structures.

</details>


### [67] [First- and Second-Order Electron Position Correlators Reveal Topological Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32743)
*Antimo Marrazzo,Christian Loer Llemit,Massimo Capone*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出以电子位置算符涨落的空间与时间两点关联函数来刻画拓扑相变（topological phase transitions），其反对称部分在等时等空间极限下给出陈数（Chern number），并在平移不变（空间或时间）条件下呈现类似临界现象（critical phenomena）的发散关联长度与关联时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临界现象理论（theory of conventional critical phenomena）建立在局域可观测量的涨落与关联子（correlators）之上，而拓扑相变通常不以此类框架描述；本文旨在建立一种基于关联子的拓扑相变普适描述。

Method: 核心对象为位置—位置关联函数（position-position correlation function）的反对称部分，其在等时等空间极限下给出陈数；在空间或时间平移不变（后者对应平衡条件）时，两点关联子退化为单变量函数。对非周期系统或真实动力学过程，则构造由位置关联子重合点极限所得局域或动力学标记构成的高阶陈—陈关联子（Chern-Chern correlators），其可视为二阶位置关联子的双重列维-奇维塔缩并（double Levi-Civita contractions），逐对指标选取反对称扇区。作者以数值模拟与解析计算加以验证。

Result: 在空间或时间平移不变条件下，退化后的单变量关联函数呈现临界行为特征，包括标志相变的发散的关联时间与关联长度（divergent correlation times and lengths）；在非周期系统与真实动力学过程中，临界性由高阶陈—陈关联子更好地刻画。结论经数值模拟与解析计算双重支持。

Conclusion: 研究确立了基于关联子的拓扑相变一般性描述框架，可适用于晶态、非晶态、平衡态与动力学系统。

Abstract: The theory of conventional critical phenomena is built around the concepts of fluctuations and correlators of local observables. Here, we show that topological phase transitions can also be characterized through spatial and temporal two-point correlation functions associated with fluctuations of the electronic position operator. The key object is the antisymmetric part of the position-position correlation function, which yields the Chern number in the equal-time and equal-space limit. When the system is translationally invariant in space or time (the latter case corresponding to an equilibrium condition), these two-point correlators reduce to one-variable functions of the remaining variable. Remarkably, these functions are both reminiscent of critical behaviour, including divergent correlation times and lengths signalling the phase transitions. Away from these conditions, namely in non-periodic systems or genuinely dynamical processes, criticality is instead better captured by higher-order Chern-Chern correlators constructed from local or dynamical markers obtained as coincident-point limits of the position correlators. These higher-order Chern-Chern correlators can be seen as double Levi-Civita contractions of second-order position correlators, selecting the antisymmetric sector in each index pair. We substantiate this framework through numerical simulations and analytic calculations. Our results establish a general correlator-based description of topological phase transitions, applicable to crystalline, noncrystalline, equilibrium, and dynamical systems.

</details>


### [68] [Spontaneous Decay of High Energy Composite Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32763)
*Mytraya Gattu,J. K. Jain*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在分数量子霍尔流体（fractional quantum Hall fluid）中，注入电子会分裂为带分数电荷的复合费米子准粒子；本文进一步指出，携带分数电荷的高能复合费米子（composite fermion）会衰变为一组粒子，其中包含准粒子、准空穴乃至完整电子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有认识是电子进入分数量子霍尔流体后分裂为分数电荷的复合费米子准粒子，但高能复合费米子自身的归宿（是否稳定、如何衰变）尚待阐明，本文以此为出发点。

Method: 摘要未给出具体推导或数值手段，仅以理论论证的方式考察高能复合费米子的衰变通道，属于概念性/理论分析。

Result: 高能复合费米子携带分数电荷时并不稳定，会衰变为多种粒子的集合，产物包括准粒子（quasiparticles）、准空穴（quasiholes），甚至完整电子（whole electrons）。

Conclusion: 复合费米子准粒子的分数电荷并非其高能态的不变量，高能激发可将其“重组”为包括整电荷电子在内的多粒子终态，提示分数量子霍尔体系中存在更丰富的激发衰变谱系。

Abstract: An electron, when added to a fractional quantum Hall fluid, famously splits into fractionally charged composite-fermion quasiparticles. We show here that a high energy composite fermion carrying a fractional charge decays into a collection of particles, including quasiparticles, quasiholes, and even whole electrons.

</details>


### [69] [Floquet Engineering of Momentum Gaps in Magnonic Time Crystals: Slow-Drive Amplification and In-Gap Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32766)
*Paweł Gruszecki,Victor Pacheco-Peña*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出磁性类比于光子时间晶体（photonic time crystals）的“磁振子时间晶体”：在周期性切换各向异性的薄膜中，动量带隙（momentum gaps）内磁振子模式指数放大，且由单一约1 GHz驱动实现多频段自旋波放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子时间晶体虽已展现动量带隙中的指数增长，但其磁振子（magnonic）对应物尚未确立；研究旨在将时间晶体概念拓展至磁振子体系并探究其带隙形成条件与放大机制。

Method: 构建周期性切换各向异性（periodically switched anisotropy）的薄膜模型，解析推导其Floquet能带结构，并通过微磁学模拟（micromagnetics）加以验证；进一步考察波形对高阶带隙的调控以及Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用（DMI）的影响。

Result: 带隙形成需要进动椭圆度对比（precession-ellipticity contrast）。波形可控制高阶带隙：单个1 GHz切换驱动可放大2.5至4.5 GHz的自旋波频梳带，且在边缘平滑化后仍高于Gilbert损耗，这是正弦调制所不具备的。引入DMI时，被放大的波包在带隙内传播并同步增长。

Conclusion: 研究确立了磁振子时间晶体及其Floquet带结构，表明切换波形是调控带隙与放大频段的关键自由度，为基于时间周期驱动的自旋波放大与传播提供新机制。

Abstract: Photonic time crystals host momentum gaps in which waves grow exponentially but cannot propagate. We establish their magnonic counterpart in a thin film with periodically switched anisotropy, deriving its Floquet band structure and confirming it micromagnetically. Gaps require a precession-ellipticity contrast. The waveform controls high-order gaps: a single 1~GHz switching drive amplifies a comb of spin-wave bands, from 2.5 to 4.5~GHz, above Gilbert losses even with smoothed edges, unlike sinusoidal modulation. With the Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interaction, amplified packets propagate inside the gap while growing.

</details>


### [70] [Magnons and excitons from Bethe-Salpeter equation and its application to graphene-based magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32847)
*Simran Chourasia,Maurício F. C. Martins Quintela,J. J. Palacios*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出在同一Bethe-Salpeter形式框架下统一计算激子（excitons）与磁振子（magnons），并通过完整求解Bethe-Salpeter方程（超出Tamm-Dancoff近似）保证磁振子的无能隙Goldstone模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维半导体磁性材料的分离促使同时研究自旋翻转集体激发（磁振子）与自旋守恒电子-空穴束缚对（激子）。现有基于Bethe-Salpeter与Hedin方程交织的多体形式虽适用于激子，但因常用近似产生虚假的有隙Goldstone模式，而对磁振子不适用。

Method: 在模型哈密顿量中完整求解Bethe-Salpeter方程，突破Tamm-Dancoff近似，使激子与磁振子在同一形式体系下计算；并以扩展Hubbard模型描述的铁磁与反铁磁石墨烯为示例进行应用。

Result: 激子与磁振子可在同一Bethe-Salpeter框架下一致地计算，且磁振子的无能隙Goldstone模式得到保留；结果阐明了在位相互作用与非局域相互作用在激子与磁振子出现中的作用，并识别出将该方法推广至第一性原理计算所需的一致性要求。

Conclusion: 完整（超越Tamm-Dancoff近似的）Bethe-Salpeter形式可在尊重Goldstone定理的前提下统一描述激子与磁振子，为向第一性原理计算的推广提供了明确的一致性条件。

Abstract: With the recent isolation of two-dimensional semiconducting magnetic materials, the theoretical study of spin-flip collective excitations (magnons) along with spin-conserving electron-hole bound pairs (excitons) has become particularly relevant. Describing both types of quasiparticles within the same theoretical framework is possible, in principle, but the well-established many-body formalism for the calculation of excitons, based on the intertwined Bethe-Salpeter and Hedin's equations, is not satisfactory for magnons, since the usual approximations produce a spurious gapped Goldstone mode. Here, we show that excitons and magnons can be computed on the same footing under the same Bethe-Salpeter formalism, while respecting the gapless Goldstone mode for magnons. We demonstrate how this can be achieved through the evaluation of the full Bethe-Salpeter equation, beyond the Tamm-Dancoff approximation in model Hamiltonians. As an example, we apply it to ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic graphene, faithfully represented by the extended Hubbard model. Our results clarify the roles of on-site and non-local interactions in exciton and magnon appearance and identify consistency requirements relevant to extending the approach to first-principles calculations.

</details>


### [71] [Configuration-Independent Low-Energy Gaps in Graphene Nanoribbons: A $δ$-Scatterer Transfer-Matrix Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32901)
*C. I. Ávila-Joachín,J. C. Sandoval-Santana,Pedro Pereyra*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文用基于有限周期系统理论的转移矩阵方法，把石墨烯纳米带中每个碳原子视为一个δ散射体，推导扶手椅型与锯齿型边缘的透射谱，发现低能输运带隙随散射强度线性增长（Δ=0.364265 γ_δ）且与边缘取向无关，从而解释实验中带隙主要依赖宽度而非晶向的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光刻图案化石墨烯纳米带（graphene nanoribbons）的输运测量显示，带隙主要取决于纳米带宽度而非晶体的边缘取向，这与理想紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）的预测相矛盾，本文旨在解释这一差异。

Method: 建立基于有限周期系统（finite periodic systems）理论的转移矩阵（transfer-matrix）方法，将蜂窝晶格（honeycomb lattice）中每个碳原子表示为相互作用强度为γ_δ的δ散射体，把纳米带视为受限多通道有限周期系统；推导扶手椅型（arm-chair）与锯齿型（zig-zag）边缘的单胞转移矩阵，并以闭式给出透射系数与通道耦合系数。

Result: 透射谱呈现有限周期系统特有的共振能带结构，每个允带内的共振数目随单胞数增加。γ_δ=0时纳米带完全透明；γ_δ>0时出现低能输运带隙并随散射强度线性增长，Δ=0.364265 γ_δ，扶手椅型与锯齿型完全一致。通道间耦合在扶手椅型带中严格为零，在锯齿型带中随宽度增加而减小。

Conclusion: 由δ势（δ-potential）形式描述的原子尺度散射为所研究的本征纳米带提供了一个与取向无关的共同低能输运带隙贡献，这与实验报道的带隙无系统取向依赖相一致；边缘几何通过通道耦合行为体现其作用。

Abstract: Transport measurements on lithographically patterned graphene nanoribbons suggest that the gap depends primarily on ribbon width rather than on crystallographic edge orientation, in contrast to predictions of ideal tight-binding models. To address this, we develop a transfer-matrix approach, based on the theory of finite periodic systems, in which each carbon atom of the honeycomb lattice is represented by an individual $δ$-scatterer with interaction strength $γ^{}_δ$ and the nanoribbon is treated as a confined multichannel finite periodic system. We derive the unit-cell transfer matrix for both arm-chair and zig-zag edge configurations and obtain the transmission and channel-coupling coefficients in closed form. The transmission spectra display the resonant band structure characteristic of finite periodic systems, with the number of resonances per allowed band increasing with the number of unit cells. For $γ_δ=0$ the ribbons are fully transparent; for $γ_δ>0$ a low-energy transport gap opens and grows linearly with the scatterer strength, $Δ=0.364265\,γ_δ$, identically for arm-chair and zig-zag edges. These results are consistent with the reported absence of a systematic dependence on orientation, while the analytical results show that interchannel coupling vanishes exactly for arm-chair ribbons and decreases with increasing width for zig-zag ribbons, revealing the role of edge geometry. Together, these findings indicate that atomic-scale scattering, represented by the $δ$-potential formalism, provides a common orientation-independent contribution to the low-energy transport gap in the pristine ribbons considered here.

</details>


### [72] [Robustness of Skyrmion-Mediated Magnetization Switching in Perpendicular Magnetic Tunnel Junctions (p-MTJs) to Material Disorder and Elevated Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32944)
*Prosenjit Das,Md Mahadi Rajib,Jayasimha Atulasimha*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过随机微磁学模拟（stochastic micromagnetic simulations）研究100 nm垂直磁隧道结（perpendicular magnetic tunnel junction）中瞬态斯格明子中介的VCMA翻转可靠性，区分了脉冲失谐导致的可恢复可靠性与强易轴无序造成的不可恢复退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瞬态斯格明子（transient-skyrmion）介导的电压控制磁各向异性（VCMA）翻转虽快速节能，但其在实际材料无序（material disorder）与热涨落（thermal fluctuations）下的可靠性尚不明确。

Method: 采用随机微磁学模拟，对100 nm垂直磁隧道结在晶粒尺度（grain-scale）的Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用、垂直磁各向异性、易轴取向与晶粒间交换耦合变化下进行翻转可靠性研究，并在300 K与350 K下进行脉冲宽度优化与10^4次随机模拟统计。

Result: 中度无序下翻转仍稳健，脉冲优化后在300 K和350 K均获得100%翻转；温度由300 K升至350 K使最优VCMA脉冲宽度从约475 ps移至约450 ps，固定脉宽下的概率下降主要源于脉冲失谐（pulse detuning）；强易轴无序即使优化脉宽仍降低最大翻转概率，且瞬态斯格明子仍形成但脉冲后动力学未能完成磁化反转；无无序室温下正确脉冲整形时10^6次模拟均为100%翻转。

Conclusion: 研究确立了因时序偏移导致的可恢复可靠性与强无序引起的永久性退化之间的区别，后者无法通过脉冲重调（pulse re-tuning）补偿。

Abstract: Transient-skyrmion mediated voltage-controlled magnetic anisotropy (VCMA) switching is a fast and energy efficient way to magnetization reversal, but its reliability under realistic material disorder and thermal fluctuations remain unclear. In this work, we use stochastic micromagnetic simulations of a 100 nm perpendicular magnetic tunnel junction to study switching reliability under grain-scale variations in Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction, perpendicular magnetic anisotropy, easy-axis orientation, and intergrain exchange coupling. We find that switching remains robust under moderate disorder, with 100% switching observed in 10^4 stochastic simulations after pulse width optimization at both 300 K and 350 K. Increasing the temperature from 300 to 350 K shifts the optimum VCMA pulse width from ~475 to ~450 ps, indicating that an apparent reduction in switching probability at a fixed pulse duration can be primarily due to pulse detuning. In contrast, strong easy-axis disorder reduces the maximum switching probability even after pulse-width optimization, indicating degradation of the switching dynamics. We further show that, under strong easy-axis disorder a transient skyrmion still forms while the subsequent post-pulse dynamics fails to complete the magnetization reversal. Our study therefore establishes the distinction between recoverable reliability due to timing shifts and permanent disorder-induced degradation that simply cannot be recovered by pulse re-tuning. Finally, for no disorders but at room temperature, 100% switching is observed with 10^6 stochastic simulations with correct pulse shaping.

</details>

### [73] [Cartographing Gravity: Operational Maps, the Conatus of Proper Time, and the Potentia of Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32569)
*Jaume de Haro,Emilio Elizalde*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 论文从操作性与动力学原理出发重构广义相对论（General Relativity），将时空几何视为最终涌现的数学综合，而非出发点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沿用古典自然哲学（natural philosophy）方法论：先确立支配自由物体运动的自然律，再构建数学形式。本文反对将时空几何作为广义相对论的先验起点，转而从固有时、惯性（inertia）与自由落体等操作性与动力学概念出发进行重构。

Method: 将大质量自由粒子的“conatus”界定为沿固有时（proper time）驻值世界线运动的自然倾向（局部条件下为固有时极大），引力则解释为物质改变局部固有时速率的“potentia”。要求伽利略自由落体（Galilean free fall）严格成立以确定弱场静态不变量；再通过洛伦兹变换（Lorentz transformation）与推迟贡献的因果叠加得到运动源的引力场。

Result: 所得线性化场方程与谐和规范（harmonic gauge）下的广义相对论场方程一致。进一步施加能量-动量守恒（energy-momentum conservation）与二阶场方程要求，借助 Lovelock 定理（Lovelock's theorem），在所述假设类中唯一得到爱因斯坦场方程（Einstein's field equations）所代表的协变完备化。

Conclusion: 该构造表明几何无需预先假定，而是基于固有时、惯性与自由落体的操作性动力学之最终数学综合而涌现。

Abstract: Classical natural philosophy sought the natural law governing the motion of free bodies long before daring to construct the appropriate mathematical formalism. Following this longstanding methodological perspective, in the present work General Relativity is reconstructed from operational and dynamical principles, rather than taking spacetime geometry as its starting point. The \textit{conatus} of a massive free particle is identified with its natural tendency to follow a worldline of stationary proper time, which, under the appropriate local conditions, corresponds to a maximum of its proper time, whereas gravitation is interpreted as the \textit{potentia} of matter to modify the local rate of proper time. By requiring the exact validity of Galilean free fall, the weak-field static invariant is determined. The gravitational field of moving sources is subsequently obtained by Lorentz-transforming the elementary static solution and causally superposing the retarded contributions of infinitesimal source elements. The resulting linearized field equations coincide with those of General Relativity in the harmonic gauge. Finally, imposing general energy-momentum conservation together with the requirement of second-order field equations yields--- within the class of theories satisfying the stated assumptions and by virtue of Lovelock's theorem---the unique covariant completion represented by Einstein's field equations. Within this construction, geometry is not postulated from the outset; instead, it emerges as the final mathematical synthesis of operational dynamics based on proper time, inertia, and free fall.

</details>
