### [74] [Conviction versus Conformity in a Random-Field Ising Electorate: Antagonistic Ties, Network Density, and a Parameter-Free Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32021)
*Natalia Romero*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 在稀疏图上研究带 ±J 键无序（bond disorder）的零温随机场伊辛模型（random-field Ising model）作为选民模型：以精确基态（ground state）与无拟合参数的空腔理论（cavity theory）定位场能占比一半的交叉点（crossover）r_h*，并给出其标度律与复制对称破缺（replica-symmetry breaking）证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将社会耦合与调查导出的三峰场（trimodal fields）纳入统一统计物理框架，考察部分对抗性（antagonistic）键对选举行为的影响，并检验理论能否无参数地预测基态性质。

Method: 对 N 至 180 的实例计算精确基态（约 78000 次优化，最密挫败情形未完全认证），启发式方法推广至 N=720；在图上系综上求解零温空腔理论（cavity theory），不引入拟合参数，并在未参与建模的七个密度上预先预测后再计算精确基态加以检验。

Result: 21 个格子中 19 个的 r_h* 预测误差在 10% 以内（中位误差 3%）；误差在 alpha=0.05 与 0.5 时无系统性符号，在 alpha=0.2 时全为负，且该处七个格子中六个的复制对称解（replica-symmetric solution）不稳定。经验上 r_h*≈0.91 e_J(0)，并满足 r_h* ~ <k>^gamma，alpha=0.05 时 gamma=1.01–1.09，alpha≥0.5 时 gamma=0.50–0.57；中间挫败度下不呈幂律，约在 <k>~10 处失效。弱挫败稠密网络中基态在 93–100% 实现里跟随总场符号。

Conclusion: 精确基态与无参数空腔理论相互印证，表明已知 ±J 能量定律可迁移至该选民模型；中间挫败区出现复制对称破缺迹象，且稀疏网络以牺牲合作键来容纳对抗键的机制在 <k>~10 附近停止生效。

Abstract: We study the zero-temperature random-field Ising model with +-J bond disorder on sparse graphs as a model of an electorate: survey-derived, trimodal fields compete with social couplings, a fraction alpha of which are antagonistic. With exact ground states up to N=180 (over 78000 optimizations, certified except in the densest frustrated cases) and validated heuristics to N=720, we locate the crossover r_h* at which the field carries half of the ground-state energy, close to where half the voters held against their own field have switched (0.93 r_h* for alpha<=0.2). Our main test is a zero-temperature cavity theory solved on the graph ensemble with no fitted parameter: at seven densities not otherwise used, predicted before their exact ground states were computed, it placed r_h* within 10% in 19 of 21 cells (median error 3%). Its errors are unsigned at alpha=0.05 and 0.5 but all negative at alpha=0.2, where it predicts the coupling energy correctly but a crossover that is early and, at high density, too sharp, and where its replica-symmetric solution is unstable in six of seven cells, consistent with replica-symmetry breaking at intermediate frustration. Empirically r_h*~0.91 e_J(0), with e_J(0) the coupling energy of the zero-field ground state, inside a rigorous per-instance factor-of-two window, so the known +-J energy laws carry over: r_h* ~ <k>^gamma with gamma=1.01-1.09 for alpha=0.05 and 0.50-0.57 for alpha>=0.5 (<k>=3-28; five graph families agree at matched density). At intermediate frustration r_h* is not a power law: sparse networks accommodate antagonistic ties at the cost of cooperative ones, which stops paying near <k>~10; the theory reproduces this without adjustment. In weakly frustrated dense networks the ground state is a near-consensus following the sign of the aggregate field in 93-100% of realisations at small r_h, against near-chance agreement in frustrated ones.

</details>

### [75] [Newtonian I-Love-Q relations: An enthalpy-based approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31730)
*Athul Kunjipurayil,J. Piekarewicz*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文以基于焓（enthalpy）的恒星结构表述，在牛顿引力（Newtonian gravity）框架下教学性地推导 I-Love-Q 关系，并证明无量纲可观测量满足精确恒等式 $\bar I^{\,2}\bar Q/\bar\lambda\equiv 1$，独立于物态方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I-Love-Q 关系通常在中子星（neutron star）相对论背景下讨论，缺乏清晰的牛顿引力教学式推导；本文旨在提供统一、简明的形式化框架以阐明其物理来源。

Method: 引入比焓（specific enthalpy）将欧拉方程（Euler's equation）化为流体静力平衡（hydrostatic equilibrium）的第一积分，并把物态方程（equation of state）改写为质量密度与焓的关系；结合泊松方程（Poisson's equation）统一描述球对称平衡及转动与潮汐（tides）引起的形变，并以多方物态方程（polytropic equations of state）与相对论自由费米气体（relativistic free Fermi gas）作示例。

Result: 在牛顿引力下，缓慢均匀转动（slow uniform rotation）与弱潮汐场（weak tidal field）激发相同的线性四极响应（linear quadrupolar response），从而给出无量纲（“bar”）可观测量的精确恒等式 $\bar I^{\,2}\bar Q/\bar\lambda\equiv1$，与物态方程无关。

Conclusion: 该精确恒等式应与 I-Love-Q 两两关系（pairwise I-Love-Q relations）的近似普适性（approximate universality）相区分；后者的残余恒星结构依赖性由本文所考察的模型予以示例说明。

Abstract: We present a pedagogical introduction to the I-Love-Q relations within Newtonian gravity using an enthalpy-based formulation of stellar structure. Introducing the specific enthalpy converts Euler's equation into a first integral of hydrostatic equilibrium and recasts the equation of state as a relation between the mass density and the enthalpy. Together with Poisson's equation, these relations provide a unified description of spherical equilibrium and deformations induced by rotation and tides. We illustrate the formalism with polytropic equations of state and a relativistic free Fermi gas. In Newtonian gravity, slow uniform rotation and a weak tidal field excite the same linear quadrupolar response, yielding the exact identity $\bar I^{\,2}\bar Q/\barλ\equiv1$ for the dimensionless (``bar'') observables---independently of the equation of state. This exact identity is distinguished from the approximate universality of the pairwise I-Love-Q relations, whose residual dependence on stellar structure is illustrated by the models considered in this work.

</details>


### [76] [Estimation Of The Mass And The Spin Of The Neutron Star 4U 1728-34 Using Triples Of X-Ray Quasi-Periodic Oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32371)
*Radostina P. Tasheva,Ivan Z. Stefanov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对环状源4U 1728-34的19次观测中报告的X射线准周期振荡（QPOs），利用三组三重态QPO的频率方程反演中子星质量、比角动量及轨道半径，并给出区间估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线准周期振荡（quasi-periodic oscillations, QPOs）可用于约束中心中子星（neutron star）的宏观参数，但单一频率难以独立确定质量与自旋，故需借助三重态QPO的联合约束。

Method: 对三个观测频率各采用独立的模型（公式），组成含三个代数方程的系统，求解三个未知量——中子星质量、比角动量（specific angular momentum）与三重态QPO起源轨道半径；并通过隐函数微分（implicit function differentiation）应用误差传播公式得到参数的区间估计，且对kHz QPO采用多种模型重复计算。

Result: 在平均后可分辨的前提下，假定了三重态QPO的同时性，从而获得质量、比角动量与轨道半径的数值解，并借助误差传播给出各参数的区间估计；结果对所选的kHz QPO模型具有依赖性。

Conclusion: 该代数方程组方法可在多模型框架下由三重态QPO频率反演中子星基本参数及其不确定度，但其有效性依赖于QPO同时性假设与所选频率模型的正确性。

Abstract: X-ray quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) were reported for 19 different observations of the atoll source 4U 1728- 34. Three of them reveal triplets of QPOs: a lower and a upper kHz QPOs and a low-frequency QPO. Even though they are discernible only after averaging we assume them to be simultaneous. We apply a separate model (formula) for each of the three frequencies of the observed QPOs and compose a system which contains three algebraic equations for the three unknown parameters - the mass and the specific angular momentum of the central neutron star, and the radius of the orbit of orgin of the given triplet of QPOs, which allows us to find the numerical values of the later. Error propagation formulas are applied for the obtaining of interval estimates of the parameters of the star. This is done through implicit function differentiation. The calculations are repeated with several different models for the kHz QPOs.

</details>


### [77] [Tracking the Evolution of Supermassive Black Hole Binaries with Pulsar Terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32736)
*Bence Bécsy,Magdalena Siwek*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种费雪矩阵（Fisher matrix）方法，用于量化脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing array, PTA）通过脉冲星项（pulsar terms）追踪超大质量黑洞双星（supermassive black hole binary）频率演化的精度，并评估环境效应（environmental effects）的可探测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTA即将能够分辨贡献于纳赫兹（nanohertz）频段随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational wave background）的最强超大质量黑洞双星，但数十年观测通常不足以直接观测其频率演化，需借助脉冲星项回溯数万年历史。

Method: 构建费雪矩阵方法计算频率随时间的可追踪精度，并将其应用于具有30年基线的现实近未来PTA；继而检验双星周围环双星盘（circumbinary disk）二维流体动力学（hydrodynamic）模拟所预言的环境效应。

Result: 在所考虑的圆轨道双星（circular binary）中，二维流体动力学模拟预言的环境效应难以被探测；但若忽略这些效应，某些情形下会导致啁啾质量（chirp mass）恢复出现偏差。该方法还可检验广义相对论（general relativity）预言的纯引力波驱动旋近（inspiral）轨道演化，并对幂律硬化模型（power-law hardening model）描述的额外一般环境效应给出上限。

Conclusion: 基于脉冲星项的长期频率演化追踪是一种有力工具，可用于检验引力波驱动演化、约束环境效应并评估参数估计偏差，但当前流体模拟预言的环境效应在近未来PTA中可探测性有限。

Abstract: Pulsar timing arrays will soon be able to resolve the loudest supermassive black hole binaries contributing to the stochastic gravitational wave background at nanohertz frequencies. While the few decades of observations are typically not enough to see these binaries evolve in frequency, the so-called pulsar terms offer a glimpse into the past and can help us map out the frequency evolution of these binaries over tens of thousands of years. Measuring this long-term frequency evolution can allow us to look for deviations from a vacuum binary evolution due to various environmental effects. We develop a Fisher-matrix approach to calculate how precisely the frequency can be tracked over time in this manner, and demonstrate its use on a realistic near-future pulsar timing array with a 30-year baseline. We find that the environmental effects predicted by 2-dimensional hydrodynamic simulations of circumbinary disks are unlikely to be detectable in the circular binaries we considered, however, in some cases they might result in biased chirp mass recovery if ignored. We also demonstrate how this approach allows one to test the orbital evolution predicted by general relativity in a purely gravitational-wave driven inspiral, and to put upper limits on additional generic environmental effects described by a power-law hardening model.

</details>

### [78] [Multi-scale patterns arising from competing chemotactic interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32591)
*Agniva Datta,Robert Großmann,Carsten Beta*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个质量守恒的趋化性（chemotaxis）模型，其中自诱导物（autoinducers）作为激活因子、营养物质（nutrients）作为抑制剂，二者竞争驱动图案形成，从而产生多种时空图案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典趋化模型仅考虑细胞对单一化学梯度的定向运动，而近期实验表明细菌等生物微泳体（microswimmers）可对多种化学物质趋化，且这些化学物质随微泳体分布动态演化，因此需要建立更贴近实验的多化学物质模型。

Method: 构建质量守恒（mass-conserving）的趋化模型，引入两种相互竞争的化学物质：自诱导物作为激活因子（activator）、营养物质作为粗化抑制剂（inhibitor for coarsening）；并通过解析方法推导各类图案出现的条件。

Result: 模型复现出多种图案，包括长波长无间断粗化（uninterrupted coarsening）、类图灵受抑粗化（Turing-like arrested coarsening）、行波（traveling waves），以及一类新型混合多尺度振荡粗化图案——其中致密聚集体与动态扩散的种群部分共存。

Conclusion: 该研究解析给出了上述图案的起始条件并讨论其物理意义，显著拓展了对信号活性物质（signaling active matter）及一般图案形成的理解。

Abstract: Classical chemotaxis models consider the directed motion of cells in response to a chemical gradient. Recent experiments suggest that biological microswimmers such as bacteria can perform chemotaxis toward multiple chemicals, which dynamically evolve depending on the distribution of the microswimmers. In this Letter, we consider a mass-conserving chemotaxis model in which pattern formation is driven by two competing chemicals, autoinducers and nutrients, where the former acts as an activator and the latter as an inhibitor for coarsening. We show that this can give rise to diverse patterns including long wavelength uninterrupted coarsening, Turing-like arrested coarsening, traveling waves as well as a novel class of mixed multi-scale oscillatory coarsening patterns, where a dense aggregate coexist with a dynamically spreading part of the population. We analytically derive the conditions for the onset of such patterns and discuss their physical implications, which significantly broadens our understanding of signaling active matter and pattern formation in general.

</details>

### [79] [Prospects of Detecting Cosmological Domain Walls in the Cosmic Microwave Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31761)
*Berkin Gürbüz,Robert Brandenberger,Yitian Sun*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文研究纯引力相互作用暗域壁（domain walls）在宇宙微波背景（CMB）上留下的印记，在低对称破缺能标 η 下利用曲线波/小波分解（curvelet/wavelet decomposition）与模拟推断（simulation-based inference）对 SPT 类巡天做灵敏度预报，给出 η∼10–100 MeV 的可探测能标范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑缺陷（topological defects）是自发破缺场论的普遍预言，域壁是最简单的二维缺陷；作者关注低对称破缺能标下暗域壁既可规避 Zel'dovich 超闭合界限（overclosure bound）又保持宇宙学相关性的参数区间，以检验其在 CMB 上的可探测性。

Method: 解析计算平面域壁相对论性排斥物质所形成的真空洞与过密壳层对光子的偏折场（deflection field），涵盖壁面正对与任意倾角情形；采用匹配壁几何的曲线波/小波分解刻画非高斯局域特征，并用模拟推断训练神经后验估计器（Neural Posterior Estimator, NPE）反演散射红移 z、能标 η 与倾角 θ，以及神经比值估计器（Neural Ratio Estimator, NRE）评估似然比检验统计量。

Result: 偏折在温度天图上留下相干、强非高斯畸变，具有由相对论性位移尺度决定的双峰结构，且倾角依赖在正对时消失而两点统计（two-point statistics）捕捉能力差；曲线波与小波阈值在可及红移范围内落在共同 η∼10–100 MeV 能带，神经比值灵敏度曲线在 z≳20 处将可探测 η 降低 1.3–1.9 倍（壁张力约低 2–7 倍），基本达到单分辨元噪声地板。

Conclusion: 暗域壁的 CMB 印记具有局域各向异性与非高斯性，需借助几何匹配的稀疏分解与基于全图形态的神经推断才能有效探测；在 SPT 类巡天下，相关方法可将灵敏度推进至噪声地板附近，为暗域壁限制设定提供预报。

Abstract: Topological defects are a generic prediction of field theories with spontaneously broken symmetries, and domain walls, the two-dimensional defects left by a broken discrete symmetry, are among the simplest. We study the imprint of dark, purely gravitationally interacting domain walls on the Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB), in the regime where a low symmetry-breaking scale $η$ allows the walls to evade the Zel'dovich overclosure bound while remaining cosmologically relevant. The relativistic repulsion of matter by a planar wall carves out an evacuated void bounded by an overdense shell, and we compute the resulting photon deflection field analytically for walls both aligned with and arbitrarily inclined to the line of sight. The deflection stamps coherent, strongly non-Gaussian distortions onto the temperature sky, with a characteristic two-peak structure set by the relativistic displacement scale and an inclination dependence that vanishes for face-on walls. Because these signatures are localised and anisotropic, they are poorly captured by two-point statistics; we therefore analyse them with a curvelet/wavelet decomposition matched to the wall's geometry, and with simulation-based inference, training a Neural Posterior Estimator (NPE) to recover the scattering redshift $z$, energy scale $η$, and inclination $θ$, and a Neural Ratio Estimator (NRE) to evaluate the likelihood-ratio test statistic for limit-setting. Casting these methods as sensitivity forecasts for a South Pole Telescope-like (SPT) survey, we find that the curvelet and wavelet statistical thresholds populate a common $η\sim 10-100$ MeV band over the accessible redshift range. The neural-ratio sensitivity curve, which exploits the full map morphology, reaches a factor 1.3-1.9 lower in $η$ at $z \gtrsim 20$, or $\sim 2-7$ in the wall tension, landing essentially on the per-resolution-element noise floor.

</details>


### [80] [The Impact of Inclination, Redshift, and Mass Ratio Selection on Cosmological Constraints from Bright Sirens Beyond Conventional Fisher Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32008)
*Gustavo B. F. Lima,Rafael F. Aranha,Rodrigo S. Gonçalves*

Conclusion: 宇宙学参数约束对天体物理假设敏感并呈现明显的观测需求二分性：H₀精度主要受事件数驱动，而全局几何约束依赖深红移样本与更宽的倾角覆盖。

Abstract: Future generations of gravitational-wave observatories are projected to detect up to thousands bright siren events within a decade of operation. However, the precision of these cosmological forecasts depends heavily on the astrophysical assumptions embedded within the simulation pipelines. In this work, we systematically investigate the impact of variations in the binary mass ratio ($q$), the maximum observable inclination angle ($ι_{\max}$), and the maximum redshift ($z_{\max}$) on the constraints of the Hubble constant ($H_0$) and the matter density parameter ($Ω_m$). We perform our analyses based on the GWDALI framework, the GW Doublet Approximation, and the phenomenological waveform approximant IMRPhenomD. By adopting sets of non-spinning binary neutron star populations we explore the impact of astrophysical parameters on the constraints of cosmological parameters. Our results reveal a clear dichotomy in the observational requirements: while the statistical uncertainty on $H_0$ falls below the falls below approximately $1.3\%$ level for $N \ge 400$ events and drops under $0.9\%$ once $N \ge 800$, a deep-redshift catalog ($z_{\max} \ge 1.0$) is strictly required to effectively break parameter degeneracies and constrain the global geometry through $Ω_m$. Furthermore, expanding the maximum inclination cutoff from the conservative $20^{\circ}$ up to $40^{\circ}$ substantially mitigates the correlation between distance and inclination.

</details>


### [81] [Joint Angular-Power Distributions of Interfering Nanohertz Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32809)
*Yan-Chen Bi,Yu-Mei Wu,Qing-Guo Huang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文通过频率分辨的勒让德系数刻画有限数目超大质量双黑洞（supermassive black-hole binaries, SMBHB）群体所导致的引力波功率与脉冲星计时（pulsar timing）相关性的涨落，并给出统计基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限群体的超大质量双黑洞会同时引起引力波功率与脉冲星计时相关性的涨落，需要对该实现相关的角度相关结构进行统计刻画。

Method: 采用亮源与高斯微弱背景的混合模型（hybrid model），将多弱源极限下的条件伽马分布（conditional Gamma distribution）推广为非中心χ²统计；结合总体边缘化（population marginalization）与解析方差分解及SMBHB群体模拟进行分析。

Result: 揭示出依赖总体的转变：低频方差由干涉（interference）主导，高频方差由总体功率（population power）主导；在多弱源情形下，多元对数正态近似（multivariate lognormal approximation）仅用前两个对数累积量即可捕捉联合分布的主要特征。

Conclusion: 研究结果为实际相关性的整体幅度与角度结构涨落提供了统计基准（statistical benchmarks）。

Abstract: Finite populations of supermassive black-hole binaries produce fluctuations in both gravitational-wave power and pulsar timing correlations. We characterize the realization-dependent, orientation-averaged Earth-term angular correlation through its frequency-resolved Legendre coefficients. A hybrid model of bright sources and a Gaussian faint background extends the conditional Gamma distribution in the many-weak-source limit to non-central $χ^2$ statistics, yielding correlated multipoles after population marginalization. Our analytical variance decomposition and SMBHB population simulations reveal a population-dependent transition from interference dominated variance at low frequencies to population-power-dominated variance at high frequencies. In the many-weak-source regime, a multivariate lognormal approximation captures the main features of the joint distribution using only the first two log-cumulants. These results provide statistical benchmarks for fluctuations in both the overall amplitude and angular structure of the realized correlation.

</details>

### [82] [Lattice Symmetry Remnants in Spatial and Temporal Entanglement of Quantum Critical Spin Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31676)
*Xiao-Han Yang,Rui-Zhen Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: Quantum critical lattice models carry universal information beyond the bulk data of their infrared conformal field theory (CFT): microscopic lattice symmetries constrain how the CFT is realized. We probe these remnants through the spatial and temporal entanglement of two Euclidean-evolved states from the same critical model. In the finite-length scaling regime, a conformal boundary encodes the long-distance effect of the short-range initial state, and boundary CFT (BCFT) organizes the universal low-lying entanglement spectrum. We formulate the temporal state as a uniform infinite matrix product state evolved by a symmetry-preserving infinite matrix product operator, using a local factorization regular even at arbitrarily small Trotter steps. For the Ising and three-state Potts chains, whose relevant symmetries are onsite, the spatial and temporal constructions approach the same BCFT structure; varying the initial state engineers conformal boundaries distinguishable spectroscopically. The spin-1/2 XXZ chain instead realizes a Lieb--Schultz--Mattis (LSM) constraint between internal U(1) symmetry and one-site translation. A U(1)-preserving short-range spatial state accommodates it through a doubled unit cell and inequivalent entanglement cuts, whereas the temporal state remains uniform along Euclidean time. Successive spatial transfer steps pump the temporal entanglement spectrum between translation-related Dirichlet sectors, equivalently between integer and half-integer compact-boson zero modes. Breaking U(1) removes both the zero mode and the alternation. Continued evolution at fixed bond dimension crosses over from the finite-length BCFT regime to a finite-entanglement regime generated by symmetry-allowed relevant deformations. Thus microscopic symmetry and anomaly data remain visible in universal entanglement by constraining the boundary realization of the infrared CFT.

</details>


### [83] [Kinetic competition controls loss-induced exceptional points of Hubbard pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32123)
*Ho-Kin Tang,Xiaosen Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在超立方晶格（hypercubic lattice）中，双粒子连续谱（two-particle continuum）的动能结构决定了由两体损耗（two-body loss）诱导的束缚（binding）是否与例外点（exceptional point, EP）简并重合；阈值仅在某一方向动能占优时才成为精确的EP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两体损耗可在Hubbard晶格中束缚两个费米子，但无限晶格中的束缚阈值是否必然对应例外点此前并无定论，需要厘清束缚与EP简并之间的关系。

Method: 固定总动量（total momentum）$\mathbf{K}$，将两粒子跳跃的干涉转化为沿各晶格方向的相对运动跳跃（relative-motion hopping），据此确定相对运动动能；分析阈值 $g_c=1/[\pi\rho_2(0)]$ 处局域对格林函数（local pair Green function）的虚部性质与横向动量散射通道（transverse-momentum scattering channel）的传播行为，并结合相干完美吸收（coherent perfect absorption）对应的实能散射零点。

Result: 在超立方晶格上，仅当某一方向的最大动能超过各横向极大值之和时，阈值才为EP：此时横向通道在带中心附近均保持传播，局域对格林函数为纯虚，两个实能散射零点在阈值处合并为解析延拓后配对问题的二阶EP，损耗恢复散射解的宇称-时间对称性（parity-time symmetry）。若不存在占优方向，则发生束缚而无零点合并、无对称性破缺。

Conclusion: 总动量即使在各向同性晶格上也能选择特定的EP扇区；更一般地，双粒子连续谱的动能结构决定损耗诱导束缚与例外简并是否同时发生。

Abstract: Two-body loss can bind two fermions in a Hubbard lattice, yet the binding threshold in the infinite lattice need not be an exceptional point (EP). At fixed total momentum $\mathbf{K}$, the hops of the two particles interfere into a relative-motion hopping along each lattice direction, which sets the kinetic energy of the relative motion. On a hypercubic lattice, the threshold $g_c=1/[πρ_2(0)]$ is an EP precisely when the maximum kinetic energy along one direction exceeds the sum of the transverse maxima. This dominance keeps every transverse-momentum scattering channel propagating near the band center, making the local pair Green function purely imaginary there. Two real-energy scattering zeros associated with coherent perfect absorption then coalesce at the threshold into a second-order EP of the analytically continued pair problem, where loss restores the parity-time symmetry of the pair's scattering solutions. Without a dominant direction, binding occurs without coalescence and without breaking of that symmetry. Total momentum can therefore select EP sectors even on isotropic lattices. More broadly, the kinetic structure of the two-particle continuum determines whether loss-induced binding and exceptional degeneracy coincide.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散 WKB 方法尚未覆盖同时具有缓慢变化的跃迁振幅与格点势的一维二分（bipartite）紧束缚链，而此类非均匀 Rice-Mele 模型可在光学超晶格（optical superlattice）中实现，因此需要相应的半经典理论描述。

Method: 将离散 WKB 方法推广到非均匀 Rice-Mele 模型，推导单粒子波函数与态密度（density of states）的显式半经典表达式，以及任意费米能下的子晶格分辨局域粒子密度与填充率；并分析局域带边（local band edges）的空间依赖、相空间拓扑对 Bohr-Sommerfeld 量子化条件的作用。

Result: 所得 WKB 密度分布与局域密度近似（local-density approximation）结果一致；局域带边的空间变化决定全局谱结构，相空间拓扑（phase-space topology）决定 Bohr-Sommerfeld 量子化条件的形式；并以谐振阱中的均匀 Rice-Mele 链与含畴壁（domain wall）的完全非均匀链两个例子加以说明。

Conclusion: 离散 WKB 方法可为非均匀二分紧束缚链提供自洽的半经典框架，其预测与局域密度近似相符，并揭示局域带边与相空间拓扑对谱结构和量子化条件的共同支配作用。

Abstract: We extend the discrete WKB method to one-dimensional bipartite tight-binding chains of free fermions with slowly varying hopping amplitudes and on-site potentials. We focus on the inhomogeneous Rice-Mele model, suggesting possible experimental realizations thereof in optical superlattices. We derive explicit semiclassical expressions for the single-particle wave functions and density of states, as well as the sublattice-resolved local particle densities and filling fraction at arbitrary Fermi energy. In particular, we show that the WKB density profiles agree with those obtained from the local-density approximation. We analyze the spatial dependence of the local band edges and its consequences for the global spectral structure, and clarify the role played by the phase-space topology in determining the Bohr-Sommerfeld quantization conditions. Finally, we illustrate our results with two representative examples: a homogeneous Rice-Mele chain subject to a harmonic trap and a fully inhomogeneous chain containing a domain wall.

</details>


### [85] [Compass Correlations in a Square-Lattice Magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32157)
*Otkur Omar,Matthias D. Frontzek,Gabriele Sala,Junying Shen,Xinmin Wang,Ze Hu,Zhuang Xu,Zecong Qin,Xin Tong,Joe Paddison,Max Hirschberger,Shinichiro Seki,Masaaki Matsuda,Matthew B. Stone,Andrew D. Christianson,Nguyen D. Khanh,Shang Gao*

TL;DR: 该研究利用单晶中子漫散射（neutron diffuse scattering）在方形晶格金属间化合物 ErRu$_2$Si$_2$ 中识别出罗盘关联（compass correlations），并结合晶体场谱学与最小罗盘-海森堡模型（compass-Heisenberg model）定量解释数据，为方形晶格罗盘物理提供实验平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方形晶格罗盘模型（square-lattice compass model）是键依赖阻挫（bond-dependent frustration）的典范，但其特征性自旋关联长期缺乏实验证据，本文旨在寻找并确认该类关联。

Result: NdRu$_2$Si$_2$ 呈近均匀漫散射环，而 ErRu$_2$Si$_2$ 在布里渊区（Brillouin zones）间表现出强烈强度调制；该调制揭示出将磁矩取向与关联波矢耦合的罗盘关联，模型定量再现漫散射并解释双-$Q$ 涡旋-反涡旋（double-$Q$ vortex-antivortex）基态。

Conclusion: 将罗盘耦合固定为由偶极能量尺度（dipolar energy scale）设定的值时拟合仍与数据吻合，支持键依赖相互作用存在可观的偶极贡献；结果表明偶极相互作用与局域晶体场各向异性可协同传导电子介导交换（conduction-electron-mediated exchange）塑造方向性磁关联，并确立方形晶格罗盘物理平台。

Abstract: The square-lattice compass model is a paradigm of bond-dependent frustration, yet experimental evidence for its characteristic spin correlations has remained elusive. Here we identify compass correlations in the square-lattice intermetallic ErRu$_2$Si$_2$ using single-crystal neutron diffuse scattering. Comparative measurements on the isostructural compounds NdRu$_2$Si$_2$ and ErRu$_2$Si$_2$ reveal a pronounced contrast between nearly uniform diffuse rings in the former and strong intensity modulation between Brillouin zones in the latter. Through the magnetic neutron polarization factor, this modulation reveals compass correlations that couple moment orientation to the correlation wavevector. Crystal-field spectroscopy identifies four preferred in-plane orientations for the Er$^{3+}$ moments. A minimal compass-Heisenberg model incorporating this anisotropy quantitatively reproduces the diffuse scattering and accounts for the double-$Q$ vortex-antivortex ground state. Fits with the compass couplings fixed at values set by the dipolar energy scale retain good agreement with the data, supporting a substantial dipolar contribution to the bond-dependent interactions. Our results establish a square-lattice platform for compass physics and suggest that dipolar interactions, acting together with local crystal-field anisotropy, can shape directional magnetic correlations alongside conduction-electron-mediated exchange.

</details>


### [86] [Intrinsic Paramagnetism of Optical Phonons and Thermal Hall Effect in Quantum Paraelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32224)
*Harsh Jain,Manideep Gone,Nabyendu Das*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出量子顺电体（quantum paraelectrics）中声子介导热霍尔效应（thermal Hall effect）的微观理论，认为其源于近临界光学声子模式的量子涨落所导致的固有顺磁性；通过手性声子（chiral phonons）格林函数与Kubo形式导出κ_xy，并预言磁场线性响应、温度非单调性与量子临界点附近的幂律标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有热霍尔输运理解多依赖外禀机制或磁序，而量子顺电体缺乏长程磁序却表现出相关输运行为，亟需从微观层面解释其固有顺磁性来源。该文旨在为SrTiO₃等材料中观测到的热霍尔行为提供统一的理论基础。

Method: 基于近临界光学声子模式的时间涨落偶极矩产生有效磁化，并与外磁场耦合，在极化场有效拉格朗日密度中引入磁电项 H·(P×Ṗ)，使横向光学声子谱劈裂为左右圆偏振手性支。随后利用手性声子格林函数（Green's functions）与Kubo形式，由热流关联函数计算热霍尔电导率。

Result: 推导得 κ_xy 正比于两个手性模式谱权重之差（A₊²−A₋²），建立声子手性与横向热输运的直接微观联系。理论预言三点：低场下 κ_xy∝H 的线性响应并在高场转为非线性；温度依赖呈非单调并在热占据与声子散射竞争下出现峰值；量子临界点 ω₀→0 附近 κ_xy 显著增强并呈幂律标度 κ_xy∝T^α，α≈0.41。

Conclusion: 该工作从固有顺磁性角度阐明了量子顺电体中的热霍尔输运机制，预言与SrTiO₃及相关材料的实验观测相符，为手性声子与横向热输运的关联提供了微观理论依据。

Abstract: We propose a microscopic theory of the phonon-mediated thermal Hall effect in quantum paraelectrics, driven by the intrinsic paramagnetism due to quantum fluctuations of the (nearly) critical optical phonon modes. The effective magnetization due to temporal fluctuations of the assosciated dipoles couple to an external magnetic field resulting the magnetoelectric term $\mathbf{H} \cdot (\mathbf{P} \times \dot{\mathbf{P}})$ in the effective Lagrangian density of the polarisation field $\mathbf{P}$. This coupling splits the transverse optical phonon spectrum into right- and left-circularly polarized chiral branches. Using the chiral phonon Green's functions and the Kubo formalism, we derive the thermal Hall conductivity $κ_{xy}$ from the heat-current correlation function. We observe that $κ_{xy}$ is proportional to the difference in spectral weights of the two chiral modes, $κ_{xy} \propto A_+^2 - A_-^2$, establishing a direct microscopic link between phonon chirality and transverse heat transport. Our theory predicts three key observations: (i) a linear-in-magnetic-field response $κ_{xy} \propto H$ at low fields, evolving into nonlinear behavior at higher fields (ii) a non-monotonic temperature dependence of $κ_{xy}$ with a peak arising from the competition between thermal population and phonon scattering and (iii) strong enhancement of $κ_{xy}$ near the quantum critical point $ω_0 \to 0$, it exhibits power-law scaling $κ_{xy} \propto T^α$ with $α\approx 0.41$. Our findings provide an understanding of thermal Hall transport in quantum paraelectrics due to intrinsic paramagnetism and is aligned with the observed behaviour in SrTiO$_3$ and related materials.

</details>


### [87] [Triplon dynamics from partial Kondo screening in frustrated heavy-fermion systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32283)
*Hiroki Nakahara,Masahito Mochizuki,Rintaro Eto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文在半满三角晶格周期性安德森模型（periodic Anderson model）中，用基于自洽Hartree-Fock解的无规相近似（random-phase approximation）计算动力学自旋磁化率，揭示部分无序（partially disordered, PD）相中磁振子与三重子共存杂化及连续量子相变由三重子模式凝聚驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分无序相（PD phase）——磁有序位点与近藤屏蔽（Kondo-screened）位点共存——被提出可能是几何阻挫重费米子体系（geometrically frustrated heavy-fermion systems）的候选基态，但其动力学性质尚待厘清。

Method: 在半满填充的三角晶格周期性安德森模型（periodic Anderson model）上，基于自洽Hartree-Fock解构建无规相近似（random-phase approximation），计算动力学自旋磁化率（dynamical spin susceptibility）。

Result: 由近藤绝缘相到PD相、再到磁有序相的连续量子相变（quantum phase transitions），主要由源自近藤屏蔽位点的三重子模式（triplon modes）凝聚所驱动；在PD相中磁振子（magnon）与三重子共存并杂化，导致激发谱中出现特征性的能级排斥（level repulsion）。

Conclusion: 结果阐明了三重激发（triplet excitations）如何跨越连续相变演化为集体磁模式，并凸显阻挫重费米子体系中磁有序与近藤屏蔽之间的动力学相互作用。

Abstract: The partially disordered (PD) phase, in which magnetically ordered and Kondo-screened sites coexist, has been proposed as a possible ground state of geometrically frustrated heavy-fermion systems. We investigate its dynamical properties in the half-filled periodic Anderson model on a triangular lattice by calculating the dynamical spin susceptibility within the random-phase approximation built on a self-consistent Hartree-Fock solution. We show that successive quantum phase transitions from the Kondo-insulating phase to the PD phase and to a magnetically ordered phase are driven by the condensation of triplon modes originating predominantly from the Kondo-screened sites. In the PD phase, magnons and triplons coexist and hybridize, leading to characteristic level repulsion in the excitation spectrum. These results reveal how triplet excitations evolve into collective magnetic modes across the successive phase transitions and highlight the dynamical interplay between magnetic order and Kondo screening in frustrated heavy-fermion systems.

</details>


### [88] [Low-Temperature Hybrid Monte Carlo for Perylene and Corannulene: Stabilizing Determinant and Force](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32378)
*Petar Sinilkov,Thomas Luu,Johann Ostmeyer,Finn L. Temmen,Marcel Rodekamp*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作以Hubbard模型模拟在室温下研究苝（perylene）与晕苯（corannulene）分子在不同掺杂水平下的单、双（激子，exciton）及三体（trion）关联函数，并发现苝在电荷中性平台与双电荷平台之间存在相互作用诱导的ΔQ=1平台，其基态为自旋双重态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实分子体系（苝、晕苯）中于室温条件下精确刻画多体关联效应，需要既能稳定数值又保持可扩展性的模拟方法，而现有行列式量子蒙特卡洛（determinant Quantum Monte Carlo）在室温下的稳定性与效率存在局限。

Method: 采用Hubbard模型（Hubbard model）模拟，在不同掺杂水平下计算选定的单体、双体（激子）及三体（trion）关联函数；所提出的算法改进在保持与标准行列式量子蒙特卡洛相当的可扩展性（scaling）的同时，将行列式与力计算稳定至机器精度（machine precision）。该形式体系保留完整的时间位移两点格林函数（time-displaced Green's function），可构造任意n体关联函数，包括含非连通图（disconnected diagrams）的关联函数。

Result: 在苝的电荷中性平台与双电荷平台之间观察到相互作用诱导的ΔQ=1平台，其宽度随相互作用强度增大而增加，且该平台的基态为自旋双重态（spin doublet）而非单重态（singlet）。

Conclusion: 该研究表明算法改进可在室温下实现高精度且可扩展的多体关联计算，并揭示相互作用可在分子掺杂平台间诱导新的量子态（自旋双重态平台），为真实分子体系的关联效应研究提供可行框架。

Abstract: We demonstrate a real world physics study of the molecules perylene and corannulene at different doping levels, and calculating selected one-, two- (exciton) and three-body (trion) correlators. This has been done by performing Hubbard-model simulations near room temperature. We find, between charge neutral and doubly charged plateaus of perylene, an interaction-induced $ΔQ = 1$ plateau whose width grows with interaction strength and in which the ground state is a spin doublet rather than a singlet. The algorithmic advancement enabling this study simultaneously stabilizes the determinant and force calculations to machine precision at room temperature, while scaling as well as standard determinant Quantum Monte Carlo simulations of these systems. Furthermore, our formalism retains the full time-displaced (2-point) Green's function, allowing us to construct any $n$-body correlator, including those that contain disconnected diagrams.

</details>


### [89] [Hybrid Monte Carlo for the extended Kane-Mele-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32458)
*Finn L. Temmen,Thomas Luu,Johann Ostmeyer,Lin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作针对扩展Kane-Mele-Hubbard模型发展连续辅助场（continuous auxiliary field）表述，以配合混合蒙特卡罗（Hybrid Monte Carlo, HMC）算法，并系统刻画其参数空间中的符号问题（sign problem），界定可模拟区域，为完整相图研究奠定第一步基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Hubbard模型忽略非局域相互作用与自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）等实际效应；为更真实地模拟石墨烯及过渡金属二硫族化物（transition metal dichalcogenides, TMDs）等相关材料，需引入Kane-Mele SOC项与最近邻相互作用，但非局域相互作用占优时半满即出现严重符号问题，该区域此前基本未被探索。

Method: 构建扩展Kane-Mele-Hubbard模型的连续辅助场表述，使其适用于混合蒙特卡罗（HMC）算法；基于该表述在模型参数空间中表征符号问题，并确定模拟可行的参数区域。

Result: 给出了可用于HMC的连续辅助场形式，并刻画了符号问题随参数的变化，明确了模拟可行与不可行的边界，作为迈向完整相图（phase diagram）的第一步。

Conclusion: 连续辅助场表述结合HMC为研究含非局域相互作用与Kane-Mele SOC的强关联电子模型提供了可行途径；后续可在已识别的可行区域内推进完整相图的确定。

Abstract: The Hubbard model offers a powerful minimal description of strongly correlated electrons in molecules and materials, yet it neglects effects observed in nature that give rise to a much richer variety of physics. For instance, including nonlocal interactions introduces a competition between strongly correlated phases, while incorporating spin-orbit coupling (SOC) can lead to the emergence of exotic topological phases. Seeking a more realistic platform for simulating graphene and, potentially, related materials such as transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs), we therefore consider the extended Kane-Mele-Hubbard model, which features a Kane-Mele SOC term and nearest-neighbor interactions. Simulating this model is particularly challenging once the nonlocal interactions become dominant over the on-site Hubbard interaction, as a severe sign problem arises already at half filling, leaving this regime largely unexplored. In this work, we develop continuous auxiliary field formulations of the model, suitable for treatment with the Hybrid Monte Carlo (HMC) algorithm. Using these formulations, we characterize the sign problem across the model's parameter space and establish where simulations are feasible, as a first step toward the full phase diagram.

</details>


### [90] [Tensor Networks for Materials Simulation and Quantum Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32754)
*Saeed S. Jahromi,Augustine Kshetrimayum,Roman Rausch,Sukhbinder Singh,Roman Orus*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该视角文章系统梳理张量网络（tensor networks）作为超越单粒子与平均场近似的多体方法在关联材料与分子中的应用，涵盖其原理、精度决定因素、复杂度递进的应用谱系、与真实材料衔接的四条路径，并给出方法选择指南与不确定性量化建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联材料（correlated materials）与分子的性质预测需要超越单粒子近似与平均场（mean-field）近似的多体描述，而张量网络此前多用于理想化模型哈密顿量，亟需评估其融入材料特异性计算的成熟度与局限。

Method: 作为视角（Perspective）类文章，其方法为：介绍张量网络的基本原理，分析网络几何结构（network geometry）、键维截断（bond-dimension truncation）与近似收缩（approximate contraction）对精度与计算可达范围的影响；并按复杂度递增综述应用，进而考察通向真实材料的四条路径。

Result: 综述覆盖从量子磁体（quantum magnets）与巡游费米子体系（itinerant-fermion systems）的基态性质，到激发谱（excitation spectra）、非平衡动力学（nonequilibrium dynamics）、有限温度与开放系统，以及分子电子结构（molecular electronic structure）的应用；四条材料衔接路径为实验约束模型、下折叠哈密顿量（downfolded Hamiltonians）、以张量网络作杂质求解器的动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）以及量子嵌入（quantum embedding）。

Conclusion: 可靠的材料预测需量化从构造材料特异性哈密顿量到提取实验可观测量的各阶段所引入的不确定性；文章据此整合出实用的方法选择指南，并指出使张量网络成为预测性材料科学与量子化学常规工具所需的算法进展与可复现计算实践。

Abstract: Predicting the properties of correlated materials and molecules often requires a many-body description that goes beyond single-particle and mean-field approximations. Tensor networks address this challenge by exploiting the entanglement structure of quantum states to construct compact, systematically improvable representations. Although developed largely for idealized model Hamiltonians, these methods are increasingly being integrated into material-specific calculations. This Perspective introduces their underlying principles and examines how network geometry, bond-dimension truncation, and approximate contraction determine the accuracy and computational reach of these approaches. We survey applications across regimes of increasing complexity, ranging from ground-state properties of quantum magnets and itinerant-fermion systems to excitation spectra, nonequilibrium dynamics, finite-temperature and open systems, and molecular electronic structure. We then examine four routes connecting these methods to real materials, including experimentally constrained models, downfolded Hamiltonians, dynamical mean-field theory with tensor network impurity solvers, and quantum embedding. Throughout, we evaluate the maturity of existing capabilities and their principal limitations, synthesizing this assessment into a practical method-selection guide. We argue that reliable materials predictions require quantifying uncertainties introduced at each stage, from constructing material-specific Hamiltonians to extracting experimentally accessible observables. Finally, we identify the algorithmic advances and reproducible computational practices needed to establish tensor networks as a routine tool for predictive materials science and quantum chemistry.

</details>


### [91] [Unconventional charge density wave order in ErTe$_{3}$: symmetry breakings and fluctuations probed by Raman scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32797)
*Théotime Freitas,Mattia Udina,Alexandr Alekhin,Niloufar Nilforoushan,Sarah Houver,Alain Sacuto,Benito A. Gonzalez,Ian R. Fisher,Indranil Paul,Yann Gallais*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究利用偏振分辨拉曼散射追踪ErTe₃中电荷密度波相变前后的点群对称性破缺，发现镜面对称破缺与CDW序的出现紧密同步，指向单分量序参量，且转变温度以上未见显著铁轴涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验在稀土三碲化物（rare-earth tritellurides）的电荷密度波（charge density-wave, CDW）态中观察到意外的镜面对称破缺（mirror-symmetry breaking），提示可能存在铁轴CDW态（ferro-axial CDW state），其序参量本质尚不清楚。

Method: 采用偏振分辨拉曼散射（polarization-resolved Raman scattering），在ErTe₃中追踪跨越CDW相变的点群对称性破缺，并测量CDW转变温度以上A₂g对称性通道的涨落。

Result: 镜面对称破缺的出现与CDW序的建立高度吻合，暗示单一分量（single component）的CDW序参量；A₂g通道在转变温度以上未显示显著的铁轴涨落（ferro-axial fluctuations）。

Conclusion: 结果支持三碲化物中CDW态具有单分量序参量，并据此讨论其对理解该类材料CDW态本质的广泛意义。

Abstract: Recent experiments have revealed unexpected mirror-symmetry breakings in the charge density-wave (CDW) state of rare-earth tritellurides, suggesting a putative ferro-axial CDW state. Here we report polarization-resolved Raman scattering measurements tracking the point-group symmetry breakings across the CDW phase transition in the rare-earth tritelluride ErTe$_3$. We show that the mirror-symmetry breakings follow closely the emergence of the CDW order, suggesting a single component CDW order parameter. Furthermore, Raman measurements in the $A_{2g}$ symmetry channel above the CDW transition temperature indicate the absence of significant ferro-axial fluctuations. We discuss the broad implications of these findings with respect to the nature of the CDW state in the tritellurides.

</details>

### [92] [Rational $S$-functions for second order ODEs with non-Liouvillian first integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32769)
*L. G. S. Duarte,L. A. C. P. da Mota*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该文研究有理二阶常微分方程（rational second order ODE）的 S-函数方法在首积分（first integral）非 Liouvillian 时的适用性，证明一个有理 S 可同时被具有与不具有 Liouvillian 首积分的方程共享，并将 Liouvillian 性的判定归约到 Kovacic 算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 S-函数方法（S-function method）借助满足 Riccati 方程的 S=I_y/I_z 处理有理二阶 ODE，但原本是为 Liouvillian 首积分设计的；本文旨在刻画有理 S 而首积分非 Liouvillian 的情形，并厘清 S 与 Liouvillian 性之间的关系。

Method: 从任意满足 g_z≠0 的有理函数 g(x,y,z) 与多项式系数线性方程 A_2w''+A_1w'+A_0w=0 出发，通过代换 w'/w=g(x,y,y') 构造有理二阶 ODE，其首积分由两个线性无关解构成且 S=g_y/g_z；反向地，给定 (φ,S)，将方程归属该类的问题归约为显式平面向量场的有理首积分与 x 上的 Möbius 不变 Riccati 检验。

Result: Wronskian 在 S 中相消，故 S 不感知线性方程，同一有理 S 可被含与不含 Liouvillian 首积分的方程共享；转移定理（transfer theorem）表明首积分 I 为 Liouvillian 当且仅当所恢复的线性方程有 Liouvillian 解，因此该类上的 Liouvillian 性可由 Kovacic 算法判定。在九个次数不超过 15、未提供构造信息的拉回方程（pull-back equations）上，程序成功复原 Airy 方程与零阶 Bessel 方程。

Conclusion: 有理 S 本身不能保证 Liouvillian 性；本文将该类上三变量、无先验次数界的问题归约为两个经典问题（平面向量场有理首积分与 Kovacic 判定），并经验证性实验支持该方法的可行性。

Abstract: The $S$-function method for rational second order ODEs $y''=\varphi(x,y,y')$ uses $S=I_y/I_z$, attached to a first integral $I$, which satisfies a Riccati equation along the flow; it was designed for Liouvillian $I$. We study the case of rational $S$ with non-Liouvillian $I$. From any rational $g(x,y,z)$ with $g_z\neq0$ and any linear equation $A_2w''+A_1w'+A_0w=0$ with polynomial coefficients, the substitution $w'/w=g(x,y,y')$ yields a rational 2ODE whose first integral is built from two independent solutions, and $S=g_y/g_z$: the Wronskian cancels, so $S$ does not see the linear equation, and one rational $S$ is shared by equations with and without Liouvillian first integrals. Conversely, given $(\varphi,S)$, membership in this class reduces to a rational first integral of an explicit planar vector field plus a Möbius-invariant Riccati test in $x$, which recovers the linear equation. A transfer theorem shows that $I$ is Liouvillian if and only if this equation has a Liouvillian solution, so Liouvillianity is decidable on the class by Kovacic's algorithm: a three-variable problem with no a priori degree bound is reduced to two classical ones. On nine pull-back equations of degree up to $15$, given without information on their construction, the procedure recovers the Airy equation and the Bessel equation of order zero.

</details>

### [93] [Prediction of the Geoneutrino Signal at the Jinping Neutrino Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32084)
*Kaikai Meng,Yang Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文针对500吨锦屏中微子实验（Jinping Neutrino Experiment, JNE），基于SWChinaCVM-2.0地震波速度模型构建0.2°×0.2°分辨率的局部地壳模型并与CRUST1.0整合，给出精细的地球中微子（geoneutrino）通量预测及逆β衰变（inverse beta decay）事件谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为JNE的数据分析提供输入，特别是用于提取地幔地球中微子信号并检验竞争性的硅酸盐地球（bulk silicate Earth）模型，需要更精细的地球中微子通量预测。

Method: 构建基于SWChinaCVM-2.0的局部地壳模型（分辨率0.2°×0.2°）并与CRUST1.0整合形成参考模型；同时以ECM1和CRUST1.0作为独立全球地壳模型作对比。假设500 p.e./MeV的能量标度和500 t×1500天的曝光量，预测逆β衰变事件谱，其中包含地球中微子信号与反应堆反中微子本底。

Result: 获得了包含地球中微子信号与反应堆反中微子本底在内的逆β衰变事件谱预测；由于²³⁸U、²³²Th与反应堆反中微子成分的谱形各异，可通过谱拟合（spectral fitting）分别获取各自的贡献。

Conclusion: 这些预测为JNE数据分析提供输入，尤其有助于提取地幔地球中微子信号并检验相互竞争的大块硅酸盐地球（bulk silicate Earth）模型。

Abstract: We present refined predictions of the geoneutrino flux for the 500-tonne Jinping Neutrino Experiment (JNE). We construct a local crustal model with a resolution of \(0.2^\circ\times0.2^\circ\) based on the SWChinaCVM-2.0 seismic velocity model and integrate it with CRUST1.0 to establish our reference model. For comparison, we also calculate the geoneutrino flux using ECM1 and CRUST1.0 as standalone global crustal models. Assuming an energy scale of 500~p.e./MeV and an exposure of 500~t$\times$1500~days, we predict the inverse beta decay event spectrum, including both the geoneutrino signal and the reactor antineutrino background. The distinct spectral shapes of the $^{238}$U, $^{232}$Th, and reactor antineutrino components allow their respective contributions to be obtained through spectral fitting. These predictions provide inputs for the JNE data analysis, particularly for extracting the mantle geoneutrino signal and testing competing bulk silicate Earth models.

</details>

### [94] [Low-energy collective electronic excitations in a gold monolayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31805)
*Vyacheslav M. Silkin,Maxim Yu. Kagan*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该文用含时密度泛函理论（time-dependent density functional theory, TDDFT）在随机相位近似（random phase approximation, RPA）下研究自由悬浮金烯（goldene, Au(111) 单层）的低能等离激元（plasmon）性质，发现除标准二维等离激元外，还存在三条异常无隙分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金烯费米面附近仅有一条 $s-p$ 能带，电子结构看似简单，其低能等离激元行为是否同样简单尚不清楚；本文旨在检验这一简单能带体系中等离激元谱的实际结构。

Method: 基于第一性原理能带结构，采用含时密度泛函理论结合随机相位近似，计算介电函数（dielectric function）与损失函数（loss function），并分析等离激元色散（dispersion）在动量-能量空间中的分布。

Result: 计算显示，在通常归属为非相干电子-空穴对（incoherent electron-hole pairs）的更低能区，出现三条异常等离激元分支；其色散在面内强烈各向异性（anisotropic）且无隙（gapless），并在小面内动量转移下于特定方向呈类声波（sound-like）色散。

Conclusion: 异常模式的来源是金单层近乎正六边形（hexagon）的费米面（Fermi surface）显著偏离圆形，使单一能带内出现不同费米速度（Fermi velocity）的多载流子群；所发现模式对应六边形费米面两侧载流子的反相电荷振荡（out-of-phase charge oscillations），表明能带形状可将单粒子激发转化为集体激发。

Abstract: The low-energy plasmonic properties of a free-standing Au(111) monolayer, frequently referred to as goldene, are investigated by time-dependent density functional theory in the random phase approximation. In the evaluation of dielectric and loss functions the first-principle band structure characterized by a single $s-p$ energy band at the Fermi level is taken into account. In this system with apparently simple band structure around the Fermi surface the calculations show the presence, besides the standard two-dimensional (2D) plasmon mode, at even lower energies of three anomalous branches in the momentum-energy region usually assigned to incoherent electron-hole pairs. This is an unexpected result since the electronic system of the gold monolayer is characterized by a single energy band crossing the Fermi level. The dispersion of these anomalous plasmonic branches is strongly anisotropic in-plane and gapless. A general feature is that at small in-plane momentum transfers the modes have a sound-like dispersion in certain momentum directions. We rely the appearance of these anomalous modes to a notable deviation of the Fermi surface of a gold monolayer from a circular shape. As a result of its almost perfect hexagon shape, there is possibility to realize in a {\it single} electronic energy band a scenario of a multi-component electron system with the carrier groups moving with different Fermi velocities in certain momentum directions. The found modes correspond to the out-of-phase charge oscillations of the carriers at different sides of the hexagonal Fermi surface. Hence our work presents evidence how in a metal system with apparently simple band structure around the Fermi level its shape can result in conversion of single-particle charge excitations into collective charge excitations.

</details>


### [95] [Zeeman Shift and Injection Currents under Light Irradiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32136)
*Ruizhi Dong,Yihua Xiao,Pengfei Wang,Ruixiang Fei*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该文提出光电场与磁场分量联合产生的两类新二阶直流光电流——塞曼漂移电流与注入电流，导出微观电导张量并系统分类其对称性约束，发现其可在保持中心对称的体系中存在，并以Mn$_3$Sn给出可达实验量级的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二阶直流光电流由光的电场分量通过电偶极耦合驱动，且要求反演对称性破缺，限制了可产生光电流的材料范围。该文旨在探索光磁场分量参与后能否拓展光电流的产生机制与对称性条件。

Method: 采用量子微扰理论（quantum perturbation theory）推导塞曼漂移电流（Zeeman shift current）与注入电流（injection current）电导张量的显式微观表达式，并系统分类其对称性约束；结合中心对称kagome模型分析与Mn$_3$Sn的第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）进行定量评估。

Result: 两类响应可在具有自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）或非共线磁序（noncollinear magnetic order）的中心对称（centrosymmetric）体系中产生，且不依赖时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）；中心对称kagome模型表现出宽带响应。对Mn$_3$Sn的计算预测，在电场振幅$E=10^{4}~\mathrm{V/m}$光照下，塞曼注入电流与漂移电流密度分别约为$10^{2}$-$10^{3}~\mathrm{A/m^{2}}$和$1$-$10~\mathrm{A/m^{2}}$。

Conclusion: 光电场与磁场分量的联合作用构成一类区别于常规光电流的新机制，其对称性要求更为宽松（容许中心对称体系），且预测电流密度处于实验可探测范围，为光电流与光电探测材料设计提供了新路径。

Abstract: The conversion of light into electrical currents enables energy harvesting and photodetection. Conventional second-order dc photocurrents are driven by the electric component of light through electric-dipole coupling and require broken inversion symmetry. Here, we show that the electric and magnetic components of light jointly generate Zeeman shift and injection currents. Using quantum perturbation theory, we derive explicit microscopic expressions for their conductivity tensors. We systematically classify the symmetry constraints on these responses and show how they differ from those governing conventional photocurrents. In particular, both responses can occur in centrosymmetric systems with spin-orbit coupling or noncollinear magnetic order, irrespective of time-reversal symmetry. A centrosymmetric kagome model exhibits broadband responses. First-principles calculations for Mn$_3$Sn further predict Zeeman injection and shift current densities of $10^{2}$-$10^{3}~\mathrm{A/m^{2}}$ and $1$-$10~\mathrm{A/m^{2}}$, respectively, under illumination with an electric-field amplitude $E=10^{4}~\mathrm{V/m}$. These magnitudes place both currents within experimental reach.

</details>


### [96] [Electronic structure of a persistent excitonic fluid far beyond the Mott limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32437)
*Weiyan Qi,Stefano Ponzoni,Ashim Pramanik,Vitaly Gorelov,Yannis Laplace,Gianluca Stefanucci,Enrico Perfetto,Esteban Morcos,Marino Marsi,Steven Francis Udaundo,Sebastian Maherlein,Amina Taleb-Ibrahimi,Kaibo Zheng,Tonu Pullerits,Luca Perfetti*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 利用时间与角分辨光电子能谱（time- and angle-resolved photoelectron spectroscopy, tr-ARPES）在二维杂化钙钛矿（2D hybrid perovskites）中直接观测到高密度下稳定的激子态，其呈现特征性墨西哥帽色散（Mexican-hat dispersion），且持续时间远超光学相干时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为激子（exciton）在高光激发密度下会因屏蔽效应（screening）削弱电子-空穴吸引而不稳定；近期研究对此提出挑战，暗示强光泵浦产生的激子甚至可在粒子数反转（population inversion）之后保持稳定，本文旨在为此提供直接证据。

Method: 采用时间与角分辨光电子能谱（time- and angle-resolved photoelectron spectroscopy）对二维杂化钙钛矿（2D hybrid perovskites）进行测量，追踪激发后激子能带结构的时间演化。

Result: 观测到具有墨西哥帽色散（Mexican-hat dispersion）的高密度激子态；该激子能带重构（excitonic band reconstruction）的持续时间比光学相干时间（optical coherence time）长约数个数量级，并延续至非相干区域（incoherent regime）；激子共振在远超光学饱和（optical saturation）、激子波函数强烈重叠的密度下仍然存在。

Conclusion: 结果表明存在一种长寿命、高密度的激子量子流体（quantum fluid of excitons），其特征类似 BCS 凝聚体（BCS condensate），其电子结构与稀释极限（dilute limit）显著不同。

Abstract: Excitons are conventionally expected to destabilize at high photoexcitation densities, as screening weakens the electron-hole attraction. Recent studies, however, challenge this paradigm, suggesting that excitons generated by strong optical pumping can remain stable even beyond the onset of population inversion. Here, using time- and angle-resolved photoelectron spectroscopy on 2D hybrid perovskites, we provide direct evidence for such a high-density excitonic state, characterized by a distinctive Mexican-hat dispersion. Remarkably, this excitonic band reconstruction endures for orders of magnitude longer than the optical coherence time, revealing a population-sustained electronic structure that survives well into the incoherent regime. The excitonic resonance also persists far beyond optical saturation, at densities where exciton wavefunctions strongly overlap. Our findings reveal a long-lived, high-density quantum fluid of excitons with characteristics reminiscent of a BCS condensate, an electronic structure markedly different from that of the dilute limit.

</details>
