### [97] [The Galactic Center Reference Frame: A 30-Year Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31766)
*Jeremy Darling,Jennie Paine,Matthew Hosek,Shoko Sakai,Stefan Gillessen,Tatsuya Akiba-Lewis,Andrea Ghez,Frank Eisenhauer,Reinhard Genzel*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文基于30年对15颗SiO脉泽恒星及Sgr A* 43 GHz连续谱的观测，构建了银心内秒差距的更新版恒星脉泽参考框架，位置精度达0.6 AU、自行精度约2–3 km/s，并指出脉泽流量与速度结构变化可能限制未来天体测量的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恒星轨道可用于探测Sgr A*大质量黑洞（massive black hole）的度规与周围质量分布并检验引力理论，但前提是建立并维持精确的参考框架（reference frame），且需将其与Sgr A*射电连续谱对齐。

Method: 综合30年天体测量（astrometry）观测，涵盖15颗SiO脉泽（stellar maser）与Sgr A*在43 GHz的连续谱（continuum）数据，建立相对Sgr A*的脉泽系综平均偏移与自行（proper motion）框架，并评估红外与脉泽天体测量之间的框架稳定性。

Result: 脉泽系综相对Sgr A*的平均偏移不确定度当前为0.08 mas、2030年将降至0.10 mas（对应8.2 kpc距离下0.6 AU与0.8 AU）；平均自行不确定度为RA 0.05 mas/yr、Dec 0.08 mas/yr（1.8与3.2 km/s）；IR与脉泽框架稳定性为RA 10 μas/yr（0.40 km/s）、Dec 15 μas/yr（0.57 km/s）。脉泽流量密度与速度结构存在显著变化，IRS 7自2022年起蓝移成分增亮约30倍。

Conclusion: 该参考框架精度已达到或优于恒星SiO发射区尺度，可支撑黑洞、周围质量与引力检验研究；但脉泽的流量与速度结构变化可能为未来天体测量改进、尤其是径向加速度（radial acceleration）测量设定精度下限。

Abstract: Stellar orbits probe the metric of and mass around the Sgr A* massive black hole. To enable study of the black hole, the surrounding mass, and tests of gravity, a precise reference frame must be established and maintained. We present an updated stellar maser-based reference frame for the inner parsec of the Galactic Center, referenced to the radio continuum of Sgr A*. This work synthesizes 30 years of astrometric observations of 15 SiO stellar masers and the Sgr A* continuum at 43 GHz. The uncertainty in the mean offset of the maser ensemble from Sgr A* is 0.08 mas in the current epoch and will decay to 0.10 mas by 2030. At a distance of 8.2 kpc, these correspond to position uncertainties of 0.6 AU and 0.8 AU, respectively, which is similar to or smaller than the stellar SiO-emitting region. The mean maser proper motion uncertainties are 0.05 mas yr$^{-1}$ in RA and 0.08 mas yr$^{-1}$ in Dec, corresponding to 1.8 km s$^{-1}$ and 3.2 km s$^{-1}$. The reference frame stability between IR and maser astrometry is 10~$μ$as yr$^{-1}$ (0.40 km s$^{-1}$) in RA and 15 $μ$as yr$^{-1}$ (0.57 km s$^{-1}$) in Dec. The masers show substantial variability in flux density and velocity structure, most notably IRS~7, which shows a 30-fold brightening in a blueshifted component since 2022. This variability may set a floor on future astrometry improvements and especially measurements of radial acceleration.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [Existence and Absence of Spin-Glass Phases on Migdal--Kadanoff Hierarchical Lattices: A Complete Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32291)
*Manaka Okuyama,Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文在Migdal–Kadanoff（MK）分形格子上，对独立高斯耦合的Ising自旋玻璃（spin glass）模型，给出有限温度自旋玻璃序存在性的充要条件，并由此完成对MK格子上有限温度自旋玻璃序的完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋玻璃在分形维度介于二维与三维之间的格子上是否具有有限温度有序，长期缺乏严格判据；MK分层格子提供可精确重整化（exact renormalization）的可解框架，用以检验维度对自旋玻璃序的影响。

Method: 在分形维度为 d=1+log s/log b 的MK分层格子上，对独立高斯耦合（Gaussian couplings）的自旋玻璃模型做严格分析，导出以分支数 s 与串联键数 b 表述的判据，并对不同参数区间分别证明存在性、不存在性及有限步判定流程。

Result: 证明当 (b/√s)·(b√(s(s−1))/(bs−1))^{s−1}<1 时存在有限温度自旋玻璃相，特别地所有三维MK格子（d=3，即 s=b²）无论 b 取何值均成立；反之，当 d<1+log17/log6≈2.58 时不存在有限温度自旋玻璃序，且在任意固定 d<3 的大 b 极限下亦不存在；中间区间给出有限步判定程序。

Conclusion: 结果构成MK分层格子上有限温度自旋玻璃序的完整刻画（complete characterization），并表明三维MK格子普遍具有有限温度自旋玻璃相，而低于约2.58的维度则不具备。

Abstract: We consider the Ising spin-glass model on Migdal--Kadanoff (MK) hierarchical lattices, characterized by the fractal dimension $d=1+\log s/\log b$, where $s$ and $b$ denote the number of parallel branches and the number of bonds in series within each branch, respectively. For independent Gaussian couplings, we rigorously establish a necessary and sufficient condition for the existence of finite-temperature spin-glass order for arbitrary integers $b,s\ge2$. We prove that a finite-temperature spin-glass phase exists whenever \[ \frac{b}{\sqrt{s}} \left( \frac{b\sqrt{s(s-1)}}{bs-1} \right)^{s-1}<1. \] In particular, this condition holds for all three-dimensional MK hierarchical lattices ($d=3$, or equivalently $s=b^2$), regardless of the choice of $b$. Conversely, we prove the absence of finite-temperature spin-glass order for fractal dimensions $d<1+\log17/\log6\approx2.58$, as well as in the large-$b$ limit for any fixed $d<3$. In the intermediate regime $1+\log17/\log6\le d<3$, we provide a finite-step procedure to determine the presence or absence of spin-glass order, which depends on $b$ and $s$. Our results provide a complete characterization of finite-temperature spin-glass order on MK hierarchical lattices.

</details>


### [99] [Boundary shape controls the stress distribution in two-component elastic materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32072)
*Kyungeun Kim,Adam Clare,Miranda Holmes-Cerfon*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究通过双组分三角弹性弹簧网络（two-component triangular elastic spring networks）模拟，揭示高刚度区域（high-stiffness regions）的形状与排布如何调控应力重分布，并提出界面键数量、分区尺度与梯度过渡的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解在空间相关与不相关随机预应力（prestress）下，材料内部结构（即高刚度区域的形态与分布）如何控制能量传递与应力集中，从而为机械鲁棒性设计提供依据。

Method: 构建双组分三角弹性弹簧网络模型，比较边界粗糙度（boundary roughness）、划分为不连通区域（partitioning）、以及梯度过渡（graded transitions）三种排布方式，并施加空间相关与不相关预应力进行数值模拟。

Result: 界面键（interface bonds）被识别为能量传递通道：其数量增加可提供额外传递路径并降低刚性区域的应力集中，但在柔性子网络中收益有限。固定高刚度占比时，分区可降低两类预应力下的应力集中，但过度细分使界面键数量接近内部节点数后收益递减；应力降低幅度依赖预应力的空间相关性（spatial correlations），随机预应力通常效果更强；梯度界面通过拓宽刚度过渡区域进一步降低峰值应力。

Conclusion: 结果提示三类设计策略：增大界面面积、使细分尺度与预应力相关性匹配、梯度化刚度过渡。该最小化框架阐明内部结构如何控制能量传递与应力重分布，可为内部与外部载荷下的机械鲁棒性设计提供原则。

Abstract: We investigate how the shape and arrangement of high-stiffness regions govern stress redistribution in two-component triangular elastic spring networks subjected to spatially correlated and uncorrelated random prestress. We compare boundary roughness, partitioning into disconnected regions, and graded transitions between regions of different stiffness. Our simulations identify interface bonds as channels for energy transfer: increasing their number provides additional transfer paths and reduces stress concentration in mechanically rigid regimes, while offering little benefit in compliant subnetworks. This is analogous to defects in naturally occurring crystal structures, where the defects themselves isolate stress. At a fixed fraction of high-stiffness material, partitioning reduces stress concentration for both correlated and uncorrelated prestress. The benefit diminishes with further subdivision as the number of interface bonds becomes comparable to the number of interior nodes. Stress reduction, however, depends on the spatial correlations of the imposed prestress: random prestress generally yields a stronger effect, though the difference diminishes in some regimes. Graded interfaces further reduce peak stress by distributing the stiffness transition over a broader region. Together, these results suggest design strategies based on increasing interface area, matching subdivision scale to prestress correlations, and grading stiffness transitions. This minimal framework identifies how internal structure controls energy transfer and stress redistribution, suggesting design principles for mechanical robustness under both internal and external loading.

</details>


### [100] [Generalization properties of the token perceptron: the role of embedding and vocabulary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32788)
*Dario Bocchi,Matteo Negri,Flavio Nicoletti,Francesco D'Amico*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文提出并求解一个最小可解模型“token 感知机（token perceptron）”，将大语言模型的词元预测视为受嵌入几何约束的分类问题，用复制方法（replica method）计算其泛化误差，揭示词表嵌入结构对学习的定量影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（large language models）的词元预测本质是分类问题，其类别由嵌入空间（embedding space）中的向量几何决定，但该几何如何影响学习尚缺乏理论刻画。作者构造最小可解模型以隔离并量化嵌入几何的作用。

Method: 研究一个线性预测器：把 N 个嵌入词元经每个位置一个 d×d 矩阵映射为 d 维输出，采用教师—学生（teacher–student）设定训练。用复制方法（replica method）在 N→∞、每位置样本数 α=P/N 固定、d 与词表大小 D 有限的条件下计算泛化误差，并分别考察球面连续嵌入与词表嵌入两种情形。

Result: 在球面连续嵌入下，自然载荷为 α/d，d>1 时归一化标签使问题退化为无噪回归（noiseless regression），教师可在 α_rec=d²/(d−1) 以上被精确恢复，球形交叉熵（spherical cross-entropy）在大逆温度下达到该阈值；随机关联的存储容量（storage capacity）在间隔为 O(1/√d) 时按 d² 标度。词表嵌入下标签仅确定教师输出的 Voronoi 胞腔，故样本给出不等式约束；通过近似谱封闭（spectral closure）求得 Bayes 最优误差，并表明最优学习器优先解析词表方差最大的方向，即使教师是各向同性的。

Conclusion: 嵌入几何显著改变学习行为：连续嵌入情形呈现清晰的恢复阈值，而词表嵌入将问题转化为感知机式不等式约束，其最优学习受词表方差方向主导。该框架为理解大语言模型中嵌入与预测性能的关系提供了解析基础。

Abstract: Large language models are trained to predict tokens from a finite vocabulary, each represented by a vector in a $d$-dimensional embedding space. Therefore, token prediction is a classification problem whose classes have a geometry determined by the embedding. We study how this geometry affects learning in a minimal solvable model: a linear predictor that maps a sequence of $N$ embedded tokens to a $d$-dimensional output through one $d\times d$ matrix per position, trained in the teacher--student setting. We call this the \textit{token perceptron}. We compute its generalization error with the replica method, taking $N\to\infty$ at fixed number of examples per position $α=P/N$ and keeping $d$ and the vocabulary size $D$ finite. As a first reference case, we consider continuous embeddings on the sphere, which isolate the role of the embedding dimension. The natural load is then $α/d$, and for $d>1$ a normalized label fixes the direction of the teacher output, so the problem becomes a noiseless regression: the teacher is recovered exactly above $α_{\rm rec}=d^2/(d-1)$, and spherical cross-entropy reaches this threshold at large inverse temperature. In the same setting the storage capacity of random associations scales as $d^2$ for margins $O(1/\sqrt{d})$. We then turn to vocabulary embeddings, where a label only identifies the Voronoi cell of the teacher output, so that each example imposes inequalities rather than equations, as in the classical perceptron. We compute the Bayes-optimal error for a given embedding matrix through an approximate spectral closure and show that the optimal learner first resolves the directions in which the vocabulary has the largest variance, even when the teacher is isotropic.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Cross-coupled photon avalanche drives room-temperature intrinsic optical bistability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32032)
*Eva Conour,Jacob Wojcik,Jory Sanders,Abigale Puccini,Trey Stephens-Cherry,Artiom Skripka*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究报告了在常压（ambient conditions）下实现无腔本征光学双稳性（intrinsic optical bistability, IOB）的Ho³⁺激活纳米晶，其非热机制源于交叉耦合光子雪崩（cross-coupled photon avalanche, XPA），并实现高达46 dB的高对比度光开关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学双稳材料在光信息处理、忆阻器件（memristive devices）和多参量传感中具有应用潜力，但现有光学双稳性多在低温条件下观测，或由微弱的热光非线性（thermo-optical nonlinearities）主导，限制了实际应用。

Method: 采用低声子能量的双钙钛矿（double-perovskite）基质承载Ho³⁺激活纳米晶，以降低非辐射损耗并通过XPA机制构建激发态布居；借助时间分辨发光测量（time-resolved luminescence measurements）验证机制，观测到与两个储能态（reservoir states）能量循环锁定相一致的双上升（double-rise）曲线。

Result: 在室温下于多条可见发射线观测到IOB，光开关对比度最高达46 dB，可支持波分复用（wavelength-division multiplexing）并与常规探测器兼容；IOB在纳米至微米尺度晶体及不同双钙钛矿组分中均得以保持，且即使在泵浦强度低于阈值时XPA仍可促进激发态布居。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种无需光学腔、非热、可在常温工作的本征光学双稳性方案，材料与尺度可扩展性强，有望广泛推动信息光子学（information photonics）与先进传感的发展。

Abstract: Optically bistable materials promise technological advances in optical information processing, memristive devices, and multiparametric sensing. However, optical bistability is predominantly observed under cryogenic conditions or is governed by subtle thermo-optical nonlinearities. Here, we report on Ho3+-activated nanocrystals that exhibit cavity-free intrinsic optical bistability (IOB) at ambient conditions. Non-thermal IOB stems from a cross-coupled photon avalanche (XPA) mechanism enabled by a low-phonon-energy double-perovskite host, which reduces nonradiative losses and facilitates excited-state population via XPA, even when the pump intensity is below threshold. Time-resolved luminescence measurements support the proposed XPA, as evidenced by a double-rise profile consistent with two reservoir states interlocked in energy looping. IOB with high-contrast photoswitching (up to 46 dB) is observed at room temperature across multiple visible emission lines, enabling wavelength-division multiplexing and compatibility with common detectors. IOB is preserved across nano- to microscale crystals and different double-perovskite compositions, broadly enabling information photonics and advanced sensing.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [CBTV: Colour Bean Tracking Velocimetry for Dense Granular Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31883)
*Kaveeshan Thurairajah,Zhao Pan*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出免训练（training-free）的多遍粒子追踪方法 CBTV，借助多色颗粒的邻域星座匹配提升密集颗粒流追踪，在高密度位移区间优于多种基线追踪器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统粒子追踪测速（particle tracking velocimetry, PTV）在密集颗粒系统中面临帧间位移接近或超过颗粒间距、相邻颗粒协同运动、以及高能碰撞后基于速度的预测失效等问题，现有方法易丢失身份或发生身份互换。

Method: CBTV 利用多色颗粒，先以互相关运动先验（cross-correlation motion prior）为引导，将相邻彩色颗粒构成的星座（constellation）整体匹配，再对剩余颗粒逐一分配。并将其与 12 种基线追踪器配置（从经典链接器到图神经网络与 transformer 模型）在 15 个模拟离散元（discrete-element）颗粒流的 59 个案例上比较。

Result: 在脉冲碰撞（impulsive collision）场景下，CBTV 在各位移量级均达到或超过所有被测追踪器；其优势集中在每帧位移约半个至两倍颗粒间距的密集流区间。搅拌剪切流（agitated shear flow）中，同样利用邻域上下文的颜色松弛追踪器（colour relaxation tracker）可持平或略优于 CBTV；位移超过约 2–3 个颗粒直径时，补偿整体运动的追踪器更准确。

Conclusion: 对实验研究者而言，使用彩色颗粒的密集颗粒流可在原本会导致无邻域上下文追踪器丢失身份的帧间位移下被成功追踪，从而在不需训练数据的前提下降低对超高帧率的要求。

Abstract: Conventional particle tracking velocimetry (PTV) has been the standard diagnostic tool for studying fluid and granular flows. However, in dense granular systems, the average distance moved by particles between frames can approach or exceed inter-particle spacing, demanding ultra-high frame rates. Furthermore, neighbouring particles may move together, and common velocity-based prediction fails after energetic collisions. Existing methods struggle to track granular particles without losing identities or swapping. We present Colour Bean Tracking Velocimetry (CBTV), a training-free multi-pass method leveraging multicoloured particles to achieve enhanced tracking. CBTV first matches constellations of neighbouring coloured particles as single units, guided by a cross-correlation motion prior, and only then assigns the remaining particles individually. We compare it with 12 baseline tracker configurations, from classical linkers to graph-neural-network and transformer models, on 59 cases from 15 simulated discrete-element granular flows with exact ground truth (0.006-9 diameters/frame). CBTV matches or exceeds every tracker tested on an impulsive collision at every displacement. Its advantage lies in dense flows in which particles move between about half and twice their spacing per frame, where links between frames are ambiguous. On agitated shear flow, a colour relaxation tracker, which also uses neighbour context, matches or narrowly exceeds CBTV. Where links are unambiguous, CBTV is close to the best tracker, and beyond about two to three particle diameters per frame, the range of its motion prior, trackers that compensate for bulk motion are more accurate. For experimentalists, dense granular flows of coloured particles can be tracked at displacements per frame where trackers without neighbour context lose identities, easing the demand for ultra-high frame rates without training data.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Photon-Number Discrimination from Increasing Time-of-Arrival Separation in Wide Superconducting Microstrip Single-Photon Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32154)
*Hsin-Yeh Wu,Shu-Xiao Liu,Boris Tuchming,Xavier-François Navick,Pu-Kai Wang,Yi-Ren Wu,Jia-Wern Chen,Marc Besançon,Jean-François Glicenstein,Pisin Chen,Yu-Jung Lu,Stathes Paganis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作测量了宽 NbN 超导微带单光子探测器（superconducting microstrip single-photon detector, SMSPD）中光子数依赖的到达时间（time-of-arrival, TOA）分布，并证明宽器件可实现高光子数下的 TOA 光子数分辨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规窄线宽超导纳米线单光子探测器（SNSPD）中相邻光子数的 TOA 时序移位随光子数增加而被压缩，限制了基于 TOA 的高光子数判别，因此需要探索宽微带器件作为替代平台。

Method: 在 \SI{515}{nm} 脉冲激发下表征两个长度 \SI{10}{\um}、宽度分别为 \SI{3.2}{\um} 与 \SI{6.4}{\um} 的 NbN 器件，用指数修正高斯（exponentially modified Gaussian, EMG）分量之和拟合 TOA 直方图，并提出受吸收位置依赖涡旋过程与集体电流重分布启发的唯象模型。

Result: 宽器件出现可指认至 n=8 的分量；高光子数下相邻 TOA 峰相对其宽度更易区分；对 n=7 与 n=8，FWHM 归一化峰间距为 2.15，等先验成对 TOA 指认保真度为 99.6%；EMG 指数尾参数 τ_n 随光子数显著下降，而高斯位置参数 μ_n 在相邻分量间持续移动。

Conclusion: 宽 SMSPD 可作为高光子数 TOA 光子数判别平台，适用于多光子态表征与光子量子信息处理，同时为研究吸收空间分布对涡旋辅助探测的影响提供实验场景。

Abstract: Measurements of photon-number-dependent time-of-arrival (TOA) distributions in wide NbN superconducting microstrip single-photon detectors (SMSPDs) are presented. Two \SI{10}{\um}-long devices with widths of \SI{3.2}{\um} and \SI{6.4}{\um} are characterized under pulsed \SI{515}{nm} excitation. The TOA histograms are described by sums of exponentially modified Gaussian (EMG) components, with the wider device exhibiting components assigned to photon numbers up to \(n=8\). Adjacent TOA peaks become more distinguishable relative to their widths at higher photon number, in contrast to the compression of neighboring photon-number timing shifts observed in conventional narrow-wire SNSPDs. For the components assigned to \(n=7\) and \(n=8\), the FWHM-normalized peak separation is \(2.15\), and the fitted EMG distributions yield an equal-prior pairwise TOA-assignment fidelity of \(99.6\%\). The EMG exponential-tail parameter \(τ_n\) decreases strongly with photon number, while the Gaussian-location parameter \(μ_n\) continues to shift between neighboring components. A phenomenological model motivated by absorption-site-dependent vortex processes and collective current redistribution captures these timing trends. These results support wide SMSPDs as a platform for TOA-based photon-number discrimination at high photon number, relevant to multiphoton-state characterization and photonic quantum information processing. The same geometry also provides a setting to examine how the spatial distribution of photon absorption affects vortex-assisted detection.

</details>


### [104] [Performing a Quantum Subroutine Using Cluster States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32227)
*Sakibul Islam Rayhan,Karima Hassan,Md Rasedujjaman,Md Mohsinur Rahman Adnan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作在测量型量子计算（measurement based quantum computation, MBQC）框架下，采用实验可行的簇态（cluster states）实现了一个可复用的相位预言机（phase oracle）子程序，并给出了其测量模式、副产品关联（byproduct correlations）、密度矩阵模拟及测量电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子子程序（quantum subroutines）是构建大型算法的关键组件，但此前 MBQC 中的 Grover 算法演示均省略了预言机的显式构造，缺乏对预言机子程序本身的透明表述。

Method: 基于门架构或 MBQC 中的自适应测量机制，利用实验可行的簇态实现相位预言机，并系统给出其测量模式、副产品关联、密度矩阵模拟以及相应的 MBQC 测量电路。

Result: 给出了相位预言机子程序的显式且透明的构造，涵盖测量模式、副产品关联、密度矩阵模拟与测量电路四个层面，且该预言机可作为可复用子程序嵌入更大算法。

Conclusion: 该工作弥补了既有 MBQC Grover 演示中预言机构造缺失的问题，为在 MBQC 框架内实现可复用量子子程序提供了可实验落地的参考方案。

Abstract: Quantum subroutines, like their classical counterparts, serve as essential components of larger algorithms. They can be realized using gate based architectures or measurement based quantum computation (MBQC), where computation proceeds through adaptive measurements on highly entangled cluster states. In this work, we implement a phase oracle as a reusable subroutine within the MBQC framework using experimentally feasible cluster states. Unlike prior demonstrations of Grover's algorithm in MBQC that omit explicit oracle construction, our approach provides a transparent formulation of the oracle subroutine, including its measurement patterns, byproduct correlations, density-matrix simulations, and corresponding MBQC measurement circuits.

</details>


### [105] [An Invitation to Algebraic Methods in Quantum Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32659)
*Peter T. J. Bradshaw,Jonte R. Hance*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以谐振子（harmonic oscillator）、氢原子（hydrogenic atom）、光学元件（optical components）与粒子自旋（particle spin）为范例，系统展示如何用代数方法（algebraic methods）分析量子系统，强调算符间代数关系对决定系统物理性质的核心作用，并定位为面向读者的代数量子力学入门导引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为感兴趣的读者提供一份关于量子力学中代数方法的温和入门（gentle introduction），并借此指明该领域活跃的研究方向。

Method: 对多个已被充分研究的量子系统采用多种代数方法进行分析，重点考察算符之间的代数关系（algebraic relationships between operators），并借助李代数（Lie algebra）结构刻画问题的（常为隐含的）对称性。

Result: 展示了若干具体结论：李代数 𝔰𝔬(2,1)≅𝔰𝔲(1,1) 作为“谱生成代数（spectrum generating algebra）”具有普遍性；𝔰𝔲(2) 与 𝔰𝔲(1,1) 在确定光学元件作用中扮演关键角色；对粒子自旋的初等代数处理相较常规方法可提供对其本质的新见解；理解问题的完整（往往隐藏的）对称性可使问题被直接求解而无需解微分方程（differential equations）。

Conclusion: 算符间的代数关系是决定量子系统物理特征的中心要素，且把握问题的隐藏对称性可避免求解微分方程而直接获得解答；本文作为入门性综述，同时标示了共同体中的活跃研究领域。

Abstract: In this paper, we present various well-studied examples of quantum systems, and discuss how they might be analysed algebraically. We analyse the harmonic oscillator, hydrogenic atom, optical components, and particle spin using multiple algebraic methods, and highlight the central importance of algebraic relationships between operators in determining the physical character of the system. In particular, we show: the ubiquity of the Lie algebra $\mathfrak{so}(2,1)\cong\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$ as the ``spectrum generating algebra''; the role of $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ and $\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$ in determining the action of optical components; that an elementary algebraic approach to particle spin offers novel insights into its nature versus the usual approaches; and how understanding the full (often hidden) symmetry of a problem enables it to be solved immediately without the need to solve differential equations. We hope this work will serve as a gentle introduction to algebraic methods in quantum mechanics for the interested reader --- we therefore signpost active areas of research within the community.

</details>


### [106] [Fermion Scattering on Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32882)
*Steven Abel,Michael Spannowsky,Simon Williams*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个混合量子计算（hybrid quantum computing）框架，用费米子—反费米子碰撞研究共振散射（resonant scattering）与不稳定激发态的衰变动力学，并以张量网络（tensor network）模拟作基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共振散射把粒子相互作用与不稳定激发的形成和衰变联系起来，但此类实时动力学缺乏可执行的量子计算基准，摘要以此为出发点构建散射基准。

Method: 采用(1+1)维汤川理论（Yukawa theory）的混合编码：费米子映射到量子比特（qubit），标量场映射到量子模式（qumode）；确定光子学实现所需操作，并用张量网络模拟对动力学进行基准测试。

Result: 在共振附近解析出局域标量激发的建立及能量向费米子的部分回流，伴随纠缠增强（entanglement）；随时间变化的关联子测量复现解析衰变率，精度达百分之几，能量扫描呈现与波包平均Breit–Wigner行为一致的共振峰。

Conclusion: 结果确立了混合量子计算机的具体散射基准，并表明局域实时可观测量（real-time observables）可探测粒子—共振动力学。

Abstract: Resonant scattering links particle interactions to the formation and decay of unstable excitations. We develop a hybrid quantum-computing framework to study these dynamics through fermion--antifermion collisions. Our formulation of $(1+1)$-dimensional Yukawa theory encodes fermions in qubits and the scalar field in qumodes. We identify the operations needed for a photonic implementation and benchmark the dynamics with tensor-network simulations. Near resonance, we resolve the build-up of a localised scalar excitation and the subsequent partial return of energy to the fermions. Enhanced entanglement accompanies this exchange. Time-dependent correlator measurements reproduce the analytically predicted decay rate to a few percent precision, with energy scans exhibiting a resonance peak consistent with packet-averaged Breit--Wigner behaviour. These results establish a concrete scattering benchmark for hybrid quantum computers and demonstrate how local real-time observables can probe particle-resonance dynamics.

</details>


### [107] [Quantum Complexity and Stabilizer-State Expansions: From Pairing to Deformation in Fermionic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31792)
*S. Momme Hengstenberg,Caroline E. P. Robin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究了兼具形变（deformation）与配对（pairing）两种集体行为（collective phenomena）的费米子系统中的量子复杂性（量子纠缠、非稳定子性 non-stabilizerness 与费米子非高斯性 fermionic non-Gaussianity），并提出结合稳定子态展开（stabilizer-state expansion）与变分基优化（variational basis optimization）的经典可扩展、保持对称性的相图描述方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解配对、形变等集体现象如何在相互作用费米子系统中涌现，以及其背后关联如何影响模拟所需资源，是多体物理（many-body physics）的核心挑战；本文据此探究该系统量子复杂性的多个方面及其可经典处理的描述方式。

Method: 通过纠缠、非稳定子性与费米子非高斯性刻画两种集体相的量子复杂性；随后结合稳定子态展开与变分基优化，并借助物理引导的哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）分解识别相关稳定子态（可由图态 graph states 构造），以经典可扩展且保持对称性的方式描述相图。

Result: 形变相中高纠缠与低非稳定子性共存；超流配对（superfluid pairing）相则表现出显著的多体纠缠（multipartite entanglement）与非稳定子性，并伴随最大费米子反平坦性（fermionic anti-flatness）。单一稳定子态即可在很大程度上刻画形变相；描述配对相所需稳定子态数量随系统规模指数增长，但保持对称性的基旋转可用远少的态实现高保真近似。

Conclusion: 保持对称性的基旋转得到的近似态可作为量子精化（quantum refinement）的实用起点，为以经典可扩展方式、保持对称性地描述该类费米子系统的相图提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Understanding how collective phenomena such as pairing and deformation emerge in interacting fermionic systems, and how the correlations underlying them shape the resources required for their simulation, is a central challenge in many-body physics. We investigate various aspects of quantum complexity in a fermionic system exhibiting two main forms of collectivity, specifically deformation and pairing, through entanglement, non-stabilizerness, and fermionic non-Gaussianity. In the deformed phase, high entanglement coexists with low non-stabilizerness, while the superfluid pairing phase, by contrast, exhibits substantial multipartite entanglement and non-stabilizerness, together with maximal fermionic anti-flatness. In a second step we examine how to describe the phase diagram of the system in a classically scalable and symmetry-preserving way, by combining stabilizer-state expansion and variational basis optimization. A physics-guided decomposition of the Hamiltonian identifies the relevant stabilizer states, which can be constructed from graph states. A single stabilizer state captures the deformed phase to a large extent, and, although the number of stabilizer states required to describe the pairing phase grows exponentially with system size, a symmetry-preserving basis rotation yields a high-fidelity approximation using far fewer states. This rotated state can then serve as a practical starting point for quantum refinement.

</details>


### [108] [QuantumMatterExplorer: An Autonomous Agent for the Discovery of Quantum Many-Body Phases of Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32001)
*Jannes Nys,Andrew Jreissaty,Juan Carrasquilla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出自主智能体 QuantumMatterExplorer，仅以哈密顿量及其参数范围为输入，通过长期规划与数值计算工具自动输出相图，并在阻挫自旋-1链上复现了近期发表的二维相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绘制新量子哈密顿量的相图依赖专家在观测量选择、参数空间采样与有限尺寸效应判别上的判断，过程耗时且难以自动化，因此需要能自主推理并驱动计算的智能体。

Method: 智能体基于大语言模型（large language model）构建，具备长时程推理、持久书面计划与专家计算物理技能库，驱动密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）计算，并以独立精确对角化（exact diagonalization）进行交叉验证；随证据积累自适应选择观测量、细化参数扫描并做有限尺寸分析（finite-size analysis）。

Result: 在已知相变的哈密顿量上完成验证后，于含单离子各向异性（single-ion anisotropy）的阻挫双线性-双二次自旋-1链上，在无任何物理提示条件下识别出Néel、Haldane与二聚化（dimerized）候选相，与近期发表的参考相图大体一致；还探测到非公度关联（incommensurate correlations）并推断出相边界的几何形状随阻挫的变化，全程在八小时计算预算内完成。

Conclusion: 结果表明该智能体可自主完成量子物态相图的数值探索，为量子物质新相的自动化计算发现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Mapping the phase diagram of a new quantum Hamiltonian demands expert judgement at every step: which observables to measure, where in parameter space to compute, or how to separate genuine order from finite-size artefacts. We introduce QuantumMatterExplorer, an autonomous agent that takes only a Hamiltonian and its parameter ranges as input and returns a phase diagram obtained from its own numerical simulations. The agent is built on a large language model and is designed to reason over a long horizon, maintain a persistent written plan, and drive a set of computational tools, with density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations cross-checked against independent exact diagonalization. The agent combines a large language model with persistent planning, a library of expert computational-physics skills, and density-matrix renormalization group calculations validated against independent exact diagonalization. It adaptively selects quantities to evaluate, refines parameter scans, and performs finite-size analyses as evidence accumulates. After validating the approach on Hamiltonians with known transitions, we challenge it with a frustrated bilinear-biquadratic spin-1 chain with single-ion anisotropy, whose two-dimensional phase diagram was published only recently and is unlikely to appear in any training corpus. Given no hint about the expected physics, the agent proposes which observables to probe, and identifies candidate Néel, Haldane and dimerized phases, in broad agreement with the reference phase diagram. It also detects incommensurate correlations and infers the frustration-dependent geometry of the phase boundaries, all within an eight-hour compute budget. These results open a practical route to the autonomous computational discovery of new phases of quantum matter.

</details>


### [109] [Near-field maps of topological edge states of molecular aggregates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32287)
*Aritra Mishra,Sidhartha Nayak,Alexander Eisfeld*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出利用空间分辨近场光谱（spatially resolved near-field spectroscopy）探测二维分子聚集体中的激子边缘态，并证明卷积神经网络（convolutional neural network）可从近场图谱中稳健重构本征态的复展开系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自组装二维分子聚集体可承载拓扑非平庸边缘态（topologically nontrivial edge states），但这类态远场跃迁强度弱，且远场谱无法判断激发是否局域于边缘，因此需要新的光学探测手段。

Method: 针对由两个分子亚晶格（sublattice）构成、具有真复跃迁偶极（complex transition dipoles）的聚集体，分析其空间分辨近场光谱；并用卷积神经网络从近场图谱反演本征态的复展开系数。

Result: 近场图谱并不反映格点布居（site populations），而呈现强烈非均匀、类干涉（interference-like）的特征；卷积神经网络可由这些图谱重构本征态的复展开系数，且对显著随机噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 空间分辨近场光谱结合神经网络反演，为表征二维分子聚集体中边缘态等激子态的全空间结构提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Self-assembled two-dimensional molecular aggregates provide a versatile platform for engineering excitonic states, including topologically nontrivial edge states. However, these edge-states are difficult to probe optically: they have weak far-field transition strengths, and far-field spectra do not reveal whether an excitation is localized at the edge. For an aggregate consisting of two sublattices of molecules with genuinely complex transition dipoles, we show that spatially resolved near-field spectroscopy encodes the full spatial structure of these states. The resulting near-field maps do not mirror the site populations, but instead exhibit strongly nonuniform, interference-like features. We demonstrate that a convolutional neural network can reconstruct the complex expansion coefficients of the eigenstates from these maps, and that this reconstruction is robust against substantial random noise.

</details>


### [110] [Entanglement Asymmetry and Asymmetry Contour under Boundary Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32733)
*Takahiro Orito,Daisuke Yamamoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究对称破缺边界磁场对横场伊辛模型中磁化与Rényi-2纠缠不对称性（entanglement asymmetry, EA）的影响，并区分基态相行为与边界淬火动力学，进一步提出"不对称性轮廓"以刻画EA的空间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注EA的整体量行为，缺乏对对称破缺信息空间结构的刻画，尤其EA作为子系统积分量难以直接分辨空间分布，因此需要引入可空间解析的补充量。

Method: 在横场伊辛模型（transverse-field Ising model）中施加对称破缺边界磁场，计算基态磁化与Rényi-2 EA，推导EA的上下界并研究临界区行为；随后研究边界淬火（boundary quench）动力学，并引入不对称性轮廓（asymmetry contour）这一类等值线（contour-like）的有限差分量。

Result: 基态磁化在有序相与无序相呈现定性不同的行为，EA亦呈相位依赖行为并被所推上下界良好描述；临界区结果与相关场论预言定性一致。动力学中磁化以符合最大准粒子速度的速度从边界向体传播；EA因是子系统积分量而空间结构不能被直接分辨，而不对称性轮廓提供空间解析刻画，且其动力学反映准粒子传播。

Conclusion: EA与不对称性轮廓分别从整体与空间层面提供对称破缺的互补信息论刻画，轮廓对非局域多体关联保持敏感，可作为局部磁化的补充。

Abstract: We investigate how symmetry-breaking boundary magnetic fields affect magnetization and Rényi-2 entanglement asymmetry (EA) in the transverse-field Ising model. For the ground state, magnetization exhibits qualitatively distinct behaviors in the ordered and disordered phases. EA also exhibits phase-dependent behavior, which is well captured by the lower and upper bounds derived in this work. In the critical regime, both magnetization and EA exhibit behavior qualitatively consistent with the field-theoretical predictions for a related setup. We also study boundary-quench dynamics and find that magnetization propagates from the boundary into the bulk with a velocity consistent with the maximum quasiparticle velocity. In contrast, because EA is a subsystem-integrated quantity, its spatial structure is not directly resolved. We therefore introduce the asymmetry contour, a contour-like finite-difference quantity that provides a spatially resolved characterization of the symmetry-breaking information encoded in the reduced density matrix. The resulting contour is complementary to the local magnetization, retaining sensitivity to nonlocal many-body correlations while its dynamics also reflects the underlying quasiparticle propagation. These results show how EA and its contour can provide complementary information-theoretic characterizations of symmetry breaking and its spatial structure.

</details>


### [111] [Generating entanglement in quantum matter with pathway-entangled photons via resonant inelastic X-ray scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32936)
*Chunjing Jia,Valentin Walther,Laimei Nie*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种干涉型共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）协议，通过分束器分束与复合，在固态材料空间上分离的两端之间产生纠缠，实现DLCZ预示方案的X射线固态类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RIXS传统上仅用于探测量子材料中的元激发与关联，作者希望拓展其功能，使其从探测工具转变为在固体中制备纠缠态的量子资源工具。

Method: 入射X射线光子经分束器（beam splitter）相干分束为两臂照射样品两端，散射光在第二个分束器复合后进行能量选择探测，从而将材料投影到两端激发的相干叠加态；采用量子化光子模式的Kramers-Heisenberg形式体系进行分析。

Result: 正交且简并的端点激发可产生最大纠缠态；在三个模型中演示：两个解耦格点给出预示型贝尔态（Bell state）；开放紧束缚链（tight-binding chain）呈现依赖反射宇称的干涉，可作为干涉仪相干性的基准；自旋为1的Haldane（AKLT）链给出分数化边缘自旋（fractionalized edge spins）的纠缠对。

Conclusion: 该协议为X射线固态体系中制备纠缠提供可行方案，并讨论了在X射线实验中实现该协议的实际考量与挑战。

Abstract: Resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) is conventionally used to probe elementary excitations and correlations in quantum materials. Here, we propose an interferometric RIXS protocol that instead creates entanglement between spatially separated regions of a solid-state material. An incident X-ray photon is coherently split by a beam splitter into two arms illuminating opposite ends of a sample. Recombining the scattered photons at a second beam splitter and detecting an energy-selected photon then projects the material into a coherent superposition of excitations at the two ends. This protocol is the X-ray, solid-state analogue of the Duan-Lukin-Cirac-Zoller (DLCZ) heralding scheme. Using a Kramers-Heisenberg formalism with quantized photon modes, we show that orthogonal and degenerate end excitations yield maximally entangled states. We illustrate this mechanism in three models: (1) two decoupled sites yield a heralded Bell state. (2) An open tight-binding chain exhibits reflection-parity-dependent interference, providing a benchmark for the interferometer's coherence. (3) A spin-1 Haldane (AKLT) chain yields an entangled pair of fractionalized edge spins. We also discuss practical considerations and challenges for realizing the protocol in X-ray experiments.

</details>


<div id='math.DS'></div>

# math.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [A generalization of Gordon's Theorem to arbitrary winding number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31872)
*M. Fenucci*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该文对周期环上具有齐次势（homogeneous potential）$1/|u|^\alpha$ 的开普勒作用量泛函（Keplerian action functional）极小元进行完整分类，填补了 $\alpha\in(1,2)$、$|k|\geq 2$ 的空白情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Gordon 与 Venturelli 定理未覆盖 $\alpha\in(1,2)$ 且 $|k|\geq 2$ 的情形，本文旨在关闭这一遗留的开放情形，并给出极小元随 $\alpha$ 与绕数（winding number）$k$ 变化的完整刻画。

Method: 在固定绕数 $k\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\{0\}$ 的周期环上研究开普勒作用量极小元；通过确定 $k$-圆解（$k$-circular solution）失去局部极小性（local minimality）的阈值 $\alpha=2-1/|k|^2$，并证明在 $\alpha=2-1/|k|$ 以上极小元无碰撞（collision-free），再结合中间区间进动周期轨道（precessing periodic orbits）的存在性完成分类，并对 $k=2$ 做数值验证。

Result: $k$-圆解在 $\alpha=2-1/|k|^2$ 以下失去局部极小性；当 $\alpha=2-1/|k|$ 以上时极小元无碰撞；中间区域存在进动周期轨道，据此得到极小元关于 $\alpha$ 与 $k$ 的完整分类，且 $k=2$ 的数值结果与之相符。

Conclusion: 研究扩展并补全了 Gordon–Venturelli 型定理，确立了以阈值 $\alpha=2-1/|k|^2$ 与 $\alpha=2-1/|k|$ 为分界的极小元完整相图（phase classification），并得到数值支持。

Abstract: We study minimizers of the Keplerian action functional with homogeneous potential of type $1/|u|^α$ on periodic loops with fixed winding number $k \in \mathbb{Z}\setminus\{0\}$. This extends the classical theorems of Gordon and Venturelli, closing the previously open case $α\in (1,2)$, $|k|\geq 2$. We show that the $k$-circular solution loses local minimality below the threshold $α= 2-1/|k|^2$, and that minimizers are collision-free above $α= 2-1/|k|$. Combining these results with the existence of precessing periodic orbits in between, we obtain a full classification of the minimizer in terms of $α$ and $k$, which we also verify numerically for $k=2$.

</details>


### [113] [Finiteness of Dziobek central configurations in the Newtonian five-body problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32751)
*Kefeng Liu,Yangshanshan Liu*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该文证明：对任意五个固定正质量，精确仿射维数为三的牛顿中心构型（Newtonian central configurations）仅有有限多个相似类（similarity classes）。证明基于 Dziobek 方程、两个常数恒等式及紧复曲线（compact complex curve）上零极点（zeros and poles）的局部分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 五体问题中三维中心构型的分类长期是未决问题，而维数受限情形下相似类是否有限，是理解其整体结构的基础性一步。

Method: 以 Dziobek 方程作为构型的代数刻画，引入两个常数恒等式（constant identities）约束解集，并在一条紧复曲线上对相关函数的零点与极点作局部解析分析。

Result: 对任意五个固定严格正质量，精确仿射维数为三的中心构型集合在相似意义下是有限的。

Conclusion: 将中心构型的有限性问题转化为紧复曲线上零极点的有限性论证，为五体问题中三维构型的计数与分类提供了有限性基础。

Abstract: We prove that, for any five fixed strictly positive masses, the Newtonian central configurations of exact affine dimension three form finitely many similarity classes. The proof uses the Dziobek equations, two constant identities, and local analysis of zeros and poles on a compact complex curve.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Strongly non-linear continuous model for wave propagation in 2D granular media: derivation and hyperbolicity analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31738)
*Sebastiano Cominelli,Régis Cottereau,Sergey Gavrilyuk,Bruno Lombard,Fabien Petitpas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该工作基于Hertz接触律与均匀化方法，建立了二维颗粒材料中波传播的非线性连续介质模型，并分析双曲性、求解沉降与波传播问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对二维颗粒材料中波传播缺乏有效连续介质描述的问题，借鉴Nesterenko的关键思想，试图在离散颗粒与连续模型之间建立桥梁。

Method: 由Hertz接触律（Hertzian contact law）出发、忽略接触摩擦，在球形单分散颗粒均匀化（homogenization）、局部周期性与Cauchy–Born假设下推导含位移一阶与二阶梯度的应变梯度（strain-gradient）模型；通过特征值与特征向量分析一阶系统双曲性（hyperbolicity），并采用显式有限体积法（finite-volume method）求解。

Result: 给出受限于特定变形域内的双曲性条件，推导纵波与横波波速（wave speed）的解析表达式并揭示其随深度的依赖性；模型预测与已有离散元模拟（discrete-element simulations）进行对比。

Conclusion: 该连续介质框架可推广至更复杂的堆积几何、接触律以及接触网络可演化的大变形（large-deformation）情形。

Abstract: In this work, we develop a nonlinear continuum model for wave propagation in two-dimensional granular materials, building on key ideas from Nesterenko's work. Starting from the Hertzian contact law and neglecting contact friction, we derive an effective continuum description through homogenization, assuming spherical, monodisperse grains, local periodicity, and the Cauchy--Born hypothesis. The resulting strain-gradient model depends on both the first and second gradients of the displacement field. We analyse the hyperbolicity of the leading-order system through its eigenvalues and eigenvectors, establishing conditions for hyperbolicity within a restricted deformation domain. The model is solved using an explicit finite-volume method to investigate gravitational settling and wave propagation. We derive analytical expressions for the longitudinal and transverse wave speeds and highlight their dependence on depth. Finally, the predictions of the continuum model are compared with existing discrete-element simulations. This formulation allows several extensions, including more complex packing geometries, contact laws, and large-deformation regimes in which the contact network can evolve.

</details>


### [115] [Quasideterminants and differential realizations of W-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31794)
*Sergey Derkachov,Daniil Getta*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究用于构造有限矩形W-代数（finite rectangular W-algebras）的哈密顿约化（Hamiltonian reduction），并将其作用于𝔤𝔩_{np}的大胞腔微分实现（big-cell differential realization）。核心结论是约化后的微分实现具有内禀因子化（intrinsic factorization），其Yangian作用等同于n个𝔤𝔩_p求值微分实现（evaluation differential realization）张量积上的余积作用，其中角准行列式（corner quasideterminants）是非交换机制的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈密顿约化是构造有限矩形W-代数的常用手段，但其对高秩微分实现的具体作用机制及约化结构与Yangian作用之间的关系尚需澄清。本文旨在揭示该约化过程的内在结构，解释高秩实现如何转化为低秩因子化形式。

Method: 对𝔤𝔩_{np}的大胞腔微分实现施加哈密顿约化，并借助角准行列式（corner quasideterminants）这一非交换工具，建立约化后微分实现的内禀因子化，进而将其Yangian作用识别为张量积上的余积（coproduct）作用。

Result: 约化后的微分实现具有内禀因子化结构，其Yangian作用与n个𝔤𝔩_p求值微分实现张量积上的余积作用一致。角准行列式被确认为实现该转化的必要非交换机制。

Conclusion: 哈密顿约化通过角准行列式将高秩微分实现转化为因子化的低秩Yangian作用，从而为有限矩形W-代数的构造提供了结构性的理解。

Abstract: In this work, we study the Hamiltonian reduction, used in constructing finite rectangular $W$-algebras, of the big-cell differential realization of $\mathfrak{gl}_{np}$. We establish an intrinsic factorization of the reduced differential realization, identifying its Yangian action with the coproduct action on a tensor product of $n$ evaluation differential realizations of $\mathfrak{gl}_p$. Corner quasideterminants provide the essential noncommutative mechanism through which Hamiltonian reduction transforms the higher-rank differential realization into a factorized lower-rank Yangian action.

</details>


### [116] [Spin Toda systems and N-point spherical Whittaker functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32149)
*Haoyi Li*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文借助连通分裂实李群（connected split real Lie group）的岩泽分解（Iwasawa decomposition），定义并研究了开Toda可积系统（open Toda integrable system）的自旋版本，并将球惠特克函数（spherical Whittaker functions）作为该自旋Toda系统的解加以研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅表明目标是定义并研究开Toda可积系统的自旋推广，并在该框架下处理球惠特克函数及其多点形式。

Method: 采用连通分裂实李群的岩泽分解定义自旋Toda系统；以球惠特克函数作为该系统的解；进一步构造其N点版本，并给出相应的卡西米尔元素（Casimir element）分解。

Result: 得到球惠特克函数作为自旋Toda系统解的性质，构造了N点版本的球惠特克函数，并发现卡西米尔元素的相应分解，由此导出渐近边界KZB型方程（asymptotic boundary KZB type equations）。

Conclusion: 开Toda可积系统的自旋推广在李群分解框架下是可行的，且通过卡西米尔元素分解可自然产生渐近边界KZB型方程，为相关可积结构与边界方程研究提供了新联系。

Abstract: We define and study a spin version of the open Toda integrable system, using the Iwasawa decomposition of a connected split real Lie group. We study spherical Whittaker functions as solutions of the spin Toda system. We also construct their N-point version and find the corresponding factorisation of the Casimir element, which yields asymptotic boundary KZB type equations.

</details>


### [117] [Averages of traces and ratios of characteristic polynomials of random unitary matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32236)
*Ada Bendel,Rhys Cahill,John Brian Conrey,Gregor Harrison Barraclough,Ayesha Irfan,Nina Claire Snaith,Leonid Tyulenev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文利用 Haar 测度下 U(N) 上特征多项式之比平均的比值定理（Ratios Theorem），系统评估酉矩阵迹的平均值，给出了最多 4 个迹的全部平均值表达式（对任意 N 成立），并计算了任意迹数平均中的零交换（zero-swap）与单交换（one-swap）贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索比值定理在计算酉矩阵迹的平均值方面的适用程度与效力，从而扩展对随机矩阵迹矩（trace moments）的解析刻画范围。

Method: 以 Haar 测度（Haar measure）下 U(N) 上特征多项式之比平均的比值定理为核心工具，推导迹平均的具体表达式，并分离出零交换与单交换贡献项。

Result: 得到最多涉及 4 个迹的所有平均值闭式表达，适用于任意矩阵维度 N；对任意迹数的平均给出零交换与单交换贡献，等价于在 Diaconis–Shahshahani 著名结果两倍范围内确定一般迹矩。

Conclusion: 所得表达式具有足够的可计算性以用于应用，例如可确定某些迹平均值取负值的参数范围。

Abstract: In this work we explore the extent to which the Ratios Theorem for averages over $U(N)$, with respect to Haar measure, of ratios of characteristic polynomials can be used to evaluate averages of traces of unitary matrices. We derive concrete expressions for all averages involving up to 4 traces, valid for any matrix size, $N$. We also calculate the so-called zero-swap and one-swap contributions to an average with any number of traces, which amounts to determining general trace moments in a range double the size of the renowned result of Diaconis and Shahshahani. The expression is tractable enough to use in applications, for example to determine the parameter range where certain trace averages assume negative values.

</details>


### [118] [On Trace Averages and Sharp Asymptotics for Powers of Haar-Unitary Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32570)
*Ayesha Irfan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 基于 Rains 的幂映射定理（power-map theorem），该文刻画了 Haar 分布矩阵 $U \in U(N)$ 的幂 $U^m$ 的谱在迹矩（trace moments）、Gaussian 与 Poisson 极限律中的精确分解结构，并给出有限 $N$ 修正与尖锐的全变差渐近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rains 的幂映射定理已给出 $U^m$ 谱作为独立低维 CUE 谱叠加的精确表示，但该精确分解如何在迹矩统计与极限律（Gaussian、Poisson）中体现尚不明确，尤其缺乏对有限 $N$ 修正与尖锐误差控制的定量刻画。

Method: 对混合迹乘积的平均，采用 Charlier–Ewens 卷积（Charlier–Ewens convolution）将稳定 Wick 项与其完整的有限 $N$ 修正分离；借助 Fredholm 分解（Fredholm factorisation）推导归一化迹的全变差（total-variation）渐近；对固定迹幂 $p$，对领先 Laguerre 修正做相干求和；并利用第二 Charlier 多项式分析局部特征值计数的修正项。

Result: 得到归一化迹在四种情形（从固定幂直至 $m \geq N$）下的尖锐全变差渐近；固定迹幂 $p$ 时相干求和给出精确因子 $e^{2/p}$；在平均间距尺度上，当 $N, m \rightarrow \infty$ 时展开谱在固定区间上服从尖锐 Poisson 极限，其一阶修正经第二 Charlier 多项式量化了局部特征值计数的残余欠离散（under-dispersion）。

Conclusion: Rains 的精确谱分解在迹矩与极限律层面具有可追踪的定量表现：Wick 项、有限 $N$ 修正、全变差误差与 Poisson 极限的一阶修正均可显式刻画，为理解高次幂矩阵谱的统计行为提供了尖锐的渐近框架。

Abstract: For a Haar-distributed matrix $U \in U(N)$, Rains's power-map theorem represents the spectrum of $U^m$, $m \in \mathbb{N}$, as a superposition of independent lower-dimensional CUE spectra. We determine how this exact decomposition appears in trace moments and in sharp Gaussian and Poisson limit laws. For averages of mixed trace products, a Charlier--Ewens convolution separates the stable Wick term from its full finite-$N$ correction. A Fredholm factorisation gives sharp total-variation asymptotics for the normalised trace in four regimes, from fixed powers to $m\geq N$. For a fixed trace power $p$, coherent summation of the leading Laguerre correction yields the exact factor $e^{2/p}$. At the mean-spacing scale, we obtain a sharp Poisson limit for the unfolded spectrum of $U^m$ on a fixed interval as $N, m \rightarrow \infty$; its first-order correction, obtained via the second Charlier polynomial, quantifies the residual under-dispersion of local eigenvalue counts.

</details>


### [119] [The solution of the Hopfield model with Gaussian patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32618)
*Francesco Camilli,Pierluigi Contucci,Emanuele Mingione*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文严格证明了具有高斯模式（Gaussian patterns）的Hopfield模型在所有载荷α与温度T下自由能的热力学极限存在，并给出显式变分原理表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hopfield模型的自由能在一般情况下是否具有严格的热力学极限尚缺乏完整证明，且Amit-Gutfreund-Sompolinsky（AGS）理论缺乏变分与严格基础。

Method: 通过Hubbard–Stratonovich变换将模型映射为N个Ising自旋与M=αN个高斯隐单元耦合，把隐层经验方差作为带熵成本的热力学变量；固定半径时模型化为高斯耦合的Ising–球面自旋玻璃并加以求解。

Result: 所给变分原理：(i)以复本对称限制形式重现AGS理论；(ii)确立T_g=1+√α为退火区（annealed region）的严格边界；(iii)在AGS方程中选出唯一自旋玻璃解；(iv)为半径给出状态方程；(v)证明T_g以下复本对称性（replica symmetry）处处破缺，其不稳定性归结为Sherrington–Kirkpatrick模型的零场de Almeida-Thouless条件。

Conclusion: 结果为高斯模式Hopfield模型提供了严格的热力学极限与变分刻画，并将复本对称破缺与SK模型的AT条件联系起来。

Abstract: We prove that the free energy of the Hopfield model with Gaussian patterns has a thermodynamic limit at every load $α$ and temperature $T$, and we give it as an explicit variational principle. The Hubbard--Stratonovich transformation maps the model onto $N$ Ising spins coupled to $M=αN$ Gaussian hidden units, whose radius, the empirical variance of the hidden layer, is a thermodynamic variable with an entropic cost. At fixed radius the model is an Ising--spherical spin glass with Gaussian couplings, which we solve. The principle (i) reproduces the Amit-Gutfreund-Sompolinsky theory as its replica-symmetric restriction, (ii) identifies $T_g=1+\sqrtα$ as the rigorous boundary of the annealed region, (iii) selects a unique spin-glass solution of the AGS equations, and (iv) gives the radius an equation of state. Moreover, (v) below $T_g$ we prove that replica symmetry is broken throughout: the instability of the AGS solution reduces to the zero-field de Almeida-Thouless condition of the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model.

</details>


### [120] [Correlation-induced suppression of Granger causality and Gaussian transfer entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32867)
*Louis Cattafesta,Scott T. M. Dawson,Ankit Srivastava*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究基于线性算子因果分析（Linear Operator Causality Analysis, LOCA）框架，推导预测性因果度量与算子因果响应之间的定量关系，指出格兰杰因果（Granger causality）因条件化而被抑制的程度等于源与接收者相关性的平方，并在高维系统、伯格斯平流链及静息态fMRI数据中加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格兰杰因果与传递熵（transfer entropy）在条件化后只度量源变量的独特预测贡献，易被与保留变量的相关性显著削弱，缺乏与系统传播算子所定义的因果响应之间的定量联系。

Method: 利用此前提出的LOCA框架，从系统传播子（propagator）测量因果响应；在二维线性系统中推导格兰杰因果的分数抑制与源—接收者相关系数平方的关系，并借助条件化后剩余的源协方差推广至高维系统，进一步给出高斯传递熵的上界；以线性化Burgers平流链、精确非线性声学Burgers解及HCP静息态fMRI数据（80脑区、8功能网络、10名被试）进行验证。

Result: 二维线性系统中格兰杰因果的分数抑制等于源与接收者相关性的平方；高维情形下额外条件化只会加剧抑制，并给出高斯传递熵的相应上界。线性化Burgers平流链在对流旅行时间峰值处出现36.6%的抑制；非线性Burgers精确解表明拟合替代惩罚由相关结构而非非线性主导，且在算子去偏后对传感器噪声保持稳健。HCP静息态fMRI中，仅条件化于接收者时单滞后格兰杰因果比算子响应低39.4%，条件化于接收者及全部其他保留变量时低73.0%。

Conclusion: 该框架在高维动力系统中为预测性因果度量与算子因果度量之间建立了定量桥梁，说明观测到的因果衰减主要源于相关结构而非非线性。

Abstract: In complex dynamical systems, Granger causality and transfer entropy are widely used to infer directional interactions, but they quantify a source's unique predictive contribution after conditioning and can therefore be substantially attenuated by correlation with the retained variables. Here, using our previously developed Linear Operator Causality Analysis (LOCA) framework, which measures causal response from the system propagator, we derive a relationship between predictive and operator-based causality. For a two-dimensional linear system, the fractional suppression of Granger causality equals the squared correlation between source and receiver. We generalize this result to high-dimensional systems using the source covariance remaining after conditioning and show that additional conditioning can only increase the suppression. The same covariance ordering yields a corresponding upper bound for Gaussian transfer entropy. A linearized Burgers advection chain exhibits 36.6% suppression at the convective travel-time peak, and exact nonlinear acoustic Burgers solutions show that the fitted-surrogate penalty is governed by correlation structure rather than nonlinearity and remains robust to sensor noise after operator debiasing. Applied to resting-state fMRI from the Human Connectome Project (80 brain regions, 8 functional networks, 10 subjects), the single-lag Granger causality measure falls 39.4% below the operator response when conditioning only on the receiver and 73.0% when conditioning on the receiver and all other retained variables. The framework provides a quantitative bridge between predictive and operator-based causal measures in high-dimensional dynamical systems.

</details>


### [121] [Variance of relative entropy over random states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32899)
*Linfeng Wei,Lu Wei*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将此前关于随机量子态平均相对熵（average relative entropy）的研究推广至涨落层面，推导了Hilbert–Schmidt与Bures–Hall系综中相对熵的方差（variance）精确表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究主要关注主要通用随机态（major generic random states）上平均相对熵的行为，但其围绕平均值的涨落（fluctuations）尚缺乏刻画，方差可作为量子态可区分性统计稳定性的判据。

Method: 结合与平均相对熵类似的矩阵层面计算（matrix-level calculations），并借助带区多项式（zonal polynomials）引入系综的特征值关联函数（eigenvalue correlation functions）以完成方差计算。

Result: 获得了取自同一系综的独立态之间、以及取自两个不同系综的态之间相对熵方差的精确结果（exact results），覆盖Hilbert–Schmidt与Bures–Hall两类系综。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在所考虑的极限情形（limiting regimes）下平均相对熵具有典型性（typicality），即涨落相对于均值可忽略。

Abstract: Average relative entropy over major generic random states is recently studied. In this work, the investigation is extended by deriving the variances of relative entropy over the Hilbert--Schmidt and Bures--Hall ensembles, which characterize its fluctuations around the average value. Exact results for the variances of relative entropy between independent states drawn from the same ensemble and from two different ensembles are obtained. These results further indicate that the average relative entropy is typical in the considered limiting regimes. In addition to the matrix-level calculations similar to the average relative entropy, eigenvalue correlation functions of the ensembles are involved in the variance computations through the use of zonal polynomials.

</details>


### [122] [Ideal interaction dynamics of defects and background fields in condensed matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32973)
*François Gay-Balmaz,Tudor S. Ratiu,Cesare Tronci*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个建模框架，用于缺陷密度（defect density）与背景场（background fields）的守恒耦合演化（conservative coupled evolution），并在量子流体与向列相液晶两个案例中进行应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝聚态系统中缺陷动力学（defect dynamics）虽有多种追踪缺陷位置的方法，但缺陷与背景场耦合演化的动力学模型构建仍具挑战性，而含运动缺陷系统的整体行为强烈受缺陷-场相互作用（defect-field interaction）影响。

Method: 将连续介质动力学的Euler-Poincaré变分框架（Euler-Poincaré variational setting）与拓扑缺陷的规范理论（gauge theory of topological defects）相结合，应用于Gross-Pitaevskii量子流体理论与Ericksen-Leslie向列相液晶模型两个案例。

Result: 规范理论有助于克服缺陷密度定义中的某些模糊性（ambiguities），Euler-Poincaré方法则在不存在缺陷重联（defect reconnection）的情形下提供统一的建模途径，从而实现缺陷密度与背景场的耦合演化。

Conclusion: 所提框架可在无缺陷重联条件下统一描述缺陷与背景场的守恒耦合演化，并以量子流体和向列相液晶为代表性案例加以说明。

Abstract: While defect dynamics in condensed matter systems is widely studied and several methods are available to track the defect position over time, the formulation of dynamical models for the coupled evolution of both defect and background field quantities seems to be more challenging. Yet, the overall behavior of a system containing moving defects is strongly influenced by this defect-field interaction. Here, we propose a modeling framework for the conservative coupled evolution of the defect density and the background fields upon focusing on two relevant cases of study: the Gross-Pitaevskii theory of quantum fluids and the Ericksen-Leslie model of nematic liquid crystals. To achieve the coupled evolution, we combine the Euler-Poincaré variational setting of continuum dynamics with the gauge theory of topological defects. While the latter allows us to overcome certain ambiguities in definition of the defect density, the Euler-Poincaré method provides a unified modeling approach in the absence of defect reconnection.

</details>


<div id='nucl-th'></div>

# nucl-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Timelike Compton scattering on a deuteron target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32741)
*Jing Han,Bo-Qiang Ma,Bernard Pire,Qin-Tao Song*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文研究氘核（deuteron）上的类时康普顿散射（timelike Compton scattering, TCS）作为探测氘核三维部分子结构的探针，给出了杰斐逊实验室（Jefferson Lab）相关运动学区域内的领头阶截面估计，并推导了次领头阶修正（特别是胶子横向度 GPD 贡献）的理论表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氘核内部结构仍需深入认识，且存在未解决的张量极化疑难（tensor-polarization puzzle）；广义部分子分布（generalized parton distributions, GPDs）可编码氘核的三维部分子结构，而 TCS 测量有望提供新视角并帮助澄清该疑难。

Method: 在强耦合 α_s 领头阶（leading-order）下对 TCS 截面做数值估计，氘核 GPD 采用卷积模型（convolution model）计算，Bethe-Heitler 贡献用氘核电磁形状因子的唯象参数化（phenomenological parametrization）处理；同时推导氘核 TCS 次领头阶（next-to-leading-order）修正的理论表达式，重点关注胶子横向度 GPD（gluon transversity GPDs）的贡献。

Result: 胶子横向度 GPD 的贡献可通过其在产生轻子对质心系（center-of-mass frame）中特征性的角依赖（angular dependence）被分离出来；数值结果表明氘核 TCS 在杰斐逊实验室具有作为互补探针的潜力。

Conclusion: 氘核 TCS 有望成为在杰斐逊实验室同时探测氘核夸克与胶子结构的互补探针（complementary probe）。

Abstract: We investigate timelike Compton scattering (TCS) on a deuteron target as a probe of the three-dimensional partonic structure of the deuteron encoded in generalized parton distributions (GPDs). Such measurements may provide new insight into the internal structure of the deuteron, and help to clarify the unresolved tensor-polarization puzzle. We present numerical estimates of the leading-order (in the strong coupling $α_s$) TCS cross section in the kinematic region relevant to future measurements at Jefferson Lab. The deuteron GPDs are evaluated within a convolution model, while the Bethe-Heitler contribution is calculated using a phenomenological parametrization of the deuteron electromagnetic form factors. In addition, we derive the theoretical expressions for the next-to-leading-order corrections to deuteron TCS, with particular emphasis on contributions from the gluon transversity GPDs. We show that these contributions can be isolated through their characteristic angular dependence in the center-of-mass frame of the produced lepton pair. Our results demonstrate the potential of deuteron TCS as a complementary probe of both the quark and gluon structure of the deuteron at Jefferson Lab.

</details>


### [124] [The renormalization of the shell-model neutrinoless double-beta decay operator starting from effective field theory at next-to-next-to-leading order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32947)
*S. L. Lyu,U. Carusone,G. De Gregorio,N. Itaco,L. Coraggio*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文延续此前在全自洽框架下基于手征微扰理论（chiral perturbation theory）推导的核哈密顿量与衰变算符，对48Ca、76Ge、82Se的无中微子双贝塔衰变（neutrinoless double-beta decay）矩阵元进行壳模型（shell-model）计算，重点评估手征展开中高阶项的贡献及理论不确定度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在评估手征展开截断与核模型有限自由度对无中微子双贝塔衰变核矩阵元（nuclear matrix elements）计算所带来的理论不确定度，从而厘清高阶算符项在中等质量候选核中的权重。

Method: 从直至次次领头阶（next-to-next-to-leading order）的两体流（two-body currents）构造有效壳模型衰变算符，并首次对实验关注的核引入在手征微扰论次次次领头阶（next-to-next-to-next-to-leading order）出现的三体流算符（three-body current operators）贡献。

Result: 给出了48Ca、76Ge、82Se中矩阵元对领先阶以上各项的敏感性评估，并联合核模型自由度有限性与算符手征展开截断两方面误差，对核矩阵元的理论不确定度进行了估计。

Conclusion: 高阶两体与三体流项对无中微子双贝塔衰变核矩阵元具有可评估的影响，将模型空间截断与手征展开截断的误差合并考虑可更可靠地量化理论不确定度。

Abstract: This paper is the continuation of a previous study, where shell-model calculations of the matrix elements for the neutrinoless double-beta decay were performed within a fully-consistent framework, starting from expressions of the nuclear Hamiltonian and decay operators which have been derived through chiral perturbation theory. The decays under our investigation are those involving 48Ca, 76Ge, and 82Se, as in our earlier analyses of electroweak nuclear decays with Hamiltonians and operators derived within effective field theory. In this work we focus on perturbative properties of the chiral expansion of the decay operator, namely in assessing the weight of terms beyond the leading order on the nuclear matrix elements of medium-mass candidates. To this end, we have constructed effective shell-model decay operators starting from two-body currents up to next-to-next-to-leading order and, for the first time, we introduce for nuclei of experimental interest contributions of the three-body current operators, which emerge at next-to-next-to-next-to-leading order of the chiral perturbation theory. Our investigation is aimed to evaluate the theoretical uncertainties affecting the calculated nuclear matrix elements of the neutrinoless double-beta decay, by conjugating the estimate of the inaccuracy related to the finiteness of the degrees of freedom of the nuclear model and the truncation of the chiral expansion of the decay operator.

</details>

### [125] [Equation of state of hot and dense QCD using the complex Langevin equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32385)
*Michael Mandl,Dénes Sexty,Daniel Unterhuber*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文给出高温、极大人子化学势（baryon chemical potential）条件下量子色动力学（QCD）状态方程（equation of state）的连续极限（continuum-extrapolated）格点结果，首次在物理π介子质量（pion mass）下执行复朗之万（complex Langevin）模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温、高重子化学势下的QCD状态方程存在符号问题（sign problem），传统格点方法难以直接处理，相关物态信息（如重子密度、压力等）因此缺乏第一性原理约束。

Method: 采用复朗之万模拟方法，在π介子质量的物理值下进行格点计算，并做连续极限外推；计算设定为奇异数中性（strangeness-neutral）、粲数中性（charm-neutral）与同位旋对称（isospin-symmetric）的框架。

Result: 报告了重子密度（baryon density）与压力随重子化学势变化的依赖关系，以及迹反常（trace anomaly）随温度与重子化学势变化的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 结果实现了在物理π介子质量下、于高温与迄今最大重子化学势范围内，对QCD状态方程进行连续极限格点计算的首次尝试，为强相互作用物质的热力学性质提供依据。

Abstract: We present continuum-extrapolated lattice results for the QCD equation of state at high temperatures and unprecedentedly large baryon chemical potentials. These results were obtained by performing the first complex Langevin simulations at the physical value of the pion mass. We work in a strangeness-neutral, charm-neutral, and isospin-symmetric setup and show results for the baryon density, pressure, and trace anomaly as a function of baryon chemical potential and temperature, respectively.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [FeynFacet: AI agents for analytic NLO QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31745)
*Zhong-Bo Kang,Werner Vogelsang,Congyue Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出 FeynFacet 工作流，由人工智能体（AI agents）在物理学家给定观测量与约定下开发并执解析次领头阶（NLO）QCD 计算程序，并据此推导单举横向自旋转移的新解析硬函数（hard functions）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析 NLO QCD 计算流程复杂，需在观测量与约定由物理学家指定的前提下高效完成；同时质子-质子与轻子-质子碰撞中单举横向自旋转移（transverse spin transfer）的解析硬函数此前尚属空白。

Method: 采用逆向幺正性（reverse unitarity）将实发射相空间积分中的在壳 delta 函数表示为割传播子（cut propagators）；以维数平移（dimensional shifts）把含消失分子（evanescent numerators）的积分化为更高维标量积分；再用分部积分恒等式（integration-by-parts identities）约化至主积分（master integrals）并解析求解。

Result: 给出覆盖所有部分子通道的质子-质子新结果，以及对散射轻子单举的轻子-质子新结果；并重现了非极化与双纵向极化散射、双横向极化质子-质子散射的既有硬函数，以及非极化夸克与胶子横向动量依赖部分子分布（TMD PDFs）的 NLO 匹配系数与相应软函数（soft functions）。

Conclusion: 新横向自旋转移系数为通过自旋依赖碎裂（fragmentation）提取夸克横向度（transversity）提供微扰输入，可用于极化超子产生、喷注中的 Collins 与双强子不对称性及自旋依赖一、两点能量关联子（energy correlators）；全部解析系数与计算程序已公开。

Abstract: We present FeynFacet, a workflow in which artificial intelligence agents develop and execute programs for analytic next-to-leading-order (NLO) QCD calculations, with the observables and conventions specified by physicists. We use it to derive new analytic hard functions for single-inclusive transverse spin transfer in proton-proton and lepton-proton collisions. The new proton-proton results cover all partonic channels contributing to transverse spin transfer, and the new lepton-proton result is inclusive in the scattered lepton. Within FeynFacet, we use reverse unitarity to represent the on-shell delta functions in real-emission phase-space integrals as cut propagators. Dimensional shifts convert integrals containing evanescent numerators into scalar integrals in higher dimensions. Integration-by-parts identities then reduce the scalar integrals to master integrals, which are evaluated analytically. Gauge invariance, pole cancellation and renormalization-group (RG) evolution provide checks throughout the calculation. We reproduce established single-inclusive hard functions for unpolarized and double longitudinally polarized proton-proton and lepton-proton scattering, and for proton-proton scattering with two transversely polarized beams. We also reproduce the NLO matching coefficients for unpolarized quark and gluon transverse-momentum-dependent parton distributions and the associated soft functions. The new transverse spin-transfer coefficients provide perturbative inputs for extracting quark transversity through spin-dependent fragmentation, including polarized-hyperon production, Collins and dihadron asymmetries in jets, and spin-dependent one- and two-point energy correlators. We make all analytic coefficients and the associated calculation programs publicly available online.

</details>


### [127] [Analytic two-loop corrections to top-quark pair production at hadron colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32543)
*Federico Buccioni,Christoph Nega,Benjamin Sauer,Lorenzo Tancredi,Fabian J. Wagner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文给出顶夸克对产生过程（夸克-反夸克湮灭与胶子聚变道）完整的两圈 QCD 振幅的解析形式，展开至 $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^2)$，保持对顶夸克质量与颜色数的完全依赖，并用 417 个主积分表示。首次发现与亏格二曲面相关的迭代积分在四维有限剩余项中存留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顶夸克对产生涉及椭圆曲线与亏格二超椭圆曲线，是检验超越多重对数（polylogarithms）的典范基（canonical basis）构造新方法的理想场所；此前尚缺乏保持完整质量与颜色依赖的两圈解析振幅。

Method: 在 $\varepsilon$-因子化（$\varepsilon$-factorised）形式下用微分方程（differential equations）计算 417 个主积分（master integrals），涵盖多条椭圆曲线及一条亏格二超椭圆曲线；并在阈值处提供高精度级数展开，结合微分方程数值解构建代码，用于在物理相空间任意点计算紫外重整化（UV-renormalised）且红外有限（infrared-finite）的剩余项。

Result: 获得两圈振幅的解析表达式（至 $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^2)$），以及阈值处的高精度级数展开与可评估任意相空间点的数值代码；据作者所知，这是首个亏格二曲面相关迭代积分在四维有限剩余项中存留的散射振幅（scattering amplitude）。

Conclusion: 顶夸克对产生为超越多重对数的典范基方法提供了检验平台，并首次揭示亏格二迭代积分在四维有限剩余中的贡献，其解析与数值结果的结合可用于物理相空间上的精确预言。

Abstract: We present the complete two-loop QCD amplitudes for the production of a top-quark pair in the quark--antiquark annihilation and gluon-fusion channels in analytic form up to order $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^2)$. Our analysis maintains full dependence on the top-quark mass and on the number of colours. The amplitudes are expressed in terms of 417 master integrals, which we compute by means of differential equations in $\varepsilon$-factorised form. Their calculation involves several elliptic curves and a hyperelliptic curve of genus two, which makes top-quark pair production an ideal arena for recent methods to construct canonical bases beyond polylogarithms. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first scattering amplitude in which iterated integrals related to a genus-two surface survive in the finite remainder in four space-time dimensions. We provide high-precision series expansions at the threshold and combine them with the numerical solution of the differential equations in a code that evaluates the UV-renormalised infrared-finite remainders at arbitrary points of the physical phase space.

</details>


### [128] [A universal probe of QCD spin physics from fragmentation energy correlator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32715)
*Qing-Hong Cao,Xiaohui Liu,Zhite Yu,C. -P. Yuan,Feng Yuan,Shu-Tao Zhang,Hua Xing Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出碎裂能量关联子（fragmentation energy correlator, FEC），将非微扰碎裂函数与红外安全能量关联子结合，作为探测QCD强子化三维动力学的新探针，并在轻子-轻子、轻子-强子、强子-强子碰撞的遍举强子产生中展示其普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探针难以直接刻画QCD强子化（hadronization）的三维动力学，且常依赖喷注算法（jet algorithms）或对其他重对象的联合测量；需要一种普适、红外安全且不依赖喷注定义的新观测量。

Method: 构造碎裂能量关联子（FEC），即将非微扰碎裂函数（fragmentation function）与红外安全能量关联子（energy correlator）相结合，并从理论上与数值上论证其在轻子-轻子、轻子-强子及强子-强子碰撞的遍举强子产生（inclusive hadron production）中的普适适用性。

Result: FEC无需喷注算法或其他重对象的同时测量即可适用；其具有非平凡的横自旋依赖（transverse-spin dependence），并随能量关联子标度（energy-correlator scale）变化，从而对QCD的手征对称性破缺标度（chiral-symmetry-breaking scale）提供灵敏探测。

Conclusion: FEC构成探测QCD强子化三维动力学的普适新探针，并可通过其标度依赖的横自旋效应约束手征对称性破缺标度。

Abstract: We study the fragmentation energy correlator (FEC), which combines the nonperturbative fragmentation function with the infrared-safe energy correlator, as a new probe of the three-dimensional dynamics of QCD hadronization. We demonstrate, both theoretically and numerically, that FEC applies universally to inclusive hadron production in lepton-lepton, lepton-hadron, and hadron-hadron collisions, without requiring jet algorithms or simultaneous measurements of other heavy objects. Through the nontrivial transverse-spin dependence, FEC provides a sensitive probe of the chiral-symmetry-breaking scale in QCD via its variation with the energy-correlator scale.

</details>


### [129] [Heavy Sterile Fermions from the Atmosphere: LZ Limits on Neutrino Up-Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32747)
*D. Aristizabal Sierra,V. De Romeri,D. K. Papoulias,I. M. Shoemaker*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文利用 LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验约 270 keV 核反冲能区、2.84 吨·年曝光量的数据，检验大气中微子通过矢量、轴矢量、标量与偶极相互作用将中微子上散射（up-scattering）为重的惰性费米子（sterile fermion）的可行性，并给出四类相互作用的新限制。结论是该机制无法令人信服地解释 248 keV 单事例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ 实验新公布的数据延伸至约 270 keV 核反冲能区，并在近本底无事件区出现一个 248 keV 单事例；需要检验该事例能否由中微子上散射为重的惰性费米子解释，同时评估暗物质探测器（dark matter detectors）在此类新物理场景下的探测潜力。

Method: 以大气中微子（atmospheric neutrinos）诱导的中微子上散射为框架，考虑矢量（vector）、轴矢量（axial-vector）、标量（scalar）与偶极（dipole）四类中介相互作用，对 LZ 的 2.84 吨·年曝光量数据进行拟合与限制分析；分别利用无超出（no excess）的数据区间设定上限，并检验 248 keV 事例与低反冲能区无事件的联合一致性。

Result: 在无超出的数据区间，四类场景均获得此前未被探测参数空间的新上限，表明暗物质探测器可对质量高于约 50 MeV 的惰性费米子给出有竞争力的中微子上散射限制；对 248 keV 事例，标量、矢量与轴矢量中介需耦合处于微扰性（perturbativity）边缘，偶极相互作用则拟合不佳。

Conclusion: 中微子上散射并非解释 248 keV 单事例的有力方案；但该分析显示暗物质直接探测实验可为重惰性费米子相关的中微子上散射提供与其它实验互补的限制。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has recently reported data extending to nuclear-recoil energies of about 270 keV, including a single event at 248 keV in a nearly background-free region, for an exposure of 2.84 tonne-years. We examine this dataset through neutrino up-scattering to a heavy sterile fermion induced by atmospheric neutrinos, mediated by vector, axial-vector, scalar and dipole interactions. Our main result is a set of constraints: using the part of the LZ data where no excess is observed, we obtain new bounds on all four scenarios that probe regions of parameter space not tested before, and show that dark matter detectors can provide competitive limits on neutrino up-scattering for sterile fermion masses above $\sim 50$ MeV. We also test whether the 248~keV event can be explained in this way while remaining consistent with the absence of events at lower recoil energies. For the scalar, vector and axial mediators this requires couplings at the edge of perturbativity, while the dipole does not provide a good fit, so we conclude that up-scattering is not a compelling explanation of the event.

</details>


### [130] [Robustness of the scaled transverse-momentum correlator and the contribution of meson--baryon fluctuations at RHIC energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32790)
*Rutik Manikandhan,Rene Bellwied*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用微观输运模型（UrQMD）验证标度双粒子横动量关联器（scaled two-particle transverse-momentum correlator）$C_{p_T}$ 对中心度估计量、跟踪效率与接受度等数据集差异的稳健性，并表明近期提出的"末态组分可解释非单调性"之贡献实际由介子-重子组分的事件逐事件涨落（event-by-event fluctuations）支配，采用 UrQMD 组分涨落后双组分计算中的极小值消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找量子色动力学临界点（QCD critical point）依赖事件逐事件涨落观测量中的非单调信号，而不同实验数据集在中心度估计量（centrality estimator）、跟踪效率（tracking efficiency）与接受度（acceptance）等条件下存在差异。此外，近期基于 UrQMD 产额的研究提出所测非单调性无需临界动力学即可由末态组分解释，需检验该论断。

Method: 采用微观输运模型 UrQMD，系统改变区分相关碰撞能量区数据集的实验条件（中心度估计量选择、有限跟踪效率、接受度，含对撞机与固定靶（fixed-target）运行模式差异），检验 $C_{p_T}$ 的稳健性；并从 UrQMD 提取事件逐事件的介子-重子组分涨落用于双组分（two-component）计算。

Result: $C_{p_T}$ 在上述条件变化下保持稳健；末态组分对非单调性的贡献由介子-重子组分的事件逐事件涨落而非平均产额主导；使用 UrQMD 的组分涨落后，双组分计算中的最小值（minimum）消失。

Conclusion: $C_{p_T}$ 可作为临界点搜索中不受数据集系统差异显著影响的稳健观测量；基于平均产额的"无临界动力学"解释不成立，组分涨落会抹去所讨论的非单调极小值特征。

Abstract: The search for the QCD critical point relies on identifying non-monotonic signatures in event-by-event fluctuation observables, such as the scaled two-particle transverse-momentum correlator $C_{p_T}$. Using a microscopic transport model (UrQMD), we establish the robustness of $C_{p_T}$ against the variations that distinguish the datasets in the relevant collision energy regime, namely the choice of centrality estimator, finite tracking efficiency, and acceptance, including the difference between collider and fixed-target running. In addition, it has recently been proposed, based on UrQMD yields, that the measured non-monotonicity requires no critical dynamics and instead follows from the event-by-event composition of the final state. We show that this contribution is governed by event-by-event fluctuations in the meson-baryon composition, rather than by the mean yields alone. Using the composition fluctuations obtained from UrQMD, we find that the minimum in the two-component calculation disappears.

</details>


### [131] [In situ enhanced detectability of axions' responses in a microwave cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31717)
*Li Gao,Suirong He,Hao Zheng,Ni Chen,Xianing Feng,Qingquan Jiang,Jin Li,L. F. Wei*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出通过预先激发目标腔模，将轴子—光子转换的二阶响应转化为一阶功率响应，使信号幅度与轴子—光子耦合参数（axion-photon coupling parameter）呈线性关系，并可借助平衡零拍探测（balanced homodyne detection）在相位随机的条件下检出信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强磁场中的微波腔（microwave cavity）是轴子暗物质（axion dark matter）探测的高灵敏手段，但其灵敏度根本上受限于轴子—光子能量转换的二阶本质，信号极其微弱。研究旨在突破这一固有限制。

Method: 通过预激发目标腔模，在原位产生一阶功率响应信号；采用标准正交平衡零拍探测（quadrature balanced homodyne detection）系统进行探测，并辅以数值模拟评估热噪声（thermal noise）抑制效果。

Result: 信号幅度随轴子—光子耦合参数线性标度，区别于传统方案的二次标度；预激发的种子场（seed field）可有效抑制热噪声，使系统探测灵敏度接近量子噪声极限（quantum noise limit），数值模拟结果表明可超越 KSVZ 与 DFSZ 模型所预测的灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该方案现有技术即可实现，有望为轴子暗物质搜寻带来重大跨越，提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Microwave cavities in strong magnetic fields are among the most sensitive detectors for axion dark matter detection. Its sensitivity, however, is fundamentally limited by the second order nature of axion photon energy conversion, which yields an extremely weak signal. Here we propose a feasible approach to break this limitation, by pre-exciting the target cavity mode to generate a first order power response signal in situ. The resulting signal amplitude scales linearly with the axion-photon coupling parameter, which is in sharp contrast to the quadratic scaling of conventional schemes, and is detectable via standard quadrature balanced homodyne detection system, even though its phase remains stochastic. Numerical simulations demonstrate that this pre-excited seed field can effectively suppress thermal noise, and thus the detection sensitivity of the system can approach the lower quantum noise limit. Consequently, the sensitivities predicted by the KSVZ and DFSZ models could be surpassed. This proposal might offer a pathway to a major leap in axion dark matter searches, as it is within reach of current technology.

</details>


### [132] [Soft scalar-field corrections to the topological susceptibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31756)
*Dietrich Bodeker,Rasmus S. K. Nielsen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文计算了高温 SU(2) 规范理论中软标量场涨落对拓扑磁化率（topological susceptibility）的贡献，并通过构造计入瞬子（instanton）存在的三维有效理论，得到当标量自耦合 λ 远大于规范耦合平方 g² 时，相对一圈（1-loop）结果约 √λ 量级的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温 SU(2) 规范理论中拓扑磁化率的已有计算多止于一遍环阶，软标量场涨落带来的高阶贡献及其在瞬子背景下的处理尚需明确。

Method: 构造标量场的三维有效理论（3-dimensional effective theory），并在该框架中显式考虑瞬子（instanton）的存在，以刻画软标量场涨落的影响。

Result: 在标量自耦合 λ 远大于规范耦合平方 g² 的极限下，相对于拓扑磁化率的一圈（1-loop）结果，得到 √λ 量级的修正。

Conclusion: 软标量场涨落对高温 SU(2) 规范理论的拓扑磁化率给出可观的 √λ 阶修正，说明在 λ≫g² 区域该效应不可忽略。

Abstract: We compute the contribution from soft scalar-field fluctuations to the topological susceptibility in SU(2) gauge theory at high temperature. We construct a 3-dimensional effective theory for the scalar field that accounts for the presence of an instanton. In the limit that the scalar selfcoupling $ λ$ is much larger than the square of the gauge coupling $g$ we obtain a correction of order $\sqrtλ$ relative to the 1-loop result for the topological susceptibility.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Bose-Einstein condensate in a shell: nonlinear modes and symmetry breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32249)
*Alexander Wolf,Vladimir V. Konotop,Maxim A. Efremov*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该研究在球形壳层中的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate）里考察球谐函数（spherical harmonics）的非线性推广模式，揭示曲面几何上非线性物质波（nonlinear matter waves）与平面系统的实质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限在球面上的量子粒子通常用球谐函数描述，而多体相互作用系统的平均场（mean-field）描述自然引出球谐函数的非线性推广；本文旨在分析这类非线性模式在同时具有排斥与吸引两体相互作用的壳形凝聚体中的涌现。

Method: 基于壳形玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体的平均场框架，分别考虑排斥与吸引的两体相互作用，对非线性模式进行分类求解，并借助对称性破缺分岔（symmetry-breaking bifurcation）分析识别无线性对应物的解。

Result: 发现两类非线性模式：第一类为单个球谐函数或其叠加在非零原子间相互作用下的解析延拓，其中部分解具有Townes孤子（Townes soliton）与Townes涡旋（Townes vortex）的平面对应物，在吸引相互作用下依然稳定，或可由三体相互作用稳定化；第二类源于排斥相互作用下的对称性破缺分岔，因而无线性对应。此外还证明了呼吸动力学（breathing dynamics）的存在，其主要在不稳定孤子与稳定涡旋项链态（vortex necklace state）之间振荡。

Conclusion: 曲面几何上的非线性物质波物理与平面系统存在显著差异，这些非常规波型（unconventional wave patterns）具备实验观测的可行性。

Abstract: The state of a quantum particle confined to a spherical surface is commonly described using spherical harmonics, which play a crucial role in solving many scientific problems featuring spherical symmetry. A mean-field description of a system of many interacting quantum particles confined to a spherical shell naturally leads to nonlinear generalizations of spherical harmonics. In this study, we analyze the emergence of such nonlinear modes in a shell-shaped Bose-Einstein condensate with both repulsive and attractive two-particle interactions. We find several classes of nonlinear modes. The first kind is an analytical continuation of a single spherical harmonic or their superposition for nonvanishing atom-atom interaction. Some of these solutions have planar counterparts in the form of Townes solitons and Townes vortices, which either appear stable even in the case of attractive interactions or can be stabilized by three-particle interactions. The second kind originate from symmetry-breaking bifurcations in the case of a repulsive interaction, and therefore have no linear counterparts. Moreover, we demonstrate the existence of a breathing dynamics that oscillates primarily between an unstable soliton and a stable vortex necklace state. Our findings show that the physics of nonlinear matter waves on curved geometries differs substantially from that in planar systems, suggesting the feasibility of observing these unconventional wave patterns experimentally.

</details>

### [134] [Neural LoFi with Backward Coupling: A Spectral Theory of Cross-Layer Feature Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32849)
*Yatin Dandi,Matteo Vilucchio,Hugo Tabanelli,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将逐层谱代理方法 Neural LoFi 扩展为含反向耦合（backward coupling）的版本，从精确反向传播梯度导出相邻层神经元的局部耦合动力学，揭示跨层相关算子的奇异模态放大，并在理论上证明反向耦合可恢复前向统计无法识别的低层方向，实验中在 CIFAR-10 二分类上优于前向 Neural LoFi。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合训练中反向传播（backpropagation）使后层重塑前层表征，而纯逐层学习（layer-wise learning）缺失这一反馈机制；已有的 Neural LoFi 作为逐层谱代理无法刻画这种跨层交互，因此需要扩展以捕捉该反馈。

Method: 从精确反向传播梯度出发，推导神经元输入权重与其下一层对应列之间的局部耦合；在小初始化（small-initialization）、平滑激活与固定环境的受控条件下，分析跨层相关算子的奇异模态放大，并据此提出反向耦合算法：上层敏感度生成样本相关的有效标签（effective labels）用于前层谱步骤，修正后的特征再精炼向上传播，同时给出函数空间变分原理与三层网络的自洽连续时间动力学。

Result: 在分层多索引模型（hierarchical multi-index model）中建立特征恢复分离：反向耦合可恢复前向标签加权统计（label-weighted statistic）不可见的低层方向；线性特例复现残差依赖的奇异模态动力学，配对模态为与 rainbow-network 图像一致的低秩协方差分量提供动力学机制；全连接与卷积网络在二分类 CIFAR-10 上于相同宽度下优于前向 Neural LoFi。

Conclusion: 反向耦合为逐层谱代理补充了跨层反馈，理论上解释了配对模态与低秩协方差结构的形成，并在实践中带来精度提升；该框架将前向特征学习理论推广至可描述后层对前层重塑的情形。

Abstract: During joint training, backpropagation allows later layers to reshape earlier representations, a feedback mechanism absent from purely layer-wise learning. We extend Neural Low-Degree Filtering (Neural LoFi), a recently proposed layer-wise spectral surrogate for feature learning, to capture this interaction. Starting from the exact backpropagation gradients, we derive a local coupling between a neuron's incoming weights and its corresponding next-layer column. In a controlled small-initialization regime with smooth activations and fixed surroundings, these dynamics amplify singular modes of a cross-layer correlation operator. Each growing mode pairs an earlier feature with an upper-layer response through shared neuron coefficients. This motivates a backward-coupling algorithm in which upper-layer sensitivities generate sample-dependent effective labels for earlier spectral steps, while corrected features refine upward transmission. A function-space variational principle characterizes the selected feature-response pairs by maximizing the label correlation of their product under kernel-norm constraints. For three-layer networks, we derive self-consistent continuous-time dynamics for the filtered hidden-layer features, the activation kernels, and the network output under the proposed backward coupling. In a hierarchical multi-index model, we establish a feature-recovery separation: backward coupling recovers lower-layer directions invisible to the original forward label-weighted statistic. The linear specialization recovers residual-dependent singular-mode dynamics, while paired modes provide a dynamical mechanism for linked low-rank covariance components consistent with the rainbow-network picture. Experiments with fully connected and convolutional networks on binary CIFAR-10 show improved accuracy over forward Neural LoFi at matched widths.

</details>
