### [135] [Phonon evidence for a partial, re-entrant spin reorientation near room temperature in LaFe$_{1-x}$Ni$_x$O$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31725)
*Subha Maity,Dushyant Singh,Vigneshwari S R,Tharundev V,Navneet Sharma,Subhavenkadesh SS,Krista R. Khiangte*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过变温磁化与拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）测量，发现无磁性稀土离子的LaFeO₃及Ni掺杂体系在室温附近存在部分再入型自旋重取向（partial re-entrant spin reorientation），并证明拉曼声子异常可作为磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy）变化的灵敏间接探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LaFeO₃缺乏磁性稀土离子，通常被认为在T_N≈740 K以下自旋方向保持沿a轴不变，因此室温附近自旋重取向（spin reorientation）在此类体系中是否可能尚无定论，需要实验与理论检验。

Method: 对LaFe₁₋ₓNiₓO₃（x=0与0.1）在200–400 K开展磁化与拉曼测量，结合主成分分析（principal component analysis）与全局拟合提取12个拉曼模式的温度响应，并构建基于朗道理论（Landau-based）的自旋-声子耦合（spin-phonon coupling）模型。

Result: 两种组分分别在285.6 K与323 K出现磁异常，伴随12个拉曼模式偏离常规非谐行为；模型比较支持约290 K附近的部分再入型Néel矢量旋转（Néel vector rotation）。Ni替代将重取向特征温区由约116 K缩窄至84 K，并使磁化异常增强约四倍；LaFeO₃在262 K附近的额外特征与微量α-Fe₂O₃的Morin转变（Morin transition）一致。

Conclusion: 部分自旋重取向可在无磁性稀土离子的正铁氧体（orthoferrites）中出现，且集体拉曼声子异常可作为探测磁各向异性变化的灵敏间接手段。

Abstract: Spin reorientation near room temperature offers a route to controlling antiferromagnetic order, yet LaFeO$3$, which lacks a magnetic rare-earth ion, is generally expected to retain its spin orientation along the $a$ axis below $T{\mathrm N}\approx740$~K. Here, we investigate LaFe$_{1-x}$Ni$_x$O$_3$ ($x=0$ and 0.1) using temperature-dependent magnetization and Raman spectroscopy over 200--400~K. Both compositions exhibit magnetic anomalies at 285.6 and 323~K, respectively, accompanied by correlated deviations of 12 Raman modes from their conventional anharmonic behavior. Principal component analysis and a global fitting approach reveal a common temperature-dependent phonon response, with mode-dependent amplitudes and signs. To interpret these anomalies, we develop a Landau-based spin-phonon coupling model describing complete, partial re-entrant, and incipient spin reorientation. Comparison with the experimental Raman profiles favors a partial, re-entrant rotation of the N'eel vector in both compositions, centered near 290~K. Ni substitution narrows the characteristic reorientation interval from approximately 116 to 84~K and enhances the magnetization anomaly by a factor of four. An additional feature near 262~K in LaFeO$_3$ is consistent with the Morin transition of trace $α$-Fe$_2$O$_3$. These findings suggest that partial spin reorientation can emerge without magnetic rare-earth ions and establish collective Raman phonon anomalies as a sensitive indirect probe of magnetic anisotropy changes in orthoferrites.

</details>


### [136] [Nonadiabatic vibrational dynamics of CO on Pt(111) studied with first-principles many-body perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31727)
*Raúl Bombín,Iñaki Fernández Tena,J. Iñaki Juaristi,Maite Alducin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用多体微扰理论结合密度泛函理论的第一性原理框架，研究CO/Pt(111)的非平衡振动动力学，并首次评估了电子介导声子-声子耦合的顶点修正屏蔽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CO吸附于Pt(111)是超快振动光谱学的典型吸附物-表面体系，其非平衡振动动力学需要显式包含电子-空穴对激发与电子介导声子-声子耦合的理论描述。

Method: 结合多体微扰理论（many-body perturbation theory）与密度泛函理论（density functional theory）的第一性原理框架，显式计入电子-空穴对激发（electron-hole pair excitations）和电子介导声子-声子耦合（electron-mediated phonon-phonon coupling），并对后者评估顶点修正屏蔽（vertex-correction screening），此为方法学创新。

Result: 计算定性再现了飞秒泵浦-探测实验（femtosecond pump-probe experiments）中内伸缩振动模（internal stretch vibrational mode）的瞬态演化，并据此在实验测量中识别出飞秒激光激发后的小幅瞬态蓝移（transient blueshift）。

Conclusion: 与前人工作预测一致，第10族过渡金属表面电子温度升高时可用于电子-声子耦合的电子态数目减少，有效屏蔽电子-声子相互作用，导致反常的瞬态蓝移。

Abstract: We investigate the nonequilibrium vibrational dynamics of CO adsorbed on Pt(111), a prototypical adsorbate-surface system for ultrafast vibrational spectroscopy, using a first-principles framework that combines many-body perturbation theory with density functional theory. Within this methodology we explicitly account for electron-hole pair excitations and electron-mediated phonon-phonon coupling. For the latter mechanism, we evaluate the vertex-correction screening, what constitutes a methodological novelty. Our calculations qualitatively reproduce the transient evolution of the internal stretch vibrational mode observed in femtosecond pump-probe experiments, allowing us to identify in the experimental measurements a small transient blueshift after excitation with a femtosecond laser pulse. As predicted in a previous work, the reduction in the number of electronic states available for electron-phonon coupling when the electronic temperature raises at a group 10 transition metal surface, effectively screens the electron-phonon interaction giving rise to an anomalous transient blue-shift.

</details>


### [137] [Altermagnetism in Folded Iron Chain Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31732)
*Ling-Fang Lin,Yang Zhang,Satoshi Okamoto,Adriana Moreo,Lun-Hui Hu,Elbio Dagotto*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出并验证“折叠铁链堆叠”机制可在块状磁序（block-type magnetic order，如↑↑↓↓）中实现交错磁（altermagnetism）；第一性原理计算表明K₃Fe₂Se₄家族即为该机制的候选平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁此前主要在Néel型反铁磁体中被实现，实现途径单一；作者希望拓展其磁序载体范围，寻找非Néel型、块状磁序中的交错磁，并探索其对称性条件。

Method: 从对称性分析入手，指出严格一维情形下交错磁被[C₂||𝒯]对称性禁止，但将链堆叠为准一维阵列时可出现；结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）筛选候选材料，识别出K₃Fe₂Se₄家族。

Result: K₃Fe₂Se₄家族即使缺少晶格C₄对称性，仍可承载d波交错磁体；由于对称性较低，块状折叠链呈现各向同性的交错磁自旋劈裂（isotropic altermagnetic spin splitting），并可能表现为交错磁缠绕效应（altermagnetic wrapping effect）。

Conclusion: 该工作将实空间块状磁性（real-space block magnetism）与倒空间交错磁性（reciprocal-space altermagnetism）统一起来，确立折叠铁链为可调零净磁矩自旋电子学（zero-moment spintronics）平台。

Abstract: Altermagnetism, a moment-compensated magnetism with momentum-dependent spin splitting, has so far been realized primarily in Néel-type antiferromagnets. Here, we demonstrate altermagnetism in folded iron-chain systems with block-type magnetic order (e.g., $\uparrow \uparrow \downarrow \downarrow$). In the strictly one-dimensional (1D) case, altermagnetism is forbidden by the $[C_2||{\cal T}]$ symmetry, but it can emerge when such chains are stacked into a quasi-1D array. To generate altermagnetic spin splitting via this chain-stacking mechanism, two essential ingredients are required: a block-type spin order that spontaneously breaks the combined parity-time reversal symmetry, and finite inter-chain hopping. Using first-principles calculations, we identify the K$_3$Fe$_2$Se$_4$ family as a promising platform that realizes this mechanism, hosting a $d$-wave altermagnet even without lattice $C_{4}$ symmetry. Moreover, owing to its lower symmetry, the block-type folded chain exhibits an isotropic altermagnetic spin splitting, which may manifest as an altermagnetic wrapping effect. Our findings unify real-space block magnetism and reciprocal-space altermagnetism, establishing folded iron chains as a tunable platform for zero-moment spintronic applications.

</details>


### [138] [Thermoelectric properties and modulation doping of ultrawide-bandgap nitride monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31777)
*Ariel R. Barr*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合第一性原理（first-principles）电子结构计算与半经典输运（semiclassical transport）方法，系统评估有序单层 B\(_{x}\)Al\(_{1-x}\)N 与 B\(_{x}\)Ga\(_{1-x}\)N 的热电性能，并给出本征击穿场估算与调制掺杂候选体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维 III 族氮化物合金（two-dimensional III-nitride alloys）具备组分可调特性，可作为研究热电输运与内在电场耐受性的平台，但其热电优值与击穿特性尚缺乏系统量化。

Method: 采用第一性原理电子结构计算结合半经典输运模型，动量弛豫时间（momentum relaxation times）由 AMSET 按态计算，包含声学形变势（acoustic deformation-potential）与压电散射（piezoelectric scattering）；晶格热导率取与温度无关的 550 W/mK；另用基于带隙与最高声子频率的唯象模型（phenomenological model）估算击穿场，并借助真空参考能带对齐（vacuum-referenced band alignments）筛选掺杂伙伴。

Result: 所选 p 型与 n 型单层预测最大热电优值 ZT>2.5，n 型 B\(_{0.25}\)Ga\(_{0.75}\)N 在 300 K 下 ZT>2；hGaN/V\(_2\)O\(_5\) 显式计算给出约 1.6×10\(^{14}\) cm\(^{-2}\) 的空穴面密度；含 Al 与含 Ga 的 x=0.75 合金本征击穿场分别为 20.06 与 20.74 MV/cm。

Conclusion: 结果支持二维 III 族氮化物合金在热电器件与高场功率电子（high-field power electronics）方向的进一步研究，但 ZT 数值依赖于所选散射与晶格热导率模型，击穿场估算仍需针对单层体系验证且未区分场方向。

Abstract: Two-dimensional III-nitride alloys provide a composition-tunable platform for exploring thermoelectric transport and intrinsic electric-field tolerance. We combine first-principles electronic-structure calculations with semiclassical transport for ordered monolayer B$_{x}$Al$_{1-x}$N and B$_{x}$Ga$_{1-x}$N. State-dependent momentum relaxation times computed with AMSET include acoustic deformation-potential and piezoelectric scattering. Using a common, temperature-independent lattice thermal conductivity of 550 W/mK, we predict maximum thermoelectric figure of merit ZT>2.5 for selected p- and n-type monolayers and ZT>2 at 300~K for n-type B$_{0.25}$Ga$_{0.75}$N. Composition-dependent band curvature, screening, and scattering help interpret the different p- and n-type responses. Vacuum-referenced band alignments identify candidate modulation-doping partners for both carrier types; explicit hGaN/V$_2$O$_5$ calculations yield a hole surface density of approximately 1.6$\times10^{14}$ cm$^{-2}$. A separate phenomenological model using the band gap and maximum phonon frequency predicts intrinsic breakdown fields of 20.06 and 20.74 MVcm for the Al- and Ga-containing x=0.75 alloys, respectively. These results motivate further investigation for thermoelectric and high-field power-electronic applications. The ZT values apply to the specified scattering and lattice-conductivity models, while the breakdown estimates require validation for monolayers and do not resolve field direction.

</details>


### [139] [Unifying Low-Frequency Raman and Infrared Signatures across Perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31830)
*Ethan Berger,Erik Fransson,Apinya Ngoipala,Lucas Svensson,Priyanka Deswal,Mehmet Baskurt,Paul Erhart,Julia Wiktor*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用机器学习分子动力学统一模拟氧化物、卤化物及双卤化物钙钛矿的红外与拉曼光谱，揭示低频谱特征与晶格不稳定性的对称性、耦合及动力学的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿材料普遍存在由极性偏心与八面体倾转等结构不稳定性引起的强晶格非谐性，但低频红外与拉曼特征通常被分开研究，缺乏跨钙钛矿类别的统一理解。

Method: 采用基于机器学习（machine learning）的分子动力学（molecular dynamics）方法，模拟氧化物钙钛矿（SrTiO$_3$、BaTiO$_3$）、卤化物钙钛矿（CsPbBr$_3$、CsSnBr$_3$、CsGeBr$_3$）及双卤化物钙钛矿（Cs$_2$AgAlBr$_6$、Cs$_2$AgBiBr$_6$）的红外（IR）与拉曼（Raman）光谱，并比较极性、倾转驱动及混合不稳定性材料。

Result: 极性偏心（polar off-centering）增强低频红外与介电响应，而非极性模式（尤其八面体倾转）对应拉曼中心峰（central peak）；低频特征强度取决于模式与极化或介电易感性的耦合强弱；慢速极性重取向使介电响应移向极低频，过阻尼倾转模式产生持续拉曼中心峰。

Conclusion: 研究建立了将低频红外与拉曼光谱同其底层非谐晶格不稳定性的对称性、耦合与动力学相联系的统一框架（unified framework）。

Abstract: Perovskites have been studied for a wide range of applications, from oxide-based ferroelectrics to halide-perovskite photovoltaics. Despite their diverse functionalities, these materials share strong lattice anharmonicity arising from structural instabilities such as polar off-centering and octahedral tilting. These anharmonic distortions strongly influence the low-frequency vibrational response and can be observed in infrared (IR) and Raman spectra. However, low-frequency IR and Raman signatures are typically investigated separately, hindering the development of a unified understanding of vibrational spectroscopy across perovskite classes. In this work, we use machine-learning-based molecular dynamics to simulate the IR and Raman spectra of oxide perovskites (SrTiO$_3$ and BaTiO$_3$), halide perovskites (CsPbBr$_3$, CsSnBr$_3$, and CsGeBr$_3$) and double halide perovskites (Cs$_2$AgAlBr$_6$ and Cs$_2$AgBiBr$_6$). By comparing materials exhibiting polar, tilt-driven and mixed instabilities, we find that polar off-centering leads to enhanced low-frequency IR and dielectric responses, whereas nonpolar modes, particularly octahedral tilting, are associated with Raman central peaks. The strength of the low-frequency features also depends on how strongly the underlying modes couple to polarization or dielectric susceptibility. Finally, we show that the dynamics of these instabilities play an important role, with slow polar reorientations shifting the dielectric response to very low frequencies and overdamped tilting modes producing persistent Raman central peaks. These results establish a unified framework connecting low-frequency IR and Raman spectra to the symmetry, coupling and dynamics of their underlying anharmonic lattice instabilities.

</details>


### [140] [Strontium Titanate Capping Layers as Kinetic Regulators of Redox Phase Transitions in Oxide Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31871)
*Pooya Abdollahi,Woo Jin Kim,Keaghan Knight,Harold Y. Hwang,Ambrose Seo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 少纳米厚 SrTiO₃（STO）覆盖层（capping layer）通过对氧扩散的限制，成为底层材料氧化/还原驱动相变的速率控制因素，其厚度与氧分压可调控相变温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧化物覆盖层广泛用于保护与稳定薄膜异质结构（thin-film heterostructures），但其在相变动力学（phase-transition kinetics）中的作用尚缺乏研究。

Method: 采用实时原位光学椭偏光谱（real-time in situ optical spectroscopic ellipsometry）观测覆盖层下的氧化与还原过程，并进行定量动力学分析。

Result: 氧穿过纳米级覆盖层的扩散为限速步骤（rate-limiting step），导致扩散控制（diffusion-controlled）的相变；覆盖层厚度与氧分压均可调节相变温度，拟合得到氧在 STO 中扩散的活化能（activation energy）E_A = 0.64 ± 0.06 eV。

Conclusion: 氧化物覆盖层可作为氧化还原驱动相变的主动动力学调节器（active kinetic regulators），为复杂氧化物异质结构中氧化还原动力学的工程设计提供通用框架。

Abstract: Oxide capping layers are widely used to protect and stabilize thin-film heterostructures, yet their role in governing phase-transition kinetics remains largely unexplored. Here, we uncover the kinetic function of few-nanometer-thick SrTiO$_3$ (STO) capping layers in controlling oxidation and reduction processes, using real-time in situ optical spectroscopic ellipsometry. We show that oxygen diffusion through the nanoscale capping layer constitutes the rate-limiting step, giving rise to diffusion-controlled phase transitions in the underlying materials. As a result, both capping-layer thickness and oxygen partial pressure provide effective handles to tune phase transition temperatures. Quantitative analysis yields an activation energy of $E_A$ = 0.64 $\pm$0.06 eV for oxygen diffusion through STO, highlighting its suitability for precise control of redox reactions in diverse material systems. These results establish oxide capping layers as active kinetic regulators of redox-driven phase transitions and offer a general framework for engineering redox dynamics in complex oxide heterostructures.

</details>


### [141] [Sub-monolayer tracking of molecular states at buried interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31924)
*Harlan Heilman,Em Biegler,Sushil Adhikari,Victor Murcia,Tanner Melody,Fred Woodall,Lars Thomsen,Obaid Alqahtani,Brian A. Collins*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习优化的光学模型分析偏振共振软X射线散射（polarized resonant soft X-ray scattering, RSoXS），实现对分子薄膜深度方向上分子取向、化学身份与堆叠状态的定量解析，分辨率达亚单层水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴技术相关现象源于埋藏界面（buried interfaces）处的新颖分子态，但多数情况下难以定量表征；共振散射虽对局域态敏感，却因缺乏精确解释模型而受限。

Method: 采用机器学习（machine learning）优化的光学模型（optical model）分析分子薄膜的偏振共振软X射线散射数据，通过锁定能量与偏振在光学模型中突破衍射极限（diffraction limit）。

Result: 该分析实现亚单层（sub-monolayer）分辨率的深度分辨定量解析；发现自界面起始的分子取向与堆叠梯度演化（gradient evolution），表明表面与体相生长动力学（growth dynamics）之间存在竞争，并与基底图案化（substrate patterning）假说相矛盾。

Conclusion: 该无损（nondestructive）方法为理解埋藏界面的形成与最终状态提供了新途径，有望用于调控下一代技术中的界面态与相关现象。

Abstract: Phenomena responsible for emerging technologies originate from novel molecular states at buried interfaces. Yet quantitatively resolving these features is out of reach in most cases. Resonant scattering has demonstrated sensitivity to these local states but is limited due to a lack of accurate interpretive models. We leverage a machine learning (ML)-optimized optical model to analyze polarized resonant soft X-ray scattering (RSoXS) from molecular thin films. This new analysis establishes the direct and quantitative interpretation of molecular orientation, chemical identity and packing states as a function of depth with sub-monolayer resolution - reaching beyond the diffraction limit via locking energy and polarization through the optical model. From our analysis, we discover a gradient evolution of molecular orientation and packing emerging from the interface, indicative of competing surface versus bulk growth dynamics and contradicting hypotheses of substrate patterning. This nondestructive insight into buried interfacial formation and final state opens possibilities in controlling interfacial states and phenomena in next-generation technologies.

</details>


### [142] [Poly(vinylidene fluoride) under finite electric displacement fields: a removable $α\!\to\!δ$ reorientation barrier and a persistent first-order $δ\!\to\!β$ conformational change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31926)
*Shaohui Qiu,Jacob C. Dean,Rajagopalan Thiruvengadathan,Ramanujam Brahmadesam Thoopul Srinivasa Raghava*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于第一性原理计算，在固定电位移边界条件下给出PVDF中α、δ、β三相的零温自由能排序与相变阈值，并解释电场极化与机械拉伸各自对应不同相变的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PVDF的α、δ、β相虽可由电极化（electrical poling）与机械拉伸（mechanical drawing）相互转化，但在受控电学边界条件下其热力学稳定性尚未从第一性原理建立。

Method: 采用范德瓦尔斯密度泛函（van der Waals density-functional）计算，在共同的24原子原胞中沿两个约束坐标考察固定电位移D下的零温自由能：gauche C–C键的倾斜（α→δ，晶胞变化<1%），以及使TG⁺TG⁻链伸直为全反式β的骨架扭转（链重复应变10.6%）；并辅以Γ点谐振动估计与路径有效电荷分析。

Result: 随D由0增至0.30 C·m⁻²，最低均匀自由能依次在D_c1=0.046 C·m⁻²由α转为δ、在D_c2=0.157 C·m⁻²由δ转为β；δ多畴态在每个D下均低于均匀α约14.3 meV/原胞，但振动估计显示室温下该排序反转。δ与β在E*=0.69 GV·m⁻¹下于0.11<D<0.21 C·m⁻²共存；倾斜势垒自0.66 eV降至17 meV（低于300 K的k_BT），扭转势垒仍为0.66 eV（D_c2）与0.34 eV（D=0.30 C·m⁻²），故δ→β保持第一类。

Conclusion: 势垒差异主要源于路径有效电荷（倾斜0.28e，扭转0.18e）；该能量面解释了电场生成δ、机械拉伸获得β的现象，并提示提高扭转路径有效电荷有望仅凭电场得到β相。

Abstract: The $α$, $δ$, and $β$ phases of PVDF are interconverted by electrical poling and mechanical drawing, yet the thermodynamics under a controlled electrical boundary condition has not been established from first principles. We evaluate the zero-temperature free energy at fixed electric displacement $D$ from van der Waals density-functional calculations along two constrained coordinates in a common 24-atom cell: a tilt of the gauche C--C bonds that maps antipolar $α$ onto polar $δ$ (cell change $<1\%$), and a backbone torsion that straightens TG$^+$TG$^-$ chains into all-trans $β$ (chain-repeat strain $10.6\%$). As $D$ increases from 0 to $0.30$~C\,m$^{-2}$, the lowest homogeneous free energy switches from $α$ to $δ$ at $D_{c1}=0.046$~C\,m$^{-2}$ and from $δ$ to $β$ at $D_{c2}=0.157$~C\,m$^{-2}$. At $T=0$ with domain-wall energy neglected, a $δ$ multidomain state lies $14.3$~meV per cell below homogeneous $α$ at every $D$. A $Γ$-point harmonic estimate suggests that vibrations reverse this ordering at room temperature. $δ$ and $β$ coexist for $0.11<D<0.21$~C\,m$^{-2}$ at $E^*=0.69$~GV\,m$^{-1}$, independent of the screening constant. The tilt barrier falls from $0.66$~eV per cell at $D=0$ to $17$~meV at $D=0.30$~C\,m$^{-2}$ (below $k_BT$ at $300$~K), while the torsion barrier remains $0.66$~eV at $D_{c2}$ and $0.34$~eV at $D=0.30$~C\,m$^{-2}$, so $δ\toβ$ stays first order. Much of the difference traces to the path effective charge ($0.28e$ for the tilt, $0.18e$ for the torsion). Scaling shows the tilt barrier would survive with the torsion charge; the smaller torsion charge accounts for about half the barrier it retains. The landscape accounts for why a field produces $δ$ and why $β$ is reached by drawing, and suggests that raising that charge could let a field alone produce $β$.

</details>


### [143] [Structural characterization of the rutile-GeO2/TiO2 (001) heterointerface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31934)
*Imteaz Rahaman,Martha R. McCartney,David J. Smith,Kai Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用高分辨透射电子显微镜（HR-TEM）比较了在840°C与925°C下于晶格高度匹配的金红石TiO2衬底上生长的GeO2薄膜异质界面，发现界面结构随温度截然不同：非晶GeOx/r-TiO2界面与全外延r-GeO2/r-TiO2界面，后者通过Lomer型或60°型界面位错缓解约4%的晶格失配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金红石GeO2（r-GeO2）作为超宽禁带（UWBG）半导体在高功率器件中具有潜力，但其异质界面的形成机制与应变弛豫行为尚不清楚，需深入理解以优化外延生长。

Method: 采用金属有机化学气相沉积（MOCVD）在晶格匹配的金红石TiO2（r-TiO2）衬底上于840°C和925°C生长GeO2薄膜，并用高分辨透射电子显微镜（HR-TEM）截面观察结合结构与化学分析进行界面表征。

Result: 观察到两类界面：非晶GeOx/r-TiO2界面呈突变、高度刻面化且非平面；全外延r-GeO2/r-TiO2界面呈波动但常刻面的形貌，含高密度应变弛豫位错（主要为Lomer型或60°型）以容纳约4%晶格失配，界面互混有限。刻面与金红石{101}面相关，特征角约114°，支持热驱动TiO2(001)衬底刻面化而非外延导致的重构。

Conclusion: 研究揭示r-GeO2/r-TiO2异质结构中界面形成与应变弛豫机制，表明进一步优化生长条件有望改善r-GeO2外延生长，服务于未来超宽禁带电子器件应用。

Abstract: Rutile-GeO2 (r-GeO2) has emerged as a promising ultrawide-bandgap (UWBG) semiconductor for future high-power device applications because of its favorable combination of physical and electrical properties. This study has used high-resolution transmission electron microscopy (HR-TEM) to compare heterointerfaces between thin films of GeO2 grown by metal-organic chemical vapor deposition on closely-lattice-matched rutile TiO2 (r-TiO2) substrates at temperatures of 840° C and 925° C. Cross-sectional TEM observations reveal interfaces with two entirely different structures: an amorphous GeOx/r-TiO2 heterostructure with an abrupt, highly faceted and non-planar interface, or a fully epitaxial r-GeO2/r-TiO2 heterostructure characterized by a more wavy but often facetted interface. Structural analysis of the latter sample revealed a high density of strain-relieving interfacial dislocations, identified primarily as Lomer-type or 60°-type, that accommodated the ~4% lattice mismatch between the two materials, while chemical analysis showed very limited interfacial intermixing. Crystallographic analysis showed that the interface faceting was associated with rutile {101} facets, with characteristic facet angles of ~114°; these observations support thermally driven TiO2 (001) substrate faceting rather than an interface reconstruction resulting from GeO2 epitaxy. Overall, these findings provide useful insights into the mechanisms governing interface formation and strain relaxation in r-GeO2/r-TiO2 heterostructures and suggest that further refinement of growth conditions should enable improved epitaxial growth of r-GeO2 for future ultrawide-bandgap electronic device applications.

</details>


### [144] [Nanoscale origin of thermal anisotropy in monoclinic \texorpdfstring{$β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$}{beta-Ga2O3}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31976)
*Kai Xu,Shuo Zhao,Luca Sung-Min Choi,Nils Bernhardt,Pouria Emtenani,Felix Nippert,Moritz Meißner,Hans Tornatzky,Oliver Bierwagen,Piero Mazzolini,Riccardo Mincigrucci,Laura Foglia,Danny Fainozzi,Filippo Bencivenga,Zbigniew Galazka,George Fytas,Bartlomiej Graczykowski,Riccardo Rurali,Matthias Scheffler,Christian Carbogno,Juan Sebastian Reparaz,Markus R. Wagner*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合热/声学光谱与第一性原理计算，揭示β-Ga₂O₃热导率各向异性（thermal anisotropy）源于高频、短平均自由程（MFP）声子，并在28 nm周期下观察到近两倍于宏观值的面内各向异性增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单斜β-Ga₂O₃是超宽禁带（ultra-wide-bandgap）功率电子候选材料，但其热导率低且各向异性（anisotropic），限制散热，故成为纳米尺度热输运研究的理想模型体系；然而其各向异性的声子起源与尺度依赖性尚不明确。

Method: 采用各向异性热反射热测量（anisotropic thermoreflectance thermometry）通过(100)、(010)、(001)晶面内的角度测量确定完整热导率张量；结合角度分辨布里渊光散射（Brillouin light scattering）、频率分辨从头算Green–Kubo计算，以及极紫外瞬态热光栅光谱（extreme-ultraviolet transient thermal grating spectroscopy）探测周期低至28 nm的尺度效应。

Result: 低频声学声子速度各向异性弱，不足以解释测得的热各向异性；仅当累积电导率包含4 THz以上模式时才出现体态各向异性图样，而3 THz以下声学贡献近乎各向同性。光栅周期降至28 nm时面内各向异性显著增强，在(001)面达宏观值近两倍；重构与计算的MFP累积函数将该增强归因于短MFP声子更强的方向对比度。

Conclusion: 高频、短MFP声子是β-Ga₂O₃热各向异性的主导来源，而低频声子在宏观尺度上削弱方向对比度，从而将纳米尺度各向异性增强与声子MFP分布联系起来。

Abstract: Monoclinic $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ is a promising ultra-wide-bandgap semiconductor for power electronics, but its low and anisotropic thermal conductivity limits heat removal, making it a compelling model system for nanoscale thermal transport studies. Here, we demonstrate a pronounced nanoscale enhancement of thermal anisotropy and uncover the underlying phonon mechanism by combining complementary thermal and acoustic spectroscopy techniques with first-principles calculations. Anisotropic thermoreflectance thermometry determines the full thermal-conductivity tensor from angular measurements within the (100), (010), and (001) crystallographic planes. Angle-resolved Brillouin light scattering reveals that low-frequency acoustic phonons exhibit only weak velocity anisotropy, insufficient to account for the measured thermal anisotropy. Frequency-resolved ab initio Green--Kubo calculations show that the bulk anisotropy pattern emerges only when the accumulated conductivity includes phonon modes above 4 THz, whereas the sub 3 THz acoustic contributions are nearly isotropic. Extreme-ultraviolet transient thermal grating spectroscopy reveals that reducing the grating period to 28 nm strongly enhances the in-plane thermal anisotropy, most markedly in the (001) plane, where it reaches nearly twice its macroscopic value. Reconstructed and calculated mean-free-path (MFP) accumulation functions link this enhancement to the stronger directional contrast of short-MFP phonons. Together, these results identify high-frequency, short-MFP phonons as the dominant origin of thermal anisotropy in $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$, while low-frequency phonons attenuate the directional contrast at macroscopic scales.

</details>


### [145] [Interface Control of Second-Harmonic Generation in Layered CuInP2S6 via PbZr0.2Ti0.8O3 Domain Wall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32228)
*Dawei Li,Juhe Liu,Yifei Hao,Qiuchen Wu,Xi Huang,Jesse Stebbing,Xinyi Hou,Gaolei Zhao,Kun Wang,Yongfeng Lu,Xia Hong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作首次在CIPS/PZT异质界面利用铁电畴壁实现了对二次谐波产生（SHG）响应幅度与偏振的纳米尺度非易失调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在纳米尺度上调控范德华铁电体的非线性光学响应，有望推动具有可编程功能的二维集成纳米光子学（two-dimensional integrated nanophotonics）发展。

Method: 通过界面PbZr0.2Ti0.8O3（PZT）薄膜的畴结构，对层状CuInP2S6（CIPS）的SHG强度进行连续调制，并借助非线性电磁理论（nonlinear electromagnetic theory）对界面耦合与偏振行为建模。

Result: 底层铁电氧化物的圆形畴壁（circular domain walls）可使CIPS/PZT异质界面的SHG强度大幅增强或近乎完全淬灭；CIPS极性轴与PZT畴壁面内极化的界面耦合将SHG光偏振调整为二重（C2V）对称性，与理论模型吻合。

Conclusion: 该研究实现了范德华铁电体中非线性光强度与偏振的纳米尺度非易失可编程设计。

Abstract: The control of nonlinear optical responses in van der Waals ferroelectrics at the nanoscale offers opportunities for developing two-dimensional integrated nanophotonics with programmable functionalities. In this work, we report the first realization of nanoscale nonvolatile control of the amplitude and polarization of second-harmonic generation (SHG) response in layered CuInP2S6 (CIPS) via the domain structure in an interfacial PbZr0.2Ti0.8O3 (PZT) thin film. The SHG intensity at CIPS/PZT heterointerface is continuously modulated, either substantially enhanced or almost entirely quenched, by the circular domain walls in underlying ferroelectric oxide. The interfacial coupling of CIPS polar axis with in-plane polarization of PZT domain walls tailors the SHG light polarization into two-fold (C2V) symmetry, which is well modeled by nonlinear electromagnetic theory. Overall, this study enables nonvolatile programmable design of nonlinear light intensity and polarization in van der Waals ferroelectrics at the nanoscale.

</details>


### [146] [Intrinsic Raman Modes and Crystal Symmetry of Altermagnetic $α$-MnTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32261)
*Yunpeng Cui,Shuai Zhang,Jia-Liang Xie,Jinxuan Liang,Hao Lin,Miao Qin,Jiawei Shao,Gongling Chen,Yidian Li,Yixuan Luo,Deng Hu,Bingru Lu,Hao Li,Xiang Li,Tiantian Liu,Haodong Yu,Hongyun Zhang,Shuyun Zhou,Zhiwei Wang,Yanfeng Guo,Tiantian Zhang,Miao-Ling Lin,Ping-Heng Tan,Feng Jin,Luyi Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过高分辨拉曼与二次谐波产生（SHG）光谱结合第一性原理计算，确认α-MnTe中多个显著拉曼特征源于析出的Te团簇而非本征晶格激发，其本征拉曼响应仅含单一E2g声子模式，符合中心对称的D6h对称性，体反转对称性得以保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体（altermagnet）中关联相反自旋子晶格的晶体对称性决定了动量依赖的自旋劈裂，因此确定α-MnTe的真实晶体对称性是理解其本征电子与磁性质的前提；而此前矛盾的拉曼指认使本征晶格激发与晶体对称性均未获解决。

Method: 结合高分辨率拉曼光谱、二次谐波产生（second-harmonic generation, SHG）光谱与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），区分本征晶格激发与Te相关谱学贡献，并检验α-MnTe的体反转对称性（bulk inversion symmetry）。

Result: 多个此前归属于α-MnTe的显著拉曼特征实际来源于偏析的Te团簇或沉淀物；剩余本征拉曼响应仅为一个拉曼活性模式，指认为计算得到的E2g声子，与中心对称的D6h对称性一致；SHG响应符合中心对称D6h晶体预期的表面电偶极与体电四极贡献。

Conclusion: 研究修正了α-MnTe的拉曼指认，并表明其体反转对称性得以保持，为在恰当的晶体对称性框架内解释其交变磁性质提供了基础。

Abstract: Crystal symmetry governs the physical properties of materials and provides the framework for interpreting their microscopic excitations and macroscopic responses. In altermagnets, the crystal symmetries that relate opposite-spin sublattices give rise to momentum-dependent spin splitting despite compensated magnetization. Determining the real crystal symmetry is therefore essential for understanding the intrinsic electronic and magnetic properties. For the altermagnetic semiconductor $α$-MnTe, conflicting Raman assignments have left both its intrinsic lattice excitations and its crystal symmetry unresolved. Here we combine high-resolution Raman and second-harmonic generation (SHG) spectroscopy with first-principles calculations to distinguish intrinsic lattice excitations from Te-derived spectral contributions and to examine bulk inversion symmetry of $α$-MnTe. We show that several prominent Raman features previously assigned to $α$-MnTe instead originate from segregated Te clusters or precipitates. The remaining intrinsic Raman response consists of a single Raman-active mode assigned to the calculated $E_{2g}$ phonon, consistent with the centrosymmetric $D_{6h}$ symmetry. The SHG response agrees with surface electric-dipole and bulk electric-quadrupole contributions expected for a centrosymmetric $D_{6h}$ crystal, supporting the preservation of bulk inversion symmetry in $α$-MnTe. Our findings revise the Raman assignments in $α$-MnTe and provide a basis for interpreting its altermagnetic properties within the appropriate crystal-symmetry framework.

</details>


### [147] [Flexo-Photoresponses from First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32341)
*Zhichao Guo,Hua Wang,Haowei Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作建立第一性原理（first-principles）框架，通过将应变梯度（strain gradients）映射为长波声子（long-wavelength phonons）并借助电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）理论，系统计算真实材料中的挠曲光响应（flexo-photoresponses），揭示其可打破中心对称材料的光学禁戒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅等高对称晶体结构禁止偶数阶非线性光学（nonlinear optical）响应，限制了其在光子学（photonics）与光电子学（optoelectronics）中的应用；应变梯度可打破对称性以激活这些响应，但此前缺乏全面的微观理论框架。

Method: 发展第一性原理方法，将应变梯度映射为成熟电子-声子耦合理论中的长波声子，从而在计算中纳入晶格形变对非线性光学响应的非局域量子力学（nonlocal quantum-mechanical）效应；并应用于Si、立方SrTiO₃（cubic SrTiO₃）与单层黑磷（monolayer black phosphorus）等中心对称（centrosymmetric）原型材料。

Result: 在10⁵~10⁶ m⁻¹的应变梯度下，可诱导出挠曲二次谐波产生（flexo-second-harmonic generation）与挠曲体光伏效应（flexo-bulk photovoltaic effects），其强度可与非中心对称晶体的本征响应相比拟。

Conclusion: 该工作为光-物质相互作用（light-matter interactions）的应变梯度工程建立了严格基础，并为纳米尺度光力学（nanoscale optomechanics）、光电子学等方向开辟新途径。

Abstract: Many functionally important materials, such as silicon, possess high-symmetry crystal structures that forbid even-order nonlinear optical responses, thereby limiting their application in e.g., photonics and optoelectronics. Strain gradients can effectively break symmetries, activating otherwise forbidden optical responses. Although such flexo-photoresponses have attracted considerable interest recently, a comprehensive microscopic theoretical framework is still lacking. Here, we develop a first-principles approach that can systematically evaluate a wide range of flexo-photoresponses in realistic materials. By mapping strain gradients to long-wavelength phonons within the established theory of electron-phonon coupling, our approach captures the nonlocal quantum-mechanical effects of lattice deformations on nonlinear optical responses. Applying the framework to prototypical centrosymmetric materials, including Si, cubic $\rm SrTiO_3$, and monolayer black phosphorus, we demonstrate that a strain gradients of $10^5\sim10^6~\rm m^{-1}$ can induce flexo-second-harmonic generation and flexo-bulk photovoltaic effects comparable to the intrinsic responses of non-centrosymmetric crystals. Our work establishes a rigorous foundation for strain gradient engineering of light-matter interactions, opening new avenues for nanoscale optomechanics, optoelectronics, and beyond.

</details>


### [148] [Electron Wavefronts Capture Nanoscale Movies of Hypersound at Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32419)
*Johannes Otto,Leon Brauns,Benjamin Schroeder,Claus Ropers*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出超快低能电子显微镜（ultrafast low-energy electron microscopy, ULEEM），利用飞秒级相干电子脉冲从样品最表层原子反射，将表面形貌变化印刻于电子波前，从而实现对激光诱导吉赫兹超音速（hypersound）声波的全场成像，灵敏度达皮米级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米尺度声波波长与器件尺寸相当，对高频信号处理、传感、电磁耦合与量子调控至关重要，但直接成像纳米声波与局域结构的相互作用需同时满足皮米级位移灵敏度与高时空分辨率，现有声学计量手段难以实现。

Method: 采用几电子伏特电子的超快低能电子显微镜，利用其本征表面敏感性；飞秒级相干电子脉冲由最表层原子反射，表面轮廓演化被印刻到波前上，通过离焦（defocus）将相位变化转换为图像衬度，并结合弹性动力学建模（elastodynamic modelling）解释测量得到的色散关系。

Result: 在薄层范德华材料（van der Waals material）中成像了皮米振幅声波的热弹激发与传播；色散关系可区分衬底支撑区与局域脱附区，揭示界面接触对传播的影响；延迟波发射显示埋藏机械缺陷处的散射。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了纳米尺度表面动力学的电子波前成像方法，为超快能量输运、结构相变以及力学—电子—磁性耦合现象的研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: Acoustic waves transport mechanical energy and elastic strain through solids, with material composition and shape governing their propagation. In the gigahertz hypersound regime, acoustic wavelengths reach tens to hundreds of nanometres, comparable to nanoscale device dimensions. Harnessing these waves underpins high-frequency signal processing, sensing, electromagnetic coupling and quantum control. Acoustic metrology probes elastic modes and internal interfaces. Yet directly imaging how nanoscale acoustic waves interact with local structure remains challenging, requiring picometre displacement sensitivity combined with high spatiotemporal resolution. Here we demonstrate full-field imaging of laser-induced hypersound at surfaces using reflected electron wavefronts. We introduce ultrafast low-energy electron microscopy (ULEEM), exploiting the intrinsic surface sensitivity of few-electronvolt electrons. Coherent electron pulses down to femtosecond durations reflect from the topmost atomic layers, imprinting the evolving surface profile onto their wavefronts. Defocus converts phase variations into image contrast. In a thin van der Waals material, we image the thermoelastic generation and propagation of picometre-amplitude acoustic waves. Measured dispersion relations, interpreted through elastodynamic modelling, distinguish substrate-supported and locally detached regions, revealing how interfacial contact shapes propagation. Delayed wave emission reveals scattering at buried mechanical defects. Our results establish electron-wavefront imaging of nanoscale surface dynamics, enabling the exploration of ultrafast energy transport, structural transformations and coupled mechanical, electronic and magnetic phenomena.

</details>


### [149] [On the Role of Atomic Mobility in the Polyamorphism of Metallic Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32434)
*Jie Shen,Antoine Cornet,Pei-Hao Sun,Valerio Di Lisio,Daniele Cangialosi,Klara Otto,Isabella Gallino,Martin Rosenthal,Eloi Pineda,Giulio Monaco,Marco Di Michiel,Yong-Hao Sun,Wei-Hua Wang,Ruta Beatrice*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过将芯片式快速差示扫描量热法与高能同步辐射X射线衍射联用，首次同步获取Pd-Ni-P金属玻璃多形性转变（polyamorphic transition）过程中的热力学与结构演化，揭示出玻璃态与平衡过冷液态下两条机制不同的转变路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多形性转变的微观机制需同时掌握热力学与结构演化，但此前实验上无法兼顾，这一同步测量能力的缺失限制了对该转变机理的认识。

Method: 结合芯片式快速差示扫描量热法（fast differential scanning calorimetry）与高能同步辐射X射线衍射（high-energy synchrotron X-ray diffraction），在升温和降温过程中同时探测Pd-Ni-P金属玻璃的热流信号与结构演化，并将二者进行关联分析。

Result: 热流信号与结构演化之间存在直接对应关系，即热力学演化与中程结构有序化（medium-range structural ordering）相关联；据此识别出两种转变机制：玻璃态中转变通过渐进式中程有序化连续进行，热稳定性增强且动力学对温度呈强依赖；平衡过冷液态中转变则以不同非晶态共存的方式发生，动力学仅呈弱温度依赖。

Conclusion: 原子迁移率（atomic mobility）通过将热力学演化与结构有序化耦合来决定转变路径，为非平衡非晶体系（nonequilibrium amorphous systems）中的多形性转变提供了新认识。

Abstract: Understanding the microscopic mechanisms underlying polyamorphic transitions requires simultaneous knowledge of thermodynamic and structural evolution, which has remained experimentally inaccessible. Here, we combine chip-based fast differential scanning calorimetry with high-energy synchrotron X-ray diffraction to simultaneously probe the thermodynamic and structural evolution during the polyamorphic transition of a Pd-Ni-P metallic glass. Correlating heat-flow signals with the corresponding structural evolution during both heating and cooling reveals a direct relationship between thermodynamic evolution and medium-range structural ordering. Based on this relationship, we identify two distinct transition mechanisms. In the glassy state, the transition proceeds continuously through progressive medium-range ordering, accompanied by enhanced thermal stability and strongly temperature-dependent kinetics. In contrast, in the equilibrium supercooled liquid, the transition proceeds through the coexistence of distinct amorphous states, with only a weak temperature dependence of the kinetics. These findings show that atomic mobility governs the transformation pathway by coupling thermodynamic evolution to structural ordering, providing new insights into polyamorphic transitions in nonequilibrium amorphous systems.

</details>


### [150] [Side-Chain Engineering Controls Molecular Packing and Vertical Electron Transport in Discrete n-Type NDI-T2 Oligomers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32456)
*Zihao Liu,Hajar Komekoz,Nisarg Trivedi,Max Dorfner,Lokesh Rasabathina,Rico Ehrler,Mike Hambsch,Chen Wang,Dietrich R. T. Zahn,Jörg Matysik,Stefan C. B. Mannsfeld,Carsten Deibel,Olav Hellwig,Frank Ortmann,Michael Sommer,Georgeta Salvan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 以两种含乙基己基（EH）与辛基十二烷基（OD）侧链的共轭低聚物为模型体系，结合AFM、C-AFM、GIWAXS、稳态光谱与TDDFT计算，揭示退火与侧链结构对分子堆积、激子耦合及垂直电子迁移率的影响，EH膜最高迁移率达1.2×10^-4 cm² V^-1 s^-1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层有机光电器件中的高效垂直电荷传输（vertical charge transport）高度依赖分子结构与排列方式，而侧链结构如何通过分子堆积与激子耦合影响垂直电子传输尚需明确的结构-性能关联依据。

Method: 采用侧链分别为乙基己基（ethylhexyl, EH）和辛基十二烷基（octyldodecyl, OD）的T2-(NDI-T2)2共轭低聚物（conjugated oligomers）作为模型体系，使用原子力显微镜（AFM）、导电原子力显微镜（C-AFM）、掠入射广角X射线散射（GIWAXS）、稳态光学光谱（steady-state optical spectroscopy）及含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）计算进行表征，并用空间电荷限制电流（SCLC）模型分析电流-电压特性。

Result: 两种材料在初铸态（as-cast）均呈较无序结构，退火提升结构有序度并引起侧链依赖的分子堆积变化；EH基薄膜的退火诱导光致发光位移小于OD基薄膜，表明其堆积与构象重排较弱，与TDDFT支持的激子分析一致。退火后两者垂直电子迁移率均提高，EH基薄膜最高达1.2×10^-4 cm² V^-1 s^-1。

Conclusion: 较短EH侧链可改变面外（out-of-plane）堆积环境并促进垂直电子传输，由此建立了侧链结构、分子有序度、激子耦合与垂直迁移率之间的结构-性能关系（structure-property relationship），为通过侧链工程（side-chain engineering）优化有机半导体提供分子设计依据。

Abstract: High-efficiency vertical charge transport in multilayer organic optoelectronic devices highly depends on molecular structure and arrangement. Using two T2-(NDI-T2)2 conjugated oligomers with ethylhexyl (EH) and octyldodecyl (OD) side chains as a model system, their effects on electron transport are investigated using, among other techniques, atomic force microscopy (AFM), conductive atomic force microscopy (C-AFM), grazing-incidence wide-angle X-ray scattering (GIWAXS), steady-state optical spectroscopy, and time-dependent density functional theory (TDDFT) calculations. The oligomers of both materials exhibit relatively disordered structures in the as-cast state, whereas annealing enhances structural order and leads to side-chain-dependent changes in molecular stacking. EH-based films show a smaller annealing-induced photoluminescence shift than OD-based films, suggesting less pronounced stacking and conformational rearrangements, consistent with TDDFT-supported exciton analysis. The current-voltage characteristics are analyzed using the space-charge-limited current (SCLC) model. After annealing, both materials exhibit improved vertical electron mobility. The EH-based films reach a maximum mobility of 1.2 x 10^-4 cm^2 V^-1 s^-1. The combined results suggest that the shorter EH side chains modify the out-of-plane packing environment and facilitate vertical electron transport. These results establish a structure-property relationship between side-chain structure, molecular order, exciton coupling and vertical mobility, providing a molecular design basis for optimising organic semiconductors through side-chain engineering.

</details>


### [151] [Divalent Yb in the Ca$_3$Pd$_4$Bi$_8$ Structure Type: Crystal Growth and Physical Properties of Yb$_3$Pd$_4$Bi$_8$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32524)
*Midori Amano Patino,Sitaram Ramakrishnan,Davide Donadio,Nils Lamouche,Elise Pachoud,Macha Meplan,Daniel Braithwaite,Jean-Pascal Brison,Gerard Lapertot,Andrea Bianchi,Georg Knebel,Valentin Taufour*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: Yb₃Pd₄Bi₈以Ca₃Pd₄Bi₈结构型单晶形式合成，Yb呈非磁性二价态，表现为金属，其能带在布里渊区边界受非简单对称性（nonsymmorphic symmetry）保护形成四重简并节线（nodal lines）及对称性强制的狄拉克点（Dirac points）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Ca₃Pd₄Bi₈结构族中是否存在稀土类似物（rare-earth analog），并表征Yb₃Pd₄Bi₈的晶体结构、价态与电子性质。

Method: 单晶生长与单晶X射线衍射（single-crystal x-ray diffraction）确定结构；磁化测量（magnetization measurements）判断Yb价态；电阻率测量（electrical resistivity measurements）；含自旋-轨道耦合（spin–orbit coupling）的第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）。

Result: 材料在常压下属正交（orthorhombic）Pbam空间群的Ca₃Pd₄Bi₈结构型；磁化呈近各向同性抗磁响应，表明Yb为二价非磁性；电阻率显示金属行为；计算表明费米能级态以Bi 6p轨道为主，态密度（density of states）与等电子化合物Ca₃Pd₄Bi₈相差在1%以内；非简单对称性在布里渊区（Brillouin-zone）边界强制四重简并节线，其中四条穿过费米能级并形成具线性横向色散的对称性强制狄拉克点。

Conclusion: 尽管Pd-Bi网络内存在强金属性与共价键合，Yb₃Pd₄Bi₈仍作为Ca₃Pd₄Bi₈结构型的稀土类似物以二价Yb形式实现，为在该结构族中寻找其他稀土类似物提供了可能途径。

Abstract: We report on the single-crystal growth, crystal structure, and physical properties of Yb$_3$Pd$_4$Bi$_8$. Single crystal x-ray diffraction shows that the material crystallizes in the Ca$_3$Pd$_4$Bi$_8$ structure-type at ambient conditions, described by orthorhombic symmetry $Pbam$. Magnetization measurements reveal a nearly isotropic diamagnetic response, indicating that Yb is stabilized in a non-magnetic divalent configuration. Electrical resistivity measurements show a metallic behavior. First-principles calculations including spin--orbit coupling describe a metal whose states at the Fermi level are dominated by Bi $6p$ orbitals, with a density of states within 1% of that of the isoelectronic compound Ca$_3$Pd$_4$Bi$_8$, consistent with a filled and chemically inert $4f$ shell. They further show that the nonsymmorphic symmetry of the crystal enforces fourfold-degenerate nodal lines on the Brillouin-zone boundary, four of which cross the Fermi level and give symmetry-enforced Dirac points with a linear transverse dispersion. Despite strong metallic and covalent bonding within the Pd-Bi network, Yb$_3$Pd$_4$Bi$_8$ is realized as a rare-earth analog of the Ca$_3$Pd$_4$Bi$_8$ structure type with divalent Yb ions. This result offers a potential avenue to discover other rare-earth analogs within this structural family.

</details>


### [152] [Modification of optical properties in ultrathin ALD-grown NbN layers using oxygen plasma treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32553)
*Mustafa Teke,Nitesh Yerra,Alireza Sadeghi,Mario Ziegler,Detlef Born,Nan Du,Marco Diegel,Uwe Hübner,Sahitya V. Vegesna,Heidemarie Krüger*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究用原子层沉积（atomic layer deposition, ALD）制备10 nm NbN薄膜，并经0–60 s氧等离子体（oxygen plasma）处理，通过椭偏光谱（spectroscopic ellipsometry, SE）等手段发现约4.9 eV处出现新吸收特征，且有效载流子浓度与迁移率随处理时间下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究氧等离子体处理对NbN薄膜光学性质的影响，理解氧致表面氧化所引起的光学变化。

Method: 在Al$_2$O$_3$缓冲Si衬底上以ALD生长名义厚度10 nm的NbN薄膜，分别进行0、20、40、60 s氧等离子体处理；采用紫外-可见-近红外（1.24–6.20 eV）与红外（0.041–0.827 eV）波段的SE测量，并以X射线反射（X-ray reflectivity, XRR）确定的膜厚作为固定参数进行光学建模，提取介电函数、光学常数与有效载流子浓度。

Result: 氧等离子体处理后约4.9 eV处出现明显吸收特征，与NbN表面氧化及其光学性质变化一致；处理时间由0增至60 s时，有效载流子浓度由$1.20 \times 10^{22}$降至$1.03 \times 10^{22}$ cm$^{-3}$，有效载流子迁移率由2.44降至2.27 cm$^2$ V$^{-1}$ s$^{-1}$。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了氧等离子体处理对NbN薄膜光学与载流子性质的影响，为理解该处理效应提供了基础依据。

Abstract: NbN thin films with a nominal thickness of 10 nm were grown by atomic layer deposition (ALD) on Al$_2$O$_3$-buffered Si substrates and treated with oxygen plasma for 0 s (untreated), 20 s, 40 s, and 60 s to investigate oxygen-induced changes in their optical properties. Spectroscopic ellipsometry (SE) measurements were performed before and after oxygen plasma treatment over the ultraviolet-visible-near-infrared (1.24-6.20 eV) and infrared (0.041-0.827 eV) spectral ranges. Film thickness determined by X-ray reflectivity (XRR) was used as a fixed parameter in the optical modeling to extract the dielectric function, optical constants, and effective free-carrier concentration. We observed the emergence of a distinct absorption feature near 4.9 eV following oxygen plasma treatment, consistent with the oxidation of the NbN surface and the resulting change in its optical properties. Increasing the treatment time from 0 to 60 s reduced the effective carrier concentration from $1.20 \times 10^{22}$ to $1.03 \times 10^{22}$ cm$^{-3}$ and the effective carrier mobility from 2.44 to 2.27 cm$^2$ V$^{-1}$ s$^{-1}$. The obtained results provide a basis for understanding the effects of oxygen plasma treatment on NbN thin films.

</details>


### [153] [High-quality, deep formation and identification of oxygen-vacancy defects in silicon carbide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32642)
*Ryo Shiotani,Fu-Chieh Chuang,Mitsuaki Kaneko,Tetsuri Nishikawa,Hiroshi Abe,Taiga Matsuoka,Shigehito Miki,Kazuki Okajima,Takeshi Ohshima,Yoshihiko Kanemitsu,Tsunenobu Kimoto,Naoya Morioka,Norikazu Mizuochi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过将氧的引入与缺陷形成过程解耦，在碳化硅（SiC）中制备出高质量氧-空位自旋缺陷（PL6等），实现高度均匀的自旋共振频率、窄光学线宽与长T2*相干时间，并借助超精细相互作用统计确认其氧-空位起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SiC中光可寻址顺磁缺陷（optically addressable paramagnetic defects）适用于可扩展量子技术，PL5、PL6、PL7亮度高、自旋读出对比度好；但氧注入（oxygen implantation）生成方式存在自旋与光学性质不均匀、生成深度受限的问题，阻碍高灵敏传感与微观鉴定。

Method: 将氧的掺入（oxygen incorporation）与缺陷形成（defect formation）两个过程解耦，以工程化方式制备高质量缺陷；并利用氧扩散（oxygen diffusion）在离子注入射程之外形成缺陷；同时对17O与宿主核的超精细相互作用（hyperfine interaction）进行统计。

Result: 获得高度均匀的自旋共振频率、窄光学线宽度与长T2*相干时间；证实氧扩散可突破离子射程限制形成缺陷，这在SiC杂质中较为罕见；基于多个PL6缺陷的超精细相互作用统计，确认其氧-空位（oxygen-vacancy）起源。

Conclusion: 微观鉴定与所展示的缺陷形成路径共同为将氧-空位自旋缺陷发展为通用量子平台（quantum platform）奠定基础，有望用于量子传感等可扩展量子技术。

Abstract: Optically addressable paramagnetic defects in silicon carbide (SiC) are attractive for scalable quantum technologies such as quantum sensing. PL5, PL6, and PL7 are promising defects for their high brightness and spin-readout contrast. Although generation by oxygen implantation has been reported very recently, nonuniform spin and optical properties and the limited generation depth of implanted defects hinder both high-sensitivity sensing and conclusive microscopic identification. Here we engineer high-quality defects by decoupling oxygen incorporation and defect formation, realizing highly homogeneous spin-resonance frequencies as well as narrow optical linewidths and long T2* coherence time. Further, we demonstrate defect formation beyond the ion range utilizing oxygen diffusion, exceptional among impurities in SiC. We further provide hyperfine-interaction statistics of 17O and host nuclei across many PL6 defects, making the oxygen-vacancy origin conclusive. Together, the microscopic identification and the demonstrated formation route establish a foundation for developing the oxygen-vacancy spin defect into a versatile quantum platform.

</details>


### [154] [Probing the limits of forward screening and inverse design of crystal materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32678)
*Zhilong Song,Zijun Pang,Lixue Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究将55个晶体数据库按结构匹配整合为含1460万晶体、1.318亿性质值的自扩展数据库，并开发等变图变换器CGTNet填补缺失性质值，据此测度了正向筛选与逆向生成两条材料发现路径的极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体材料设计依赖筛选已知晶体或生成新晶体两条路径，二者均以已知晶体公共数据库为判据，但各数据库对同一晶体命名不一致，导致近期若干宣称“新发现”的物相实为已知相或其近似变体；两条路径的能力边界此前未被量化。

Method: 按结构匹配整合55个数据库，构建可自我扩展的统一数据库（1460万晶体、1.318亿性质值，均可溯源），并发现仅4.5%的晶体—性质对含数值；为此提出CGTNet——一种等变图变换器（equivariant graph transformer），在两个公开基准的多项任务上达到当前最优精度，逐任务训练后形成性质模型库并公开预测结果。

Result: 正向筛选的覆盖范围被低估：已知晶体中存在数万个未记录体积模量的超高不可压缩候选，事后合成证据审计在其中发现ReN2，其体积模量实测为428 GPa。逆向设计的新颖性被高估：在相同验证预算下，刚度条件生成器返回的刚性命中数多于筛选（100对55），但全部含锇且82个为已有记录成分的重复。

Conclusion: 整合并补全已知晶体数据库可同时移动两条路径的边界：既拓宽筛选可达范围，也为判定生成晶体是否“新颖”提供参照基准。

Abstract: Crystal materials are designed either by screening known crystals for a target property or by generating new crystals with that property. Both routes are judged against public databases of known crystals, but these databases name the same crystal differently. Several materials announced as new by recent generative and autonomous discovery campaigns have since proved to be known phases or close variants of them. The limits of both routes remain unmeasured. Here we probe them by matching the crystals of 55 databases by structure into one self-extending database of 14.6 million crystals and 131.8 million property values, each traced to its source. Only 4.5% of its crystal-property pairs carry a value. To fill the empty pairs, we developed CGTNet, an equivariant graph transformer with state-of-the-art accuracy on several tasks of two public benchmarks. Trained on each task, it forms a library of property models whose predictions we release. The reach of forward screening has been underestimated. Known crystals hold tens of thousands of ultra-incompressible candidates without a recorded bulk modulus, and a post hoc audit of synthesis evidence finds among them ReN2, whose bulk modulus was measured at 428 GPa. The novelty claimed for inverse design has been overestimated. At an equal verification budget, a stiffness-conditioned generator returned more stiff hits than screening (100 versus 55), but all contained osmium and 82 repeated recorded compositions. Integrating and completing the databases of known crystals therefore moves the limits of both routes: it widens what screening can reach and sets the reference against which generated crystals are judged new.

</details>


### [155] [On the Spontaneous Structural Reconstructions of Chalcogen-Depleted MoS$_2$, MoSe$_2$, and Janus MoSSe Monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32712)
*Bruno Ipaves,Guilherme S. L. Fabris,Raphael B. Oliveira,Pulickel M. Ajayan,Marcelo L. Pereira Junior,Douglas S. Galvão*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过从头算（ab initio）与反应力场分子动力学（reactive MD）模拟，揭示缺失一层硫族元素（chalcogen）的 MoS₂、MoSe₂ 及 Janus MoSSe 单层中，剩余硫族元素的种类主导结构重构（structural reconstruction）模式，母体晶格影响较小；该机制可用于在单一单层内图案化晶态与无序区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从过渡金属硫族化合物（transition-metal dichalcogenide, TMD）单层移除一层硫族元素后，会留下配位不饱和（undercoordinated）的薄层，其通过剩余硫族原子跨越金属平面而重构；然而剩余硫族元素种类与母体晶格如何分别影响重构行为尚不明确。

Method: 结合从头算与反应分子动力学模拟，研究对象为去硫族化的 MoS₂、MoSe₂ 与 Janus MoSSe，并将每种剩余硫族元素置于两种母体晶格上考察；同时在自由面内晶胞条件下考察各向异性晶格收缩，并对 50 nm 尺寸薄层及去硫族化 MoS₂/MoSe₂ 横向异质结构（lateral heterostructure）进行反应 MD 模拟。

Result: Janus 衍生薄层的从头算轨迹显示，Se 在 300 K 即跨越 Mo 平面，而 S 在 300 K 保持原位、仅在 900 K 发生重构；自由面内晶胞下跨越先于各向异性晶格收缩，表明收缩源于结构重构。反应 MD 显示：S 残留时 Mo 晶格在纳米畴内保持晶态，随时间与温度升高而消失并在 900 K 出现孔洞；Se 残留时 Mo 晶格在首个皮秒内即失去晶序，且各温度下均有 36–39% 的 Se 原子跨越。在横向异质结构中两种模式共存，各自半区如同其单硫族对应物般重构。

Conclusion: 剩余硫族元素类型是决定去硫族化 TMD 单层结构重构的主导因素，母体晶格仅起次要作用；S 与 Se 分别对应晶态保持与快速无序化两种重构模式，并可在单一单层中并存，提示硫族元素图案化（chalcogen patterning）可作为在单层内定义晶态与无序区域的途径。

Abstract: Removing one chalcogen layer from a transition-metal dichalcogenide monolayer leaves an undercoordinated sheet that reconstructs as the remaining chalcogen atoms cross the metal plane. Here, we combine \textit{ab initio} and reactive molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of depleted MoS$_2$, MoSe$_2$, and Janus MoSSe, examining each remaining chalcogen on two parent lattices, to show that the remaining chalcogen type determines the structural reconstruction, with only a minor influence from the parent lattice. In \textit{ab initio} trajectories of the Janus-derived sheets, Se crosses the Mo plane already at 300~K, whereas S stays in place at 300~K and the sheet reconstructs only at 900~K. With a free in-plane cell, the crossings precede an anisotropic lattice contraction, which therefore results from the structural reconstruction. Reactive MD simulations of 50~nm sheets show that, when S remains, the Mo lattice stays crystalline in nanometric domains that disappear with time and temperature, with pores/holes at 900~K. In contrast, when Se remains, the Mo lattice loses crystalline order within the first picosecond, and 36--39\% of the Se atoms cross at every temperature. In a depleted lateral MoS$_2$/MoSe$_2$ heterostructure, both modes coexist in one sheet, each half reconstructing like its single-chalcogen counterpart, suggesting chalcogen patterning as a route to define crystalline and disordered regions in a single monolayer.

</details>


### [156] [Controlled Fabrication of Uniform Columnar Porous GaN Architectures by Electrochemical Etching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32722)
*Thom R. Harris-Lee,Ben Thornley,Jiawei Zhang,Martin Frentrup,Simon Fairclough,Menno J. Kappers,Rachel A. Oliver*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在Na2C2O4等非酸性刻蚀液中加入NaOH，可将n型c面GaN的电化学刻蚀（electrochemical etching, ECE）导向纳米级、垂直排列、高度均匀的柱状孔结构，并可通过调节NaOH浓度调控孔径、间距与孔隙率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: n型c面GaN的ECE可制备多孔结构以提升光电器件性能并拓展GaN应用范围，但需要一种可控、通用的方法来获得高孔隙率、有序且定制的孔形貌。

Method: 在Na2C2O4及其他非酸性刻蚀液中添加NaOH，并考察不同NaOH浓度对孔形貌的影响；同时对比Na2CO3、Na3PO4等经水解本身含较高氢氧根浓度的碱性电解液；结合计时电流法（chronoamperometry）与孔结构分析解析ECE机制。

Result: 添加NaOH可形成纳米级、垂直取向、高度均匀的柱状孔形貌且表面粗化有限；改变NaOH浓度可调节柱状孔形状、直径、间距及体/表面孔隙率；Na2CO3与Na3PO4亦产生类似柱状孔，表明氢氧根在引导垂直孔生长中起关键作用；混合电解液体系呈现区别于各阴离子单独ECE行为简单叠加的新机制。

Conclusion: 该工作提出多步反应、氢氧根参与生成活性中间体的ECE机制，为可控制备柱状多孔GaN结构提供了通用途径，有利于高孔隙率、有序及定制结构的应用。

Abstract: Electrochemical etching (ECE) of n-type c-plane GaN enables the formation of porous architectures capable of enhancing the performance of optoelectronic devices and broadening the range of possible GaN applications. Here, we demonstrate that the addition of NaOH to Na2C2O4 (and other non-acidic etchant solutions) directs ECE toward the formation of nanoscale, vertically aligned, highly uniform columnar pore morphologies with relatively limited surface roughening. By varying the NaOH concentration within mixed electrolyte systems, the columnar pore shape, diameter, spacing, and bulk and surface porosity can be readily tuned. In addition to manually mixing NaOH with another etchant, aqueous basic electrolytes such as Na2CO3 and Na3PO4, each inherently containing significant hydroxide concentration through hydrolysis, also produce similar columnar pore morphologies, indicating that hydroxide plays the key role in directing the vertical pore growth. Chronoamperometry and pore structure analysis reveals that the mixed hydroxide-containing electrolyte systems exhibit a distinct, new ECE mechanism rather than a simple superposition of the individual ECE behaviour of each anion present. This work provides significant insight into the ECE mechanism for porous GaN, which is proposed to contain multiple reaction steps where hydroxide anions are involved in the formation of a reactive intermediate. These findings establish a versatile and controllable route for engineering columnar porous GaN architectures, offering significant advancement in applications requiring high porosity, ordered, and bespoke structures.

</details>


### [157] [Ginzburg-Landau Theory with Elastic Incompatibility Describes the Liquid-G-phase-Crystal State Diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32791)
*Alessio Zaccone,Konrad Samwer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出一个力学自洽的金兹堡-朗道（Ginzburg-Landau）理论，用于描述深过冷金属液体中报道的亚稳非均匀G态，指出类第二类超导混合态物理可在结构层面涌现，随机内应力场扮演磁场角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深过冷金属液体中观察到的亚稳非均匀G态缺乏力学自洽的理论描述，需要解释有序核与无序基体共存并受内应力调控的机制。

Method: 构建力学自洽的Ginzburg-Landau理论：有序核与无定形基体间弹性不相容产生冻结本征应变（frozen eigenstrain）与不相容应力，液体状基体中的非仿射内应变（non-affine internal strain）在有限屏蔽长度λ_σ内松弛应力，结构有序在力学修饰长度ξ_G上愈合，比值κ_G=λ_σ/ξ_G对应结构版Ginzburg-Landau参数。

Result: 理论给出反Abrikosov类比织构：结构有序位于核内，不相容应力屏蔽由周围无序基体承担；残余不相容阻碍粗化，形成有限尺寸有序核及屏蔽的、取向依赖的核间相互作用；矩阵的瞬态刚性-局部松弛特性使慢速协议选晶体、中间速率选G态、快速冻结选均匀L类玻璃。

Conclusion: 该理论可通过应力衰减、结构愈合、非仿射磁化率（non-affine susceptibility）与动力学测量直接接受模拟与实验检验。

Abstract: We derive a mechanically consistent Ginzburg-Landau theory for the metastable heterogeneous G state reported in deeply undercooled metallic liquids. The central result is that type-II-like mixed-state physics can emerge structurally with the random internal stress field playing the role of the magnetic field. Frozen eigenstrain due to elastic incompatibility between ordered cores and the amorphous matrix generates incompatible stress, while the non-affine internal strain in the liquid-like matrix in between the ordered cores relaxes that stress over a finite screening length $λ_σ$ (analogous to the London length in the superconducting problem). Structural order heals over a distinct mechanically-dressed length $ξ_G$; their ratio $κ_G=λ_σ/ξ_G$ provides the structural counterpart of the Ginzburg-Landau parameter. Because the matrix is transiently rigid yet locally relaxational only over an intermediate time window, the same mechanism selects crystal for slow protocols, a heterogeneous G state at intermediate rates, and a homogeneous L-like glass under rapid arrest. Residual incompatibility frustrates coarsening, allowing finite ordered cores and screened, orientation-dependent core interactions. The resulting texture is an inverted Abrikosov analogue: structural order resides in the cores, whereas incompatible-stress screening is carried by the surrounding disordered matrix. The theory yields direct simulation and experimental tests through stress-decay, structural-healing, non-affine-susceptibility, and kinetic measurements.

</details>


### [158] [Rationalizing charge transfer: a single-molecule case study across three distinct surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32903)
*Benjamin Lowe,Jan Berger,Martin Ondráček,Oleksandr Stetsovych,Shayan Louie,Marco Lozano,Diego Soler-Polo,Shiladitya Karmarkar,Andrés Pinar Solé,Iva Plutnarová,Pingo Mutombo,Manish Kumar,Michael Píro,Ali Cem Demirok,Michael L. Steigerwald,Zdeněk Sofer,Colin Nuckolls,Pavel Jelínek*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作系统比较孤立大环翠绿亚胺（emeraldine，Me₆）分子在Au(111)、FeCl₂单层与2H-NbSe₂表面的性质，表明单独使用扫描隧道谱（STS）与密度泛函理论（DFT）不足以定量电荷转移（charge transfer），需结合开尔文探针力显微镜（KPFM）与模型哈密顿量计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面合成（on-surface synthesis）正从贵金属表面拓展至绝缘、磁性及超导表面，而这些替代表面可能通过电荷转移与屏蔽（screening）改变吸附物性质，因此需理解不同表面对吸附物的影响并建立可跨表面比较的方法。

Method: 在Au(111)、FeCl₂铁磁半导体单层及解理的2H-NbSe₂超导表面上研究孤立Me₆分子，将STM/STS与DFT同KPFM测量及模型哈密顿量（model Hamiltonian）计算相结合。

Result: 在Au(111)与FeCl₂上，Me₆分子向衬底转移一个电子；而在NbSe₂上，分子向衬底转移两个电子。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了电荷转移、屏蔽与分子-衬底耦合（molecule-substrate coupling）之间的相互作用，并为跨不同表面比较吸附物性质提供了实验与理论方法。

Abstract: Organic molecules are versatile building blocks for the formation of complex low-dimensional nanostructures on surfaces. Recently, interest has grown in translating on-surface synthetic protocols from noble metal surfaces to insulating surfaces, which offer technological relevance, and to magnetic or superconducting surfaces, which offer new routes to correlated phases. It is important, therefore, to understand how these alternative surfaces may alter the properties of adsorbates through processes like charge transfer and screening. Evidence for charge transfer is often obtained experimentally via scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) and spectroscopy (STS), and theoretically via density functional theory (DFT). In this work, we systematically compare the properties of isolated macrocyclic emeraldine (Me$_6$) molecules on three different surfaces: metallic Au(111), a semiconducting ferromagnetic monolayer of FeCl$_2$, and a cleaved superconducting surface of bulk 2H-NbSe$_2$. We show that STS and DFT alone are insufficient to quantify charge transfer for these cases. Instead, we complement these methods with Kelvin probe force microscopy (KPFM) measurements and model Hamiltonian calculations to demonstrate that on Au(111) and FeCl$_2$, Me$_6$ molecules donate a single electron to the substrate, whereas on NbSe$_2$ two electrons are transferred from molecule to substrate. Our work provides insight into the interplay of charge transfer, screening, and molecule-substrate coupling, and develops experimental and theoretical methods for comparing the properties of adsorbates across different surfaces for future studies.

</details>


### [159] [Combining Simulations and Spectroscopy to Design Protective Coatings on Sulfide Solid Electrolytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32905)
*Zhiyi Wang,Leonie Braks,Michele Ceriotti,Ali Coskun,Hanna Türk,Davide Tisi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合机器学习势（machine-learning potential）驱动的大尺度原子模拟与实验，建立了在Li$_6$PS$_5$Cl（LPSCl）硫化物固态电解质表面设计分子涂层（molecular coating）的框架，揭示头基化学、锂化状态与烷基链长度对吸附、形貌及水/离子传输行为的调控规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硫化物固态电解质（sulfide solid electrolyte）虽具高锂离子电导与良好加工性，但受电化学不稳定与湿气诱导降解限制；分子表面涂层是提升耐湿性与界面稳定性的可扩展策略，然而对支配表面相互作用的分子层面因素理解不足。

Method: 基于在r$^2$SCAN参考数据集上微调的PET-MAD原子间势（interatomic potential），对LPSCl表面、分子吸附物及其相互作用进行大尺度原子模拟，建模不同链长羧酸（CA）、磺酸（SA）与膦酸（PA）的吸附及有限温度下水与涂层表面的相互作用；并结合阻抗谱（impedance spectroscopy）等靶向实验验证。

Result: 模拟表明锂化头基（lithiated head group）通常具有更有利的吸附能；阻抗谱显示锂化涂层在粉末加工后保持初始离子电导的40–55%。吸附强度主要由头基化学与锂化状态决定，涂层形貌则主要取决于烷基链长度与表面覆盖度；长链锂化羧酸盐形成致密刷状疏水层，在约0.8 nm厚度时开始阻隔水接触反应性Li表面位点，且长链羧酸盐更能有效抑制H$_2$S生成。

Conclusion: 更厚涂层会阻碍锂离子传输，因此中等链长在防潮保护与离子电导之间取得最佳平衡；该框架为硫化物电解质表面分子涂层的分子级设计提供了可迁移的模拟—实验协同路径。

Abstract: Sulfide solid electrolytes are attractive for all-solid-state batteries because of their high Li-ion conductivity and favorable processability, but their practical use is limited by electrochemical instability and moisture-induced degradation. Molecular surface coatings offer a scalable strategy to improve humidity tolerance and interfacial stability, yet there is limited understanding of the molecular-level factors governing surface interactions. By combining large-scale atomistic simulations enabled by a machine-learning potential with targeted experiments, we present a comprehensive framework for designing molecular coatings on Li$_6$PS$_5$Cl (LPSCl). Using a PET-MAD interatomic potential fine-tuned on an r$^2$SCAN reference dataset covering LPSCl surfaces, molecular adsorbates, and their interactions, we model surface adsorption of carboxylic (CA), sulfonic (SA), and phosphonic (PA) acids with alkyl chains of varying lengths and the interaction of these coated surfaces with water at finite temperature. The simulations show that lithiated head groups generally exhibit more favorable adsorption energies. Impedance spectroscopy shows that the lithiated coatings maintain 40--55\% of the initial ionic conductivity after powder processing. While adsorption strength is largely controlled by head-group chemistry and lithiation state, coating morphology depends primarily on alkyl-chain length and surface coverage. In particular, long-chain lithiated carboxylates form dense, brush-like hydrophobic layers that reduce water access to reactive Li surface sites, with protection setting in at a coating thickness of approximately 0.8 nm. Consistent with these predictions, longer carboxylate chains suppress H$_2$S evolution more effectively. Because thicker coatings also hinder Li-ion transport, intermediate chain lengths offer the best balance between moisture protection and ionic conductivity.

</details>


### [160] [CO2-Induced Shifts in the Confined Phase Transformation of CH4 in Metal-Organic Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32918)
*Anthony Dorhauer,Bartosz Mazur,Bogdan Kuchta,Carlos Wexler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在刚性IRMOF-1与IRMOF-8中，CO2共吸附虽降低CH4最大吸附量，却将CH4吸附等温线的阶跃显著推向更低压力，从而促进高密度CH4相的形成，表明CO2可作为受限CH4吸附转变的热力学调节剂（thermodynamic modifier）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米多孔材料（nanoporous materials）如金属有机框架（MOFs）中，受限流体的气体吸附在窄压力区间内可能急剧上升，反映吸附态之间的转变并常伴随亚稳态（metastable states）；理解共吸附组分（co-adsorbed species）如何改变该行为，对气体储存与分离的预测和优化具有重要意义。

Method: 采用跃迁矩阵蒙特卡洛（Transition-Matrix Monte Carlo, TMMC）模拟，研究CO2对刚性IRMOF-1与IRMOF-8中受限CH4吸附转变的影响；IRMOF-1体系含0–75个CO2分子，IRMOF-8体系含0–150个CO2分子。

Result: 加入CO2使CH4最大吸附量下降，符合孔空间竞争预期；但相较纯CH4体系，CO2的存在使CH4吸附等温线（adsorption isotherm）的不连续跃变显著移至更低压力，即低压下高密度CH4相被促进。

Conclusion: CO2通过改变流体-流体（fluid-fluid）与流体-骨架（fluid-framework）相互作用，实质性地重塑支配受限CH4的热力学图景（thermodynamic landscape）；结果确立CO2为受限CH4行为的热力学调节剂，并提示受控共吸附可作为调控纳米多孔材料中吸附转变的手段。

Abstract: A sharp increase in gas uptake over a narrow pressure range can occur for fluids confined in nanoporous materials such as metal-organic frameworks (MOFs), reflecting a transformation between distinct adsorption states, often accompanied by the existence of metastable states. Understanding how co-adsorbed species modify this behavior is important for predicting and optimizing gas storage and separation. Here, we investigate the effect of CO2 on the adsorption transformation of confined CH4 in rigid IRMOF-1 and IRMOF-8. Transition-Matrix Monte Carlo (TMMC) simulations were performed for systems containing 0-75 and 0-150 CO2 molecules in IRMOF-1 and IRMOF-8, respectively. While the addition of CO2 decreases the maximum CH4 uptake, consistent with competition for pore space, remarkably, the presence of CO2 significantly shifts the discontinuity in the CH4 adsorption isotherm to substantially lower pressures relative to the pure CH4 system. This promotion of the high-density CH4 phase at lower pressures demonstrates that CO2 substantially alters the thermodynamic landscape governing confined CH4 through changes in both fluid-fluid and fluid-framework interactions. These results establish CO2 as a thermodynamic modifier of confined CH4 behavior and suggest that controlled co-adsorption provides a means of tuning adsorption transformations in nanoporous materials.

</details>


### [161] [Vacancy-enhanced Li solid-solution anodes for solid-state batteries: computational screening enabling high critical current density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32938)
*Arseniy S. Burov,Nadezhda A. Ashikhmina,Nurlan Zhanabayev,Anton O. Boev,Aleksandra D. Radina,Ilya V. Chepkasov,Alexander G. Kvashnin,Dmitry A. Aksyonov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）、相图自动构建与溶解度约束，筛选50种置换固溶元素以提升bcc Li中的有效空位浓度，预测Ag、Ge、Cd可在溶解度极限内使临界电流密度（critical current density, CCD）达1 mA cm⁻²，其中Ag约9 at.%时可达约4 mA cm⁻²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂金属中缓慢的空位介导自扩散（vacancy-mediated self-diffusion）在剥离过程中促进界面空洞（void）形成，从而限制了固态电池锂负极的临界电流密度；需在保持bcc Li基体的前提下提高有效空位浓度。

Method: 基于自动相图构建、固溶能（solution energy）、溶质-空位结合能（solute-vacancy binding energy）与实验溶解度极限构建筛选漏斗（funnel），用DFT筛选50种置换溶质；采用稀溶限下的Lomer模型修正估算CCD，并进行迁移势垒（migration barrier）计算；另以DFT数据训练机器学习模型作为前置筛选。

Result: 仅Ag、Ge、Cd固溶体被预测可在溶解度极限内达到1 mA cm⁻²；Ag在约9 at.%时热力学估算CCD约4 mA cm⁻²（理论容量1522 mAh g⁻¹）。迁移势垒计算表明该提升可能被空位捕获（vacancy trapping）部分抵消，需分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟进一步验证。

Conclusion: 溶质合金化可提升锂负极CCD，但需权衡空位捕获效应；单一Hume-Rothery规则不足以作为前置筛选条件，而基于DFT数据训练的机器学习模型可早期剔除多数不适宜溶质，并将流程推广至相关固溶体搜索。

Abstract: Sluggish vacancy-mediated self-diffusion in lithium metal limits the critical current density (CCD) of Li anodes in solid-state batteries by promoting interfacial void formation during stripping. To enhance the effective vacancy concentration while preserving the bcc Li host, we screen 50 substitutional solutes using density functional theory (DFT) within a funnel based on automatic phase diagram construction, solution energies, solute-vacancy binding energies, and experimental solubility limits. Using an adaptation of the Lomer model for the dilute limit, we show that only Ag, Ge, and Cd solid solutions are predicted to reach $1~\mathrm{mA~cm^{-2}}$ within their solubility limits. The thermodynamically estimated CCD of Ag at $\sim 9$ at.% reaches $\sim 4~\mathrm{mA~cm^{-2}}$ at a theoretical capacity of $1522~\mathrm{mAh~g^{-1}}$. Migration barrier calculations indicate that the enhancement may be partially offset by vacancy trapping, necessitating further validation via molecular dynamics simulations. Notably, Hume-Rothery rules alone prove insufficient as prefilters, whereas machine-learning models trained on the DFT data can eliminate most unsuitable solutes early in the funnel and extend the workflow to related solid-solution searches.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Bounded compositions and products of chains, or bi(s)nomials everywhere from de Moivre's formula to spin multiplicities and constant-sum codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31865)
*Hacène Belbachir,José Ricardo G. Mendonça*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文统一整理了多项式系数 ∏_{i=1}^{n}(1+x+⋯+x^{s_i}) 的系数在受限组合（bounded compositions）、子多重集（submultisets）、独立离散均匀随机变量和（sums of discrete uniform variables）及 SU(2) 张量积权重重数等经典视角下的含义，并给出对称性、对数凹性、中心渐近与泊松极限等新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源自 de Moivre 的骰子问题，当各类上限相等时可由容斥原理（inclusion-exclusion）处理；上限任意时虽有多种经典解释，但这些解释分散于不同文献。作者旨在以统一记号整合并记录其来源，并在此基础上系统推导该秩多项式（rank polynomial）系数的结构与渐近性质。

Method: 采用互补对合（complement involution）证明对称性与边界恒等式；对后半部分作一阶差分（first differences），联系对称链（symmetric chain）起点与混合自旋重数（mixed-spin multiplicities）；通过卷积（convolution）论证对数凹性（log-concavity）与中心众数；对等界情形用局部展开（local expansions）给出含 1/n 与 1/n^2 修正的中心渐近；并借助泊松极限（Poisson limit）分析边界交叉。

Result: 得到的主要结果包括：任意上限下，下半部分一阶差分同时刻画对称链起点与混合自旋重数，链长轮廓与不可约维数轮廓一致；卷积给出对数凹性与中心众数；等界固定 s≥1 且 ns 为偶数时，系数及其一阶差分获得显式中心渐近修正；一阶差分在尺度 k ~ λn^{s/(s+1)} 处出现首个边界交叉，当等于最小界 s 的比例趋于 α>0 时带因子 e^{-αλ^{s+1}}；中心一阶差分包含 s-Catalan 数（即 Littlewood-Richardson 系数），中心层给出基于哈希签名（hash-based signatures）的最大规模常和码本（constant-sum codebooks）；在多项式二项式约定下，完整容斥变换为零，而省略项之和等于受限组合数的负值。

Conclusion: 该研究将秩多项式系数的多重经典解释统一于共同记号，并系统刻画了其对称性、对数凹性、中心渐近、泊松型边界交叉以及与编码理论的联系，为受限组合与随机变量和分布的研究提供了连贯框架。

Abstract: How many ways are there to select $k$ objects from $n$ types when type $i$ is available in only $s_{i}$ copies? For equal bounds, this is de Moivre's problem of obtaining a prescribed total with $n$ dice, now a textbook application of the principle of inclusion-exclusion. For arbitrary bounds, the answer is encoded by the rank polynomial $\prod_{i=1}^{n}(1+x+\cdots+x^{s_{i}})$. Its coefficients enumerate bounded compositions and submultisets, give, after normalization, the probability mass function of a sum of independent discrete uniform random variables, and are the weight multiplicities of a tensor product of $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ representations. Most of these interpretations are classical but dispersed; here we place them in a common notation and document their provenance. A complement involution proves symmetry and boundary identities. For arbitrary bounds, lower-half first differences give both symmetric-chain starts and mixed-spin multiplicities, and the chain-length and irreducible-dimension profiles agree. Convolution gives log-concavity and the central mode. For equal bounds with fixed $s \geq 1$ and even $ns$, local expansions give central asymptotics with explicit $1/n$ and $1/n^{2}$ corrections for the coefficient and its first difference. A Poisson limit gives the first boundary crossover of these differences at scale $k \sim λn^{s/(s+1)}$, with factor $e^{-αλ^{s+1}}$ when the proportion of bounds equal to the fixed minimum $s \geq 1$ tends to $α>0$. Central first differences include the $s$-Catalan numbers, themselves Littlewood-Richardson coefficients; central layers yield maximum-size constant-sum codebooks for hash-based signatures. Under the polynomial binomial convention, the full inclusion-exclusion transform vanishes, and the omitted terms sum to the negative of the number of bounded compositions.

</details>
