### [163] [Commuting Families of Flag Quasideterminants in Reflection Equation Algebras of Type A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32521)
*Dmitry V. Talalaev*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文针对 \(A_{n-1}\) 型反射方程（reflection equation, RE）代数生成矩阵连续分块所对应的东北反对角拟行列式（quasideterminants）族，证明了其两两交换性（pairwise commutativity）的既有猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已提出关于该类反对角拟行列式两两交换性的猜想，但缺乏证明；本文旨在给出该猜想的严格论证。

Method: 证明借助 RE 代数在量子包络代数（quantum enveloping algebra）中的三角实现（triangular realization），并利用拟行列式的旗性质（flag property）。

Result: 成功证明了该族东北反对角拟行列式的两两交换性猜想；并进一步讨论这些元素在量子簇坐标（quantum cluster coordinates）框架下的解释及其与广义 Toda 系统（generalized Toda systems）的可能联系。

Conclusion: 该交换性结果为将上述拟行列式元素纳入量子簇坐标表述、并探索其与广义 Toda 系统的关联提供了基础。

Abstract: We consider a family of northeastern antidiagonal quasideterminants associated with the successive blocks of the generating matrix of a reflection equation (RE) algebra of type $A_{n-1}$. Our main result proves the conjecture on their pairwise commutativity formulated earlier. The proof uses the triangular realization of the RE algebra in a quantum enveloping algebra and the flag property of quasideterminants. We also discuss the interpretation of these elements in terms of quantum cluster coordinates and their possible connection with generalized Toda systems.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Measurement speed limit and Cramér-Rao bound for time-averaged estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32252)
*Hiroyuki Odajima,Andreas Dechant*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究了通过耦合测量系统Y估计系统X中参数θ的精度极限，证明平衡态下Y的Fisher信息不超过X，而非平衡态下可超越，并针对无反作用测量导出仅依赖X性质的Fisher信息增长速度极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数估计问题中，经由第二个耦合系统Y进行间接测量十分常见，但耦合方式与热力学条件如何限制或提升估计精度缺乏统一定量刻画，本文旨在厘清平衡与非平衡、保守与非保守耦合对Fisher信息的影响。

Method: 以参数化系统X与测量系统Y的耦合为框架，比较两者关于参数θ的Fisher信息（Fisher information）；分别在热力学平衡与非平衡条件下给出信息量关系，并针对无反馈作用（back-action-free）的理想测量推导Fisher信息增长的速度极限（speed limit）。

Result: 平衡态下，保守相互作用（conservative interactions）使Y的Fisher信息受限于X，即降低估计精度；非平衡下非保守相互作用（non-conservative interactions）可使Y的Fisher信息超过X，从而潜在提升精度。无反馈测量下，Fisher信息的增长速度仅取决于被测系统X的性质，构成普适精度界，并由此得到时间平均估计量的广义Cramér-Rao界（Cramér-Rao bound, CRB）。

Conclusion: 耦合测量系统中的参数估计精度受热力学条件本质约束：保守、平衡耦合限制精度，非平衡耦合可增强精度；所导出的速度极限与广义CRB为无反馈测量提供基本界限。文中以线性动力学（linear dynamics）作示例，并提出一种高精度的无扰动非平衡传感（non-equilibrium sensing）方案。

Abstract: We consider a measured system $X$ containing a parameter $θ$ of interest and the problem of estimating this parameter by observing a second, coupled measurement system $Y$. We show that, in thermodynamic equilibrium, the Fisher information of $Y$ associated with the parameter is bounded by that of $X$, implying that conservative interactions between $X$ and $Y$ reduce the precision of parameter estimation. Conversely, out of equilibrium, we show that the Fisher information of $Y$ can exceed that of $X$, so that non-conservative interactions can potentially increase the precision of parameter estimation. For idealized, back-action-free measurements, where the coupling to $Y$ does not perturb $X$, we derive a speed limit for the increase of Fisher information of $Y$. This speed limit only depends on the properties of the measured system $X$ and places a fundamental bound on the precision of any back-action-free measurement. As a corollary, we obtain a generalized Cramér-Rao bound (CRB) for time-averaged estimators. We illustrate our results for linear dynamics and suggest a scheme for disturbance-free non-equilibrium sensing with improved accuracy.

</details>


### [165] [Banded Chaos and Time-Semicrystalline Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32297)
*Matteo Baggioli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文从经典非线性动力学（classical nonlinear dynamics）视角重新解释“时间半晶（time semicrystal）”现象，指出带状混沌吸引子（banded chaotic attractor）中多个混沌扇区（chaotic sector）的循环置换可自然产生亚谐（subharmonic）时间序与宽带混沌连续谱共存的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作提出时间半晶概念，即驱动非平衡体系（driven nonequilibrium regime）中，亚谐时间序可在确定性混沌（deterministic chaos）内部存活；本文旨在用成熟的非线性动力学框架重审这一现象学，明确其基本要素的来源。

Method: 采用经典非线性动力学分析：将带状混沌吸引子刻画为有限个由频闪演化（stroboscopic evolution）循环置换的混沌扇区，并以驱动Duffing振子（driven Duffing oscillator）与逻辑斯蒂映射（logistic map）为例进行示例性论证。

Result: 该结构可自然重现时间半晶动力学的特征信号；周期亚谐运动可经由倍周期级联（period-doubling cascade）进入混沌，并保留由普适Feigenbaum标度（Feigenbaum scaling）支配的时间骨架（temporal skeleton）；混沌内的时间序亦可经由不同机制重组或崩溃，产生连续或突变转变，并可能与对称性变化交织。

Conclusion: 研究将时间半晶现象学与耗散非线性动力学（dissipative nonlinear dynamics）的既有结构相连接，表明其核心要素已在标准非线性系统中出现，并为在多体（many-body）与实验情境中推广和检验该范式提供简洁框架。

Abstract: Recent work has introduced the notion of a time semicrystal, a driven nonequilibrium regime in which subharmonic temporal order survives inside deterministic chaos. Here we revisit this phenomenology from the viewpoint of classical nonlinear dynamics. Banded chaotic attractors consist of a finite number of chaotic sectors cyclically permuted by stroboscopic evolution, allowing discrete subharmonic spectral components to coexist with a broadband chaotic continuum. Exemplified through the driven Duffing oscillator and the logistic map, this structure naturally reproduces the characteristic signatures of time-semicrystalline dynamics. Within this framework, periodic subharmonic motion can enter chaos through the familiar period-doubling cascade while retaining a well-defined temporal skeleton governed by the universal Feigenbaum scaling. Temporal order within chaos can also reorganize or collapse through distinct mechanisms, producing continuous or abrupt transitions that may be intertwined with symmetry changes. These results connect time-semicrystal phenomenology with established structures of dissipative nonlinear dynamics, showing that its essential ingredients already arise in standard nonlinear systems and providing a simple framework for extending and testing the paradigm in more complex, many-body, and experimental settings.

</details>


### [166] [Stochastic and quantum autonomous information machine: efficiency fluctuations, precision and optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32683)
*Lucas Trigal,Gonzalo Manzano*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究热库与信息库（information reservoir，以磁带表示）耦合的自主机器（autonomous machine）中热资源与信息资源的相互转换，并分析电流涨落（fluctuations）与随机效率（stochastic efficiencies）的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示小尺度信息—功转换器件中，热与信息如何相互转化，以及涨落与最优性能运行的影响，为相关器件的设计与优化提供依据。

Method: 构建与热库和信息磁带耦合的自主机器模型，可实现热驱动信息擦除（information erasure）与信息驱动制冷（information-powered refrigeration），并为此提出合适的效率定义；同时分析电流涨落与随机效率，并考察最优性能下的运行，进一步将模型扩展至相干信息库（coherent information reservoir）。

Result: 模型可同时实现热驱动擦除与信息驱动制冷两类操作；涨落与随机效率在器件运行及最优性能中起重要作用；引入相干信息库后可改变器件的运行方式与性能表现。

Conclusion: 该工作为小尺度下信息—功转换（information-to-work conversion）器件的设计与优化提供了理论洞见。

Abstract: We investigate the conversion between thermal and informational resources in an autonomous machine coupled to thermal reservoirs and a tape, playing the role of an information reservoir. The machine is able to perform information erasure driven by heat, and information-powered refrigeration, for which we introduce suitable efficiency definitions. We focus on the role of fluctuations in the currents and stochastic efficiencies, as well as in operation at optimal performance. The model is also extended to allow a coherent information reservoir, which is able to modify operation and performance. All in all, our work provides insights for the design and optimization of information-to-work conversion devices at small scales.

</details>


### [167] [Extended Hydrodynamics of Planar Heisenberg Ferromagnetic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32929)
*Dwigbijoy Paul,Sreedhar B. Dutta*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文在扩展不可逆热力学（extended irreversible thermodynamics）框架下，将动力学耗散流（dissipative currents）作为准流体动力学变量（quasi-hydro variables），推导了适用于顺磁与铁磁相平面海森堡铁磁系统的修正流体动力学方程，并表明新引入的输运系数与弛豫时间可调控流体动力学模式的色散关系（dispersion relations）性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洁净磁性材料制备技术的进步使磁系统中流体动力学（hydrodynamics）与准流体动力学（quasi-hydro）区域的实验可达性不断增强，因而有必要研究超越常规流体动力学区域的物理并探测非平衡输运（non-equilibrium transport）。

Method: 采用扩展不可逆热力学（extended irreversible thermodynamics）框架，将动力学耗散流（dissipative currents）纳入准流体动力学变量（quasi-hydro variables），据此推导适用于平面海森堡铁磁（planar Heisenberg ferromagnetic）系统在顺磁相与铁磁相的修正流体动力学方程。

Result: 由准流体动力学变量引入的新输运系数（transport coefficients）与弛豫时间尺度（relaxation time scales）可组合出多样的色散关系（dispersion relations），并实现对流体动力学模式性质的调控；例如可通过调节磁化流（magnetization current）的弛豫时间，以多种方式将扩散型能量模式（diffusive energy mode）转变为传播型模式。

Conclusion: 准流体动力学变量的引入为磁性系统中流体动力学模式的工程设计提供了可控途径，扩展不可逆热力学框架适用于描述顺磁与铁磁相的平面海森堡铁磁系统，并支持对非平衡输运的进一步研究。

Abstract: Access to hydro and quasi-hydro regimes in magnetic systems is steadily increasing with the advances in fabrication of clean materials, and motivates study of hydrodynamics beyond conventional regimes and probe non-equilibrium transport. We employ the framework of extended irreversible thermodynamics which includes dynamical dissipative currents as quasi-hydro variables, and effect the modified hydrodynamic equations suitable to describe planar Heisenberg ferromagnetic systems in both paramagnetic and ferromagnetic phases. We show that the new transport coefficients and relaxation time scales introduced by quasi-hydro variables can engineer diverse combination of dispersion relations and provide control over the nature of hydrodynamic modes. For instance, we can change the diffusive energy mode into propagating one in multiple ways, including by tuning the relaxation time of magnetization current.

</details>


<div id='physics.ins-det'></div>

# physics.ins-det [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Measurements of the neutron background for the Colorado Underground Research Institute (CURIE)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31856)
*Dakota K. Keblbeck,Uwe Greife,Eric Mayotte,Wouter Van De Pontseele,Kyle G. Leach*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 首次在CURIE浅层地下设施直接测量中子本底，给出放射成因与缪子诱发中子通量，为未来低本底实验提供定量依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CURIE为有效覆盖约400 m.w.e.的浅层地下设施，缺乏直接中子本底数据，而低本底实验规划需要定量掌握放射成因与宇宙成因中子环境。

Method: 用³He正比计数管加六个聚乙烯慢化体组成的Bonner Cylinder谱仪测放射成因分量，响应函数由Geant4模拟、²³⁹Pu-⁹Be源与影锥法标定并做各向同性修正后解谱；用BC-501A液体闪烁体运行1431小时，经脉冲形状甄别（pulse shape discrimination）鉴别中子。

Result: E_n≲10 MeV放射成因主导的标量中子通量为(2.51±0.58_stat±0.39_sys)×10⁻⁵ cm⁻²s⁻¹；>10.5 MeV缪子诱发中子积分通量为(2.09±0.29_stat⁺⁰·⁷⁹₋₀.₅₃_sys)×10⁻⁷ cm⁻²s⁻¹，比站点预测高约12%但在不确定度内一致。

Conclusion: 测量刻画了CURIE的放射成因与高能宇宙成因中子环境，为设施未来低本底实验规划提供定量输入。

Abstract: We present the first direct measurements of the neutron background at the Colorado Underground Research Institute (CURIE), a shallow-underground facility with an effective overburden of approximately $400$~m.w.e.\ in the Edgar Experimental Mine in Idaho Springs, Colorado. The radiogenic and muon-induced components were characterized in the Subatomic Particle Hideout. The radiogenic component was measured with a custom Bonner Cylinder Spectrometer comprising a $^{3}$He proportional counter and six cylindrical high-density polyethylene moderators. Its response functions were simulated in \textsc{geant4}, empirically calibrated using a $^{239}$Pu-$^{9}$Be source and the shadow-cone method, and then corrected to account for the approximately isotropic underground field. Unfolding the measured count rates gives a total local scalar neutron flux, dominated by radiogenic production, for $E_n\lesssim\SI{10}{\MeV}$ of $(2.51\pm0.58_{\rm stat}\pm0.39_{\rm sys})\times10^{-5}$~\si{\per\square\centi\meter\per\second}. The muon-induced component was measured with a BC-501A organic liquid scintillator operated for \SI{1431}{\hour}. Pulse shape discrimination identified 55~$\pm$~7 neutron candidates. Using the simulated detector response, these counts yield an integral muon-induced neutron flux at the rock--cavern boundary above \SI{10.5}{\MeV} of $(2.09\pm0.29_{\rm stat}\,{}^{+0.79}_{-0.53}{}_{\rm sys})\times10^{-7}$~\si{\per\square\centi\meter\per\second}. This flux uses the first-entry, boundary-area normalization of a previous CURIE simulation study. Its central value is approximately \SI{12}{\percent} higher than the site-specific prediction but is consistent within the reported uncertainties. Together, these measurements characterize the radiogenic and high-energy cosmogenic neutron environments at CURIE and provide quantitative input for planning future low-background experiments at the facility.

</details>


### [169] [Radiation damage and recovery of SiPMs exposed to low-momentum muon beams for innovative scintillation-based muon beam monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32852)
*Riccardo Rossini,Sayani Biswas,Adrian D. Hillier,James S. Lord,Lisa Malliolio,Alessandro Menegolli,William Mourad,Daniel E. Pooley,Marco C. Prata,Lina Quintieri,Massimo Rossella,Francesco Zamperoni*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 研究评估了8款硅光电倍增器（Silicon PhotoMultipliers, SiPM）在29 MeV/c低动量正μ子、约10^6 μ子/秒通量下辐照13小时后的性能变化，发现暗电流可测上升但器件仍保持工作稳定性，且125°C热退火可恢复其最优特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分闪烁体探测器设计需将SiPM置于放射性区域或直接暴露于粒子束，低动量μ子能量沉积高且广泛应用于材料科学与粒子物理，其辐照损伤评估因此尤为重要。

Method: 将来自不同制造商的8款SiPM模型暴露于29 MeV/c正μ子束（强度约10^6 μ子/秒）持续13小时，以使耗尽区（depletion region）内沉积电荷最大化；辐照与退火前后测量I-V曲线（I-V curves），并开展暗计数率（dark count rate, DCR）与光学串扰（optical crosstalk）分析；另以125°C加热测试热退火（thermal annealing）效果。

Result: 器件暗电流（dark current）出现可测的上升，但仍保持工作稳定性；125°C热退火后观察到SiPM最优特性的恢复。暗计数率与光学串扰的提取分析仍在进行中。

Conclusion: 低动量μ子辐照会使SiPM暗电流升高但未破坏其运行稳定性，热退火可实现性能恢复。该工作属于帕维亚大学（University of Pavia）与ISIS的合作项目，旨在开发基于薄闪烁体的低动量μ子（< 40 MeV/c）探测器。

Abstract: Silicon PhotoMultipliers (SiPMs) are among the standard devices for light detection in scintillator detectors. Some detector designs require these devices to be placed in radioactive areas or directly exposed to particle beams. Specifically, the damage from exposure to low-momentum muons is particularly important, as they have a high energy deposit and they have several applications in material science and particle physics. Eight SiPM models from different manufacturers were exposed to 29 MeV/c positive muons, with an intensity of O(10^6) muons/s for 13 hours, maximising the charge deposited in the depletion region. I-V curves were measured before/after irradiation and annealing; DCR and crosstalk analyses are ongoing, to extract dark count rate and optical crosstalk. Despite showing a measurable increase in dark current, the devices preserved operational stability. Thermal annealing has also been tested by heating the devices at 125°C, showing a recovery of the optimal SiPM features. This work is part of a collaboration between the University of Pavia and ISIS, to develop detectors for low-momentum muons (< 40 MeV/c), based on thin scintillators.

</details>


### [170] [The scintillating fibre beam monitor for the CHNet-MAXI experiment at ISIS: characterisation of the light collection and detection system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32865)
*Lisa Malliolio,Riccardo Rossini,Andrea Abba,Roberto Bertoni,Sayani Biswas,Matteo Cataldo,Giancarlo Ceruti,Carlo De Vecchi,Daniela Di Martino,Adrian D. Hillier,James S. Lord,Giulia Marcucci,Alessandro Menegolli,Alessio Mereghetti,Paola Monza,Maya Musa,Piernicola Oliva,Federico Palenzona,Maurizio Perego,Daniel E. Pooley,Marco C. Prata,Marco Pullia,Lina Quintieri,Maria Pia Riccardi,Alice Roggio,Massimo Rossella,Simone Savazzi,Valeria Sipala,Erik S. Vallazza,Claudio Viviani,Massimiliano Clemenza*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: CHNet-MAXI实验在ISIS开展基于μ子原子（muonic atom）形成的铅样品无损同位素表征，本文报道其低能μ子束流监测探测器——由64根闪烁光纤（scintillating fibres）与SiPM阵列构成的束流监测器（beam monitor）——的光学性能表征、均匀性校正、质子束测试及Geant4模拟等前期工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHNet-MAXI实验需要对铅样品进行非破坏性同位素表征（non-destructive isotopic characterisation），而实验所用的低能μ子束（low-energy muon beam）需重建其时间与空间分布，因此需要一个专用的闪烁体探测器作为束流监测器，并在此之前完成各项性能验证。

Method: 采用基于闪烁体的定制探测器：两个正交平面、各由10 cm长、3 mm×3 mm方形闪烁光纤（共64根）组成，由四组各16个滨松S14160-3015PS SiPM读出。对探测器光学性能（重点为SiPM行为）进行表征，并用⁶⁰Co源检查与校正各通道光响应均匀性；同时在CNAO-XPR完成首次质子束测试，并开展Geant4模拟。

Result: 完成了探测器光学性能表征，重点考察了SiPM的工作行为，并借助⁶⁰Co源检查与校正了各通道光响应的均匀性；在CNAO-XPR获得了首次质子束测试结果；Geant4模拟结果为探测器运行与数据分析提供了基础支撑。

Conclusion: 上述光学表征、均匀性校正、质子束测试与模拟工作均为探测器最终投入运行的前期准备；最终探测器将用于ISIS的RIKEN-RAL Port4衰变μ子束线（decay muon beamline）上的CHNet-MAXI实验。

Abstract: The CHNet-MAXI experiment at ISIS aims at a non-destructive isotopic characterisation of lead samples, based on muonic atom formation. A custom scintillation-based detector is used for the reconstruction of the time and space distributions of the low-energy muon beam employed in the experiment. The beam monitor consists of two orthogonal planes of 10 cm-long, 3 mm $\times$ 3 mm square scintillating fibres, totalling 64 fibres, read out by four arrays of 16 Hamamatsu S14160-3015PS SiPMs each. The optical performance of the detector has been characterised, focusing on the SiPM behaviour, and also checking and correcting the uniformity of the light response across channels with a $^{60}$ Co source. A first detector test with protons at the CNAO-XPR has been carried out. The results of the Geant4 simulation of the detector, fundamental for the detector operation and data analysis, are also presented. All these activities are preliminary to the final detector operation in the CHNet-MAXI experiment at the RIKEN-RAL Port4 decay muon beamline at ISIS.

</details>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [New time-periodic solutions to the cubic conformal wave equation on $\mathbb{S}^{3}$: I Spherical symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32600)
*Filip Ficek,Maciej Maliborski*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文严格构造了爱因斯坦圆柱（Einstein cylinder）$\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{S}^{3}$ 上径向对称三次共形波动方程（cubic conformal wave equation）的新型时间周期解，其存在性此前由数值研究提示，证明基于压缩映射原理（contraction principle）并结合计算机有理算术（rational arithmetic）验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有结果主要涉及无穷小解的 Cantor 集，而早期数值研究（arXiv:2504.10513）暗示存在其他时间周期解，本文旨在为其提供严格数学证明并弥补这一空白。

Method: 采用压缩映射原理（contraction principle）进行构造性证明，所需大量计算通过计算机以有理算术（rational arithmetic）完成；同时通过对相关线性算子逆的深入分析获得更精细的解析界（analytical bounds），并引入若干相关算子的简化版本以显著减少计算量。

Result: 成功构造出具有特定频率的三个解作为示例，并保持了证明的严格性；所引入的简化算子与更精细的界有效降低了计算机验证的计算规模。

Conclusion: 该工作证实了数值研究预言的解的存在性，且所发展的技术与工具可直接推广，用于证明 arXiv:2504.10513 中猜想的其他解的存在性。

Abstract: We present a rigorous construction of novel time-periodic solutions to the radially symmetric cubic conformal wave equation on the Einstein cylinder $\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{S}^{3}$. Their existence, hinted by the earlier numerical studies (arXiv:2504.10513), complements prior results on Cantor sets of infinitesimally small solutions. Our proof, based on the contraction principle, requires extensive computations that are performed on a computer by the means of rational arithmetic. We are able to significantly reduce their amount by a thorough analysis of the inverse of the associated linear operator, leading to sharper analytical bounds, as well as introduction of simplified versions of certain relevant operators. We provide examples of three solutions with specific frequency; however, our techniques and tools can be directly adapted to prove the existence of other solutions conjectured in (arXiv:2504.10513).

</details>


### [172] [Linear Stability and a Turning Point Principle for Barotropic Liquid Stars with a Free Boundary in General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32138)
*Zeming Hao,Jianchao Xu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究液滴星（liquid star）在真空包裹下爱因斯坦-欧拉系统（Einstein-Euler system）球对称渐近平坦稳态的线性稳定性，证明大中心红移（central redshift）稳态存在增长模，小中心红移稳态不存在，并给出质量-半径曲线拐点决定核维数与交叉特征值符号的"转向点原理"（turning point principle）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于相对论星体稳定性的分析多依赖启发式判据，缺少对线性化算子谱结构与平衡族参数依赖的严格刻画，尤其缺乏将增长模存在性与质量-半径曲线几何联系起来的统一框架。

Method: 在表面密度为正、声速因果且高密度极限渐近线性的状态方程（equation of state）下构造平衡态族，导出带 Robin 边界条件的线性化方程；证明线性化算子具有离散实谱，且负 Morse 指标（Morse index）等于增长模数目，并借助平衡族对参数的导数构造内部正则零解。

Result: 大中心红移稳态存在增长模，小中心红移稳态无增长模；参数导数给出的内部零解其边界亏损恰为总质量参数导数的非零倍数，由此核（kernel）在质量临界点处为一维，且交叉特征值（crossing eigenvalue）的符号由质量-半径曲线的弯曲方向决定。

Conclusion: 该文以严格方法建立了相对论液滴星线性稳定性的谱刻画与转向点原理，将稳定性转变与质量-半径曲线的临界点和弯曲几何直接对应，为相对论星体稳定性判据提供了数学上可验证的基础。

Abstract: We study the linear stability of spherically symmetric, asymptotically flat steady states of the Einstein-Euler system describing a liquid star surrounded by vacuum. The equation of state has positive surface density, positive causal sound speed, and an asymptotically linear high-density limit. We construct the equilibrium family and derive the linearized equation with a Robin boundary condition. The linearized operator has discrete real spectrum, and its negative Morse index equals the number of growing modes. We prove that the linearized operator around steady states with large central redshift admits a growing mode, while such growing modes do not exist for steady states with small central redshift. A parameter derivative of the equilibrium family provides a regular interior zero solution of the linearized equation, whose boundary defect is a nonzero multiple of the parameter derivative of the total mass. This gives a turning point principle: the kernel is one-dimensional precisely at mass critical points, and the sign of the crossing eigenvalue is determined by the bending of the mass-radius curve.

</details>


### [173] [Corner Regularity and Asymptotics for the Two-Dimensional Radiation Transport Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32732)
*Dean Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文为二维辐射输运方程（radiation transport equation）在矩形角点附近的标量通量（scalar flux）建立了尖锐的连续性模估计（modulus-of-continuity estimate）ω_φ(h)=O(h log(D/h))，从而证明其满足Dini连续性并给出严格的微分公式，进而导出角点附近标量通量及其一阶导数的固定角度渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kaper角点奇异性问题（corner singularity problem）的先前研究将物理输运核分解为主逆距离奇异项与低阶余项，把角点渐近分析归结为标量通量是否具有足够连续性以对相应奇异势求导；但所需的正则性此前一直未解决。

Method: 针对物理输运核生成的标量通量，建立尖锐的连续性模估计（modulus of continuity），证明ω_φ(h)=O(h log(D/h))，并利用该估计得到微分主势（differentiated principal potential）中涨落项的绝对收敛性及输运解的严格微分公式，据此进行固定角度（fixed-angle）的角点渐近分析。

Result: 对每条固定内部射线（interior ray），梯度呈现对数增长（logarithmic growth），标量通量带有r log(1/r)修正项，其系数由通量与源在角点处的值显式确定；矩形角点不产生强于光滑边界输运理论中已有对数一阶导数奇异性的更强奇异性。基准问题的数值计算与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 研究严格解决了Kaper角点奇异性问题所依赖的正则性问题，并为二维辐射输运中的角点渐近分析建立了数学框架。

Abstract: The regularity of solutions to the two-dimensional radiation transport equation plays a central role in understanding the behavior of scalar flux near geometric corners. In a previous study of Kaper's corner singularity problem, the physical transport kernel was decomposed into its principal inverse-distance singularity and a lower-order remainder, reducing the corner asymptotic analysis to the question of whether the scalar flux possesses sufficient continuity to justify differentiation of the associated singular potential. The required regularity, however, remained unresolved. In this paper, we establish a sharp modulus-of-continuity estimate for the scalar flux generated by the physical transport kernel and prove the estimate $ω_φ(h)=O\!\left(h\log(D/h)\right)$, which implies Dini continuity. This estimate yields absolute convergence of the fluctuation term arising in the differentiated principal potential and permits a rigorous differentiation formula for transport solutions. Using this regularity framework, we derive the fixed-angle asymptotic behavior of the scalar flux and its first derivatives near a rectangular corner. For every fixed interior ray, the gradient exhibits logarithmic growth, while the scalar flux admits an $r\log(1/r)$ correction with an explicitly identified coefficient determined by the corner values of the flux and source. The analysis shows that a rectangular corner does not generate a singularity stronger than the logarithmic first-derivative singularity already present in smooth-boundary transport theory. Numerical calculations on benchmark problems are consistent with the theoretical predictions. These results provide a rigorous resolution of the regularity issue underlying Kaper's corner singularity problem and establish a mathematical framework for corner asymptotics in two-dimensional radiation transport.

</details>


### [174] [The Two-Dimensional Narrow Escape Problem with Deterministic Initial Condition: Mean Escape Time and Escape location probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32829)
*Loïs Delande,Tony Lelièvre,Boris Nectoux,Thomas Normand*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文对二维窄逃逸问题（narrow escape problem）进行严格渐近分析，除平均逃逸时间外，核心贡献是推导逃逸分布的显式渐近公式，并处理逃逸窗边界无接触角情形下的混合边值问题（mixed boundary value problem）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多关注平均逃逸时间，而对逃逸分布的严格渐近刻画不足；同时逃逸窗边界处解的正则性（regularity）损失使混合边值问题的严格处理具有挑战性。

Method: 采用两种相互独立的方法推导显式渐近公式，并借助混合边值问题解梯度最大正则性（maximal regularity）的近期结果，保证在无接触角（contact angle）条件下分析的解析有效性。

Result: 得到刻画逃逸行为依赖于起点与窗中心相对构型的显式渐近公式，结果在起点趋近边界窗时仍成立，并给出精细误差估计（error estimates）；对窗尺寸按特定相对速率缩放的区域给出完整的尖锐渐近等价式（sharp asymptotic equivalents）。

Conclusion: 该工作为窄逃逸问题提供了涵盖逃逸分布的严格渐近理论框架，并拓展至无接触角与起点贴近边界的退化情形。

Abstract: This paper presents a rigorous asymptotic analysis of the two-dimensional narrow escape problem for a Brownian particle initiated at a deterministic position x. While we address the mean escape time, the main novelty lies in our derivation of the asymptotic escape distribution. We derive explicit asymptotic formulas that characterize the dependence of the escape behavior on the relative configuration of the starting point and the window centers. These results remain valid as the starting position approaches the boundary windows and include refined error estimates. The formulas are obtained with two independent methods, each of them having their own interest. A central feature of this work is the rigorous treatment of the underlying mixed boundary value problems in the absence of a contact angle at the escape window boundaries. By leveraging recent results on the maximal regularity of the gradient of the solutions of such mixed boundary value problems, we ensure the analytical validity of our approach despite the inherent loss of regularity of these solutions at the escape window boundaries. Finally, we apply these general results to provide a full sharp asymptotic equivalents for regimes where window sizes scale at specific relative rates.

</details>

### [175] [Heterogeneous superconductivity emerging from a charge-stripe state in kagome lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32097)
*Hongyu Li,Shuikang Yu,Huachen Rao,Zhuying Wang,Xingchen Zhou,Houpu Li,Yunmei Zhang,Zhao Gong,Xin Yu,Rui Wu,Tao Wu,Zhenyu Wang,Ziji Xiang,Xianhui Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在kagome超导体CsV3Sb5-xSnx中，当三重-Q电荷密度波（triple-Q CDW）在x_c=0.07转变为短程条纹状CDW（short-range stripelike CDW）时，超导性质同步发生突变，呈现空间不均匀的超导态与大量残余正常流体，暗示超导序参量变号并受条纹电荷强烈配对破坏散射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷密度波（charge density wave, CDW）与超导（superconductivity）长期被视为竞争序，但近期研究表明二者合作可产生涌现现象；本文旨在探究CDW序形式变化如何影响超导特性。

Method: 通过Sn替代调控CsV3Sb5-xSnx体系，结合热力学、电输运与扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM）实验，表征CDW形式转变及超导态性质。

Result: 在x_c=0.07处CDW由三重-Q型转为短程条纹状时，超导性质突变；超导能隙呈现显著空间变化，与临界温度分布及超导前驱效应一致，且超导态深处仍存在大量正常流体（电子比热残余与零能准粒子干涉）。

Conclusion: 上述现象与Sn替代引入的化学不均匀性及残余应变无关，而与变号超导序参量（sign-changing superconducting order parameter）受短程电荷条纹强配对破坏散射的情形相符，反映电荷条纹态中超导的非规整（unconventional）本质。

Abstract: Charge density wave (CDW) and superconductivity have long been recognized as competing orders. However, recent achievements establish that the interplay between these two collective electronic phenomena can be more perplexing, with emergent phenomena arising from their cooperation. Here we report a distinctive case of the kagome superconductors CsV3Sb5-xSnx, in which the characteristics of superconductivity evolve in accordance with the form of CDW order. As the triple-Q CDW in CsV3Sb5 transforms to a short-range stripelike CDW at xc = 0.07, a sudden change in superconducting properties occurs concomitantly. A combination of thermodynamic, electrical transport and scanning tunneling microscopy experiments reveals a heterogeneous superconducting state beyond xc: the superconducting gap exhibits pronounced spatial variation, in conformity with a distribution of critical temperature and the resulted superconducting precursor effects. Moreover, abundant normal fluid persists deep into the superconducting state, as evidenced by remnants of electronic specific heat and zero-energy quasiparticle interference. These observations are unrelated to the chemical inhomogeneity and residual strain effect introduced by Sn substitution. Instead, they emulate the phenomenology that a sign-changing superconducting order parameter is subject to strong pair-breaking scattering with the short-range charge stripes and hence reflect the unconventional nature of superconductivity developing inside the charge-stripe state.

</details>


### [176] [Strain-tunable van Hove singularity and superconducting dome in monolayer kagome metal CsTi$_3$Bi$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32359)
*Run Lv,Wenqian Tu,Dingfu Shao,Yuping Sun,Wenjian Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文利用第一性原理计算系统研究了双轴机械应变对单层钛基kagome金属（kagome metal）CsTi₃Bi₅电子结构与超导电性的调控作用，发现拉伸应变可将M点范霍夫奇点（van Hove singularity, VHS）单调调至费米能级，超导转变温度（superconducting transition temperature）呈穹顶状随应变变化并在+2%处达到3.6 K最大值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新发现的钛基kagome金属CsTi₃Bi₅在无伴随电荷密度波（charge density wave）的情况下表现超导电性，为研究本征电子结构与超导机制、以及kagome晶格二维极限下的超导行为提供了理想平台。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）系统研究双轴机械应变（biaxial mechanical strain）对单层CsTi₃Bi₅电子结构与超导电性的影响。

Result: 拉伸应变可将M点显著的范霍夫奇点单调调至费米能级；超导转变温度随应变呈穹顶状依赖，在最优拉伸应变ε=+2%处达到3.6 K最大值；平均超导能隙（superconducting gap）大小为0.35 meV，且具有轨道依赖的各向异性分布。

Conclusion: 研究结果深化了对单层CsTi₃Bi₅超导电性的理解，并表明应变是一种有效且连续的调控手段，可用于操纵其电子结构与超导电性。

Abstract: The recently discovered titanium-based kagome metal CsTi$_3$Bi$_5$, which exhibits superconductivity without an accompanying charge density wave, provides an ideal platform to investigate the intrinsic electronic structure and superconductivity. Moreover, it enables the exploration of superconductivity in the two-dimensional limit of the kagome lattice. Here, we systematically investigate the influence of biaxial mechanical strain ($\varepsilon$) on the electronic structure and superconductivity of monolayer CsTi$_3$Bi$_5$ using first-principles calculations. We demonstrate that the prominent van Hove singularity (VHS) at the M point can be monotonically tuned to the Fermi level by tensile strain. Furthermore, the superconducting transition temperature $T_C$ exhibits a dome-shaped dependence on strain, reaching a maximum of 3.6 K at an optimal tensile strain of $\varepsilon=+2\%$. The average superconducting gap magnitude is 0.35 meV, with an orbital-dependent and anisotropic distribution. Our results provide a detailed understanding of the superconductivity of monolayer CsTi$_3$Bi$_5$ and show that strain is an effective, continuous tuning knob for manipulating the electronic structure and superconductivity.

</details>


### [177] [Interplay of Chemical Disorder, Vortex Pinning, and Electron Correlations in the Mechanically Robust Medium-Entropy Alloy Superconductor $\text{Nb}_{0.45}\text{Ta}_{0.25}\text{Ti}_{0.15}\text{Hf}_{0.15}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32449)
*Pooja Chourasia,Samiul Sk,Nikita Sharma,B. Vignolle,Abhishek Sarkar,Olivier Toulemonde,Sanjoy Mahatha,S. K. Panda,Sourav Marik*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究结合实验与第一性原理计算，系统表征了难熔中熵合金（medium-entropy alloy）Nb₀.₄₅Ta₀.₂₅Ti₀.₁₅Hf₀.₁₅的结构、超导与电子性质，揭示熵工程化学无序可稳定强超导并诱导关联电子行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中高熵合金（medium and high-entropy alloys）的本征化学无序有望同时提升力学鲁棒性并调控超导性能，但其微观机制与电子关联效应尚需系统阐明，故开展结构与超导性质的联合研究。

Method: 采用实验与第一性原理（first-principles）计算相结合的方式，通过比热分析、输运测量与密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算，显式建模本征化学无序并量化局域晶格畸变（local lattice distortion）。

Result: 材料呈第二类超导（type-II superconductivity），T_C^onset ≈ 7.8 K，临界电流密度 J_c = 0.48×10⁶ A/cm²；比热给出强电子-声子耦合（λ = 1.07）与各向同性 s 波超导能隙；DFT 预测稳定的体心立方（body-centered cubic）相并显示费米面处显著的 Ti、Ta 态；局域晶格畸变 Δd = 0.086 Å，且 Kadowaki-Woods 比异常偏大，接近重费米子（heavy-fermion）区间。

Conclusion: 熵调控的化学无序可稳定鲁棒超导性、增强磁通钉扎（flux pinning）并诱发关联电子行为，表明难熔中熵合金（refractory medium-entropy alloys）可作为下一代超导材料的候选平台。

Abstract: Medium and high-entropy alloys have emerged as a promising class of superconducting materials because their intrinsic chemical disorder can simultaneously enhance mechanical robustness and modify superconducting properties. Here, we present a combined experimental and first-principles investigation of the structural, superconducting, electronic, and local lattice-distortion characteristics of the mechanically robust medium-entropy alloy $\text{Nb}_{0.45}\text{Ta}_{0.25}\text{Ti}_{0.15}\text{Hf}_{0.15}$. The material shows type-II superconductivity with a transition temperature $T_C^{onset} \approx 7.8 ~\mathrm{K}$. A high critical current density of $J_c = 0.48 \times 10^{6}~\mathrm{A/cm^2}$ is observed, and this is attributed to strong vortex pinning at grain boundaries. Specific-heat analysis indicates a strong electron-phonon coupling ($λ$ = 1.07) with a large isotropic s-wave superconducting gap. Density functional theory calculations explicitly model the intrinsic chemical disorder, predict a stable body-centered cubic phase. These calculations further reveal a metallic electronic structure with substantial Ti- and Ta-derived states at the Fermi level. Additionally, our results quantify a pronounced disorder-induced local lattice distortion ($Δd = 0.086$ A), providing a microscopic basis for the experimentally observed enhanced vortex-pinning landscape. Remarkably, the alloy also exhibits an unusually large Kadowaki-Woods ratio, placing it close to the heavy-fermion regime and indicating enhanced electronic correlations beyond conventional alloy superconductors. Our results demonstrate that entropy-engineered chemical disorder can stabilize robust superconductivity, promote strong flux pinning, and induce correlated electronic behavior, establishing refractory medium-entropy alloys as a promising platform for next-generation superconducting materials.

</details>


### [178] [Effect of oxygen plasma treatment on the structural and superconducting properties of atomic-layer-deposited NbN thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32529)
*Nitesh Yerra,Mustafa Teke,Felix Otto,Marco Diegel,Detlef Born,Uwe Hübner,Nan Du,Mario Ziegler,Sahitya Varma Vegesna,Heidemarie Krüger*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过O₂等离子体（O₂ plasma）对约11.4 nm厚的NbN超导薄膜进行0–60 s的氧化处理，发现表面氧化生成自限制生长的Nb₂O₅层而块体化学性质与晶体δ-NbN保持不变；氧化使正常态电阻率上升约21%（直流）与25%（光学），但临界温度（critical temperature）与转变宽度不变，低温开关电流密度无系统性退化，氧的影响主要体现在Tc附近超电流温度区间的收窄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面氧化（surface oxidation）是超薄NbN超导器件（superconducting devices）的普遍问题，但对等离子体增强原子层沉积（plasma-enhanced atomic layer deposition, PEALD）生长的NbN薄膜，氧化究竟影响哪些超导性质尚未确立。

Method: 将约11.4 nm厚的NbN薄膜暴露于O₂等离子体0–60 s（该处理只氧化表面而不刻蚀薄膜），对同一系列样品开展X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）、X射线光电子能谱（X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy, XPS）以及500 nm宽纳米线（nanowire）的输运测量（transport measurements）。

Result: XPS显示表面转化为Nb₂O₅、而处理膜内部化学性质与未处理膜一致，晶体δ-NbN保持完整；总膜厚在40–60 s间饱和，表明自限制氧化生长（self-limiting oxide growth）。正常态电阻率（normal-state resistivity）随剂量升高21%（直流）与25%（光学），临界温度与转变宽度不变；4.2 K下开关电流密度为4.9–6.2 MA/cm²且无系统性剂量依赖，其温度依赖符合Kupriyanov–Lukichev脏极限（dirty-limit）退配对电流（depairing current）模型，外推开关电流达由光学电阻率与扩散系数计算所得退配对电流的39–46%。

Conclusion: 氧信号仅局限于Tc附近温度，处理线材的超电流比未处理线材至少提前0.5 K消失，符合部分氧化表层通过邻近效应（proximity effect）作用于薄膜的解释；在该剂量范围内，氧从高温侧收窄超电流的温度区间，但不系统性降低线材的低温载流能力。

Abstract: Surface oxidation is a common concern for ultrathin NbN superconducting devices, yet which superconducting properties it affects has not been established for films grown by plasma-enhanced atomic layer deposition. We expose NbN films about 11.4 nm thick to an O$_2$ plasma for 0 to 60 s, which oxidizes the surface without etching the film, and characterize the same sample series by X-ray diffraction, X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (XPS), and transport measurements on 500 nm wide nanowires. XPS of the surface and the underlying layer shows that the plasma converts the surface to Nb$_2$O$_5$, whereas the bulk of the treated film is chemically identical to that of the untreated film. Crystalline $δ$-NbN remains intact. During exposure, the total film thickness rises and saturates between 40 and 60 s, indicating self-limiting surface oxide growth. The normal-state resistivity rises by 21% (dc) and 25% (optical) with the plasma dose, while the critical temperature and transition width are unchanged. The switching current densities of 4.9-6.2 MA/cm$^2$ at 4.2 K show no systematic dependence on the plasma dose. Their temperature dependence follows the dirty-limit depairing current of Kupriyanov and Lukichev, and the extrapolated switching currents reach 39-46% of the depairing current calculated from the optical resistivity and diffusion coefficient, in agreement with the width dependence reported for nanowires from the same process. The oxygen signature is confined to temperatures near $T_c$, where the supercurrent of the treated wires vanishes at least 0.5 K earlier than that of the untreated wire, consistent with a partially oxidized surface layer that acts on the film by the proximity effect. At these doses, oxygen narrows the temperature range of the supercurrent from above but does not systematically reduce the low-temperature current-carrying capacity of the wires.

</details>


### [179] [Majorana Edge-to-bound State Transition in Interferometric Superconductor Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32767)
*Xiangyu Bi,Aoshen Yang,Hongzi Cao,Ganyu Chen,Yuqiang Fang,Zeya Li,Qizheng Sun,Junwei Huang,Da Wang,Fuqiang Huang,Toshiya Ideue,Qianghua Wang,Yoshihiro Iwasa,Hongtao Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过在2M-WS₂拓扑超导纳米环器件中调节涡旋尺寸与环宽之比，可在降温过程中将拓扑边缘态驱动为涡旋束缚态，表现为振荡周期的异常增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑超导体中的磁通量子操控对量子计算中的涡旋编织（vortex braiding）至关重要，而干涉超导环既可承载受拓扑保护的边缘态，也可承载磁通芯中的涡旋束缚态，如何在这两类态之间进行调控是核心问题。

Method: 在2M-WS₂拓扑超导纳米环干涉器件中，通过温度调控相干长度（coherence length）与环宽的相对尺度，并结合Little-Parks振荡测量、Ginzburg-Landau模拟以及基于Bogoliubov-de Gennes方程的局域态密度（density-of-states）计算进行分析。

Result: 在超导转变附近，相干长度大于环宽时，Little-Parks振荡呈现环心磁通束的预期周期，符合边缘态位于环内、外缘的行为；继续降温使相干长度小于环宽后，振荡周期意外增大，反映磁通以“磁通项链（flux necklace）”构型沿环区分布，暗示由扩展边缘态向局域涡旋束缚态的转变。模拟表明较大的Ginzburg-Landau参数与磁通钉扎能（flux-pinning energy）是该转变的关键条件。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一种通过调节涡旋尺寸与环宽比例来操控磁通的策略，为理解磁通操控并进一步实现磁通编织与拓扑量子计算提供了新途径。

Abstract: Manipulating flux quanta in topological superconductors is central to vortex braiding in quantum computing. Their interferometric superconducting rings can host either topologically protected edge states with fluxes confined inside the central hollow area or vortex bound states in flux cores at the annulus walls. Here we demonstrate a strategy to drive topological edge states into vortex bound states by tuning the relative scale of vortex size and annulus width in nano-ring interferometric device on 2M-WS$_2$ topological superconductor. Near its superconducting transition where the coherence length exceeds the annulus width, Little-Parks oscillations exhibit expected periods for flux bundles in ring-center, consistent with edge states residing at inner and outer rims of the annulus. Upon cooling, where the coherence length shrinks below the annulus width, the unexpectedly increased oscillation period reflects multiple fluxes distributed along annulus region in flux necklace configuration, implying a transition from extended edge states to localized vortex bound states. Ginzburg-Landau simulations and local density-of-states calculations with Bogoliubov-de Gennes equations reveal that large values for both Ginzburg-Landau parameter and flux-pinning energy are vital for such a transition. Our findings provide a novel strategy for understanding flux manipulation towards flux braiding and topological quantum computing.

</details>


### [180] [Finite-Temperature Transconductance of Multiterminal Josephson Junctions with Topological Andreev Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32839)
*Gleb S. Seleznev,David C. Ohnmacht,Wolfgang Belzig*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文基于线性响应理论（linear-response theory）推导多端约瑟夫森结（multiterminal Josephson junctions, MTJJs）的有限温度跨导（transconductance），指出温度既破坏跨导的精确量子化，又可使响应探测零温下隐藏的有限能拓扑交叉（topological crossings）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零温下具有非零陈数（Chern number）的安德烈夫束缚态（Andreev bound states, ABSs）可产生超导端间的量子化跨导，但实际器件处于有限温度，温度对跨导量子化及拓扑响应的影响尚不明确，需要建立有限温度下的输运描述。

Method: 采用线性响应理论（linear-response theory）推导MTJJs的有限温度跨导，分析ABS热占据（thermal occupation）变化对量子化的影响，并考察跨导对有限能拓扑交叉的敏感性。

Result: 温度引起的ABS热占据变化破坏跨导的精确量子化；同时有限温度可使跨导对零温响应中不可见的有限能拓扑交叉产生敏感性，即温度兼具破坏性与建设性作用。

Conclusion: 研究确立了跨导量子化的热限制（thermal limitations），并表明有限温度输运可作为探测安德烈夫谱中原本不可及拓扑（topology）的探针。

Abstract: Multiterminal Josephson junctions (MTJJs) provide a controllable platform for engineering topological Andreev spectra. At zero temperature, Andreev bound states (ABSs) with nonzero Chern numbers give rise to quantized transconductance between superconducting terminals. We derive the finite-temperature transconductance of MTJJs using linear-response theory. We first demonstrate the destructive role of temperature: changes in the thermal occupations of the ABSs destroy exact transconductance quantization. We then show that finite temperature can also play a constructive role by making the transconductance sensitive to finite-energy topological crossings hidden from the zero-temperature response. Our results establish the thermal limitations of transconductance quantization and identify finite-temperature transport as a probe of otherwise inaccessible topology in Andreev spectra.

</details>

### [181] [From Heterogeneity to Function: Enhanced Modular Dynamic Range in Hierarchical Brain Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32328)
*Filipe V. Torres,Mauro Copelli,Leonardo L. Gollo*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究用层级模块化网络（hierarchical-modular networks）模型表明，连接与放电率的异质性（heterogeneity）无需临界点附近微调，即可自然产生类临界动力学、层级化内禀时间尺度（intrinsic timescales）与分布式计算敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑结构具有层级与模块化组织，但该结构如何支持灵活计算与功能特化仍不清楚；动态范围（dynamic range）作为高效信息处理的标志，其跨模块与跨层级的分布尚未被系统探讨。

Method: 构建层级模块化网络模型，分析其模块动力学与放电率分布，提出"模块动态范围（modular dynamic range）"指标，并比较同质与异质网络在全局刺激、局部刺激以及选择性刺激中间层级时的响应差异。

Result: 层级模块化网络无需精细调节即可扩展类临界动力学区间，表现出临界慢化（critical slowing down）与多样标度指数；内禀时间尺度随层级升高而增大，层级内离散度仅在连接与放电率异质时出现；局部刺激在同质网络中降低动态范围，在异质网络中反而提升；刺激中间层级可最大化全局与模块动态范围，且未受刺激模块亦受益。

Conclusion: 异质性是层级模块化网络维持类临界动力学、生成层级化时间尺度并分布化计算敏感性的机制，为不依赖临界微调的功能特化（functional specialization）提供了基础。

Abstract: Hierarchical and modular organization defines brain architecture, yet how it supports flexible computation and functional specialization remains unclear. Although dynamic range is a hallmark of efficient information processing, its distribution across modules and hierarchical levels remains largely unexplored. Here, we show that hierarchical-modular networks naturally generate heterogeneous modular dynamics and firing rates, extending regimes of critical-like dynamics with enhanced intrinsic timescales, reflecting critical slowing down, and diverse scaling exponents without fine-tuning near criticality. Intrinsic timescales increase across hierarchical levels, while their dispersion within levels emerges only when connectivity profiles and firing rates are heterogeneous. We introduce modular dynamic range to quantify how individual modules respond to external inputs and show that heterogeneity enhances both global and module-specific sensitivity, with modular intrinsic timescales increasing with dynamic range. Local stimulation reduces dynamic range in homogeneous networks but enhances it in heterogeneous networks relative to global stimulation. Moreover, selectively stimulating intermediate hierarchical levels maximizes both global and modular dynamic range, revealing a principle of functional specialization. In heterogeneous networks, modular dynamic range is more variable and increases even in unstimulated modules, demonstrating distributed computational benefits beyond directly stimulated regions. Together, these findings establish heterogeneity as a mechanism through which hierarchical-modular networks sustain critical-like dynamics, generate hierarchical timescales, and distribute computational sensitivity, providing a basis for functional specialization without fine-tuning to criticality.

</details>
